1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du /sarba ta thA ga tA kA yA bA ka tsi ta ta gu h+ya a lang ka b+h+ya ha tan trA ra dza nA ma/bod skad du /
1b2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang thugs kyi gsang ba brgyan gyi bkod pa zhes bya ba'i rgyud kyi
1b3(rgyud): rgyal po//bla ma dang 'byam pa'i dbyangs kyi zhabs kyi pad mo la gus bar phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag
1b4(rgyud): gis thos pa'i dus gcig na /bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi sku dang gsung dang /thugs
1b5(rgyud): rdo rje ye shes rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs
2a1(rgyud): @##//rdo rje ye shes phyag rgya chen po'-i dam tshig gi byin gyis brlabs kyi rdo rje'i sku chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs
2a2(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang thugs rdo rje ye shes chen po'i gcod phan gyi rtse mo'i gtsug phud kyi dbang bskur ba brnyes pa
2a3(rgyud): dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung rdo rje ye shes ting nge 'dzin chen po dang ldan pa dang /de bzhin gshegs/
2a4(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi sku dang/gsung dang thugs rdo rje yi shes las sna tshogs pa chen po dang/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi sku
2a5(rgyud): dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje ye shas chen po spro ba'i dam tshig gi gsang ba dang /de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi sku dang gsang
2a6(rgyud): dang thugs rdo rje ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor chen po'i dam tshig gi de kho na nyid dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i thams cad kyi sku dang gagasung
2b1(rgyud): dang;thugs rdo rje ye shes gsung ba chen po'i snying po dang/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje ye shes kyi mnga' bdag
2b2(rgyud): chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje ye shes kun du bzang po chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams
2b3(rgyud): cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thags rdo rje ye shes dpe med cing mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa nam mkha' lta bu dang/de bzhin gshegs par thams cad kyi
2b4(rgyud): sku dang gsung dang thugs rdau rje ye shes rigs dang snying po dang/phyag rgya dang sngags dang rig pa gnyis su med par gyur pa dang /de bzhin gshegs
2b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi sku dang /gsung dang thugs rdo rje ye shas pha rol tu phyin pa'i snying po la dga' ba dang /de bzhin gshegs/
2b6(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsang dang thugs rdo rje ye shesalhag pa'i lha'i snying po mdzes pa dang /de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyi sku dang bsung
3a1(rgyud): @##//dang thugs ye shes rgya mtsho chen po'i bkod pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i snying po la nam mkha'i khams thams cad kyi rdul dang mnyam 
3a2(rgyud): pa'i rdo rje ye shes chen po 'ba' zhig dang /thabs gcig du bzhugs so//de 'ang 'di lta ste sgo mtha' yas pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i yum chen mo dang 
3a3(rgyud): nam mkha'i sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje yum chen mo dang /oM po dza'i sgo zhes bya ba'i yum chen mo dang /'gyur ba med pa'i sgo zhes bya ba'i yum chenamo dang /
3a4(rgyud): shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin ma zhes bya ba'i rdo rje yum che mo dang /gzhan yang ma lus cing lus pa med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rdul phra rab 
3a5(rgyud): dang mnyam pa'i brtson 'grus dang ldan pa'i rdo rje btsun mo stong phrag brgya yang 'di lta ste/mda'i mtshon cha'i lag ces rdo rje btsun mo dang /
3a6(rgyud): dbang phyug la lta ba'i rim pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun mo dang nye bar zhi ba'i bzhin ras zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsunamo dang /gyen du lta ba'i bzhin ras zhes
3a7(rgyud): bya ba'i rdo rje btsun mo dang/zla mtshan rdo rje zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun ma dang /brda sprod pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsum mo dang /dbang phyug gi bde ba'i sgo 
3b1(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun mo dang /dmigs pa'i sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun modang /brang las skyes zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsunamo ste/de dag la sogs pa rdo rje
3b2(rgyud): btsun mo rnams kyis yongs su bskor cing mdun du bdar ro//gzhan yang dpag tshad du med pa'i rdo rje pho nya mo chen mo rnams dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs
3b3(rgyud): te/de yang 'di lta ste snying bskul bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje pho nya mo chen po dang /gdug pa can thams cad 'gugs pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje phonya mo chen po dang /
3b4(rgyud): rdo rje su mnyes par rdo rje zhes bya ba'i rdo rje pho nye ma chen mo dang /khrag 'gugs pa zhes bya ba'i rdo+e pho nya ma chen mo dang /thams cad 'phrog pa zhes bya ba'i
3b5(rgyud): rdo rje pho nya ma chen mo dang gzhan yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgyas pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i sgo srungs chen mo rnams dang yang thabs gcig ste /de 'ang 'di lta ste
3b6(rgyud): rdo rje 'bebs pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje sgo bsrungs ma chen mo dang /dam tshig gsum ldan zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i sgo bsrungs ma chen mo dad /'jigs pa'i yan lag
3b7(rgyud): ces bya ba'i rdo rje sgo bsrung ma chen mo dang /thog ma pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje chen mo dang /gdul dka' 'dul ba zhes bya ba'i rdo rje sgo bsungs ma chen mo
4a1(rgyud): @#//laswogs pa'o//gzhan yang mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i rdo rje bran mo chen mo dang /brtsigs shing rnam par 'gyur ba'i gdong zhes bya ba'i rdo rje bran mo chen mo yang yang thabs gcig
4a2(rgyud): ste /de 'ang 'di lta ste/sku gsung thugs thams cad rdo rje 'chi ba ma zhes bya ba'i rdo rje bran ma chen mo dang /'jig pa'i me'i gdong zhes bya ba'i rdo rja bran mo chen mo rnam dang /brtsigs shing rnam
4a3(rgyud): par 'gyur ba'i gdong zhes ba'i rdo rje bran mo chen po dang /'jig rten gsum 'ching ba zhes bya ba'i rdo rje bran po chen mo dang /bskal par gnas ma zhes bya ba'i rdo rje bram mo laswogs pa'o//
4a4(rgyud): gzhan yang dpe med pa'i rdo rje rol mo pa rnams dang yang thabs gcig ste /de 'ang 'di lta ste /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje dgas pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje
4a5(rgyud): rol pa dang /kun nas 'bebs pa zhes bya ba rdo rje rol pa chen mo dang /'jig rten gsum rab tu 'joms pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje rol pa chen mo dang/rab tu dga' ba rdo rjes
4a6(rgyud): su 'gro ba zhes bya ba'i rdo rje rol ba chen mo dang /thams cad dga' bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje rolapa chen mo rnams laswogs pa'o//gzhan yang nam mkha'i khams thams cad rgyas par gang
4a7(rgyud): ba'i rdo rje yab rnams dang thabs gcig ste /de'ang 'di lta ste 'dod pa'i dbang phyug ces bya ba'i rdo rje'i yab chen po dang /mkha' 'gro'i dbang phyug rdo rje dang /dri'i dbang phyug dang /sdug
4a8(rgyud): po bkod pa dang /zla ba'i 'od dang /'dun pa'i srel ba dang /skye ba yongs su dor ba dang /legs par sbyangs pa dang /'dod pa'i rdo rje dang /bdud bskyed pa dang /'dul ba byed pa dang /gdug pa
4b1(rgyud): thams cad sel bar byed pa dang /de kho na nyid kyi 'gon po dang /gnas po dang /rtse ba can dang /chos 'chang dang /skyong ba po dang /spro ba po dang /'ching pa po dang /bskor ba po dang /de dag la swogs pa
4b2(rgyud): rdo rje yab rnams so//gzhan yang mtha' las 'das pa'i rdo rje pho nya mo 'phrog pa'i bran po'i rdo rje'i tshogs dang thabs gcig ste/de 'ang 'di lta ste/rdo rje rlung zhes bya ba dang /rdo rje me dang /rdo rje mi
4b3(rgyud): 'khrugs pa dang /rdo rje chu dang /gzhan yang rdo rje rgyal po chen po bzhi la 'di lta ste/zhi bar byed pa dang /rgyas par byed la dang /dbang du byed pa dang /mi shes pa thams cad zad par byed
4b4(rgyud): pa de dag la swogs pa'o//gzhan yang mchog tu rgya chen por sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rab tu gnas pa'i mchod pa dang ldan pa/bcom ldan 'das ston pa rtag tu mchog
4b5(rgyud): gi mnga' bdag de//kun du bzang po'i sku yis ni//'jig rten gsum gyi dkyil 'khor spros//bcom ldan 'das rdor 'dzin legs par bzhugs//gsang ba brgyan 
4b6(rgyud): gyi sbyor bas so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad bskyed pa'i yum gsang ba'i rdo rje 'dus par ma thob ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi yab dang lhan cig tu rab tu dga' ba'i
4b7(rgyud): bde ba rdo rje su 'byung ste/'khor dang bcas pa rnams bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar lhags te/rdo rje'i klu 'dis mngon par stod nas/zhu ba zhus pa/sangs rgyas kun khyed phyag 'tshal/
4b8(rgyud): rdo rje 'byung ba khyod la 'dud//thugs kyi rin chen mchog khyod 'dud//chos kyi blo gros khyod la 'dud//chos rnams kun mchog khyod la 'dud//sna tshogs dgongs pa khyod la 'dud//dam
5a1(rgyud): @#//tshig mchog khyod 'dud //rdo rje sems dpar phyag 'tshal 'dud //grags pa chen po bdag cag ni//mgon po khyod la zhu ba zhu //sangs rgyas thams cad skyed byed yum //
5a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor kyi ni dkyil 'khor mchog ////de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i yum rnams la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//de bzhin
5a3(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyis de skad ma zer cig/'jig rten thams cad la khyab pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po chen po 'di ma 'ongs pa'i sems can rnams dang bar mi 'gyur zhing /rang gi sems yongs
5a4(rgyud): su sun 'byin pa des ma mthong ba'i chos la ma dad pas /rang gi sems yang dag par sun phyung ste /gnar med pa rnams su skyes nas bskal pa stong phrag mang po gnar med pa'i
5a5(rgyud): sdug bsngal rjes su 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de rgya lam na mnar med pa'i sdug bsngal las grol te /mi rnams su skyes par gyur kyang rnam par 'gyur ba'i rang bzhin du 'gyur ro//
5a6(rgyud): rje dangs pa rnams dang /blo gros chung ba rnams dang /khyi dang bya rog dang /byi la dang /phag dang /stag dang /glang po che dang /seng ge'i nga ro 'dra ba dag du 'gyur ro//de dag rta ma 'dre bar mi
5a7(rgyud): bya zhing /lta zhing bar mi bya'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad bskyed pa'i yum de rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ji ltar sems can de rnams
5a8(rgyud): kyi gnar med pa'i sdug bsngal zad par 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa /gang gis 'di'i ming bzung nas yongs su zad par 'gyur ro //bsod nams de'i rgyus de'i las kyi
5b1(rgyud): bsgrib pa zad de/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi skal pa can du 'gyur ro//de nas de dag yid dga' ba skyes nas yang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//
5b2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das bstan du gsol//bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs kyi rgyus dkyil 'khor chen po'i rgyal po sems can thams cad la sman pa dang /bde ba'i phyir gang ma 'ongs pa'i sems
5b3(rgyud): can rnams kyis dran pa tsam gyis sangs rgyas kyi byang chub thob par 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis kun du bzang po chen po 'phro ba gsal ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms
5b4(rgyud): par bzhugs so//rang gi sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje rnams las de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku'i dam pa 'di bka' stsal pa//dkyil 'khor kun gyi yum gyur pa//
5b5(rgyud): stong nyid ye shes nyid du bshad//ci zhig nga yis bstan par bya//rtog pa thams cad rnam par spangs//sems can rnams la phan gzhed pas//gang zhig cung
5b6(rgyud): zad bstan par bya//stong nyid ye shes nyid du ba shad//ci zhig nga yis bsan par bya//rtog pa thamasacad rnam par spangs//sem can rnams la phan gzhedapas//gang zhig cung
5b7(rgyud): zadabastan par bya//de kun sgyu mas sgyu ma'o//'gro ba sgyu ma lta bur shes//des na sgyu ma lta bu'i dkyil 'khor bya//nam mkha'i khams kyis khyab pa'i//thams cad yum gyis yang dag 'khrugs
5b8(rgyud): phyi rol yang ni rtag par bya//gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor yum rnams kyi//dben zhing dga' ba'i gnas su ni//yid kyis legs par 'dus byas pa//nam mkha' la ni thig gis gdab//thog mar
6a1(rgyud): //nga yis dam pa'o//de nas rnal 'byor chen po'i thig//gsang ba'i yum gyi dkyil 'khor ro//mu khyud dag ni rnam bdun du//kun nas yongs su bskor bar bya//dbus kyi char ru
6a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor ni//'khor lo lta bu bri bar bya//kun nas brgyad gnyis mdzes pa ni//dkyil 'khor bzhi'i dbus su'o//'khor lo'i lte ba'i dbus su ni//pad ma'i ze 'bru ldan pa bri//mtshams sam 
6a3(rgyud): mtshams su 'dab ma ni//ji lta ba bzhin snyems bcas pas//srad bu lnga yis yang dag 'khrugs//sbrad bu bzhi yis nye bar sbas//sgo bzhi dang ni yang dag ldan//rta babs seng ge dag gis brgyan//
6a4(rgyud): gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan sogs//bla re steng du bris pa dang //rin chen rdo rje pad ldan sngon//dpag bsam shing gi lcug mas khyab;bum bzang rin chen ldan pa'i mchog//brgyan 'phreng 
6a5(rgyud): glang po ldan pa ni//phyi rol gnas de legs par bri//rtog dang mi rtog rnam spangs te//dkyil 'khor rgyal po'i dbus su ni//gsang ba brgyan gyi rdo rje bya//mnyam par gzhag cing
6a6(rgyud): spro ba'i//sems kyi bri ba nyid du bya//de nas lha mo zhal bzhi ma//shes rab pha rol phyin dge ma//phyag bzhi pa la spyan yangs ma//phyag gnyis kyis ni chos 'chad cing //zhi ba'i g.yas pa
6a7(rgyud): rdo rje ste//g.yon pa pad mas rnam par brgyan//dkar dang dmar po cher sngo ba//sngo sangs rin chen mchog mdzes 'od//gtsug na rin chen gtsug tor che//gtsug rgyan gcod phan dam pa can//bskal
6a8(rgyud): pa chen mo 'od ldan ma//brgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par klubs//gos kyis stod ni legs bsgribs pa//bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor 'dzum bcas shing //sna tshogs gos kyis legs brgyan pa//
6b1(rgyud): dbu ma'i zhal ras gser gyi 'od//zhabs gnyis dbus su 'khor lo ste//mdun na rin ri chen bdun gnas pa //pad ma mchog ldan seng ge'i khri//zla ba rdo rje 'dzin pa'i bdan //nyi ma'i dkyil 
6b2(rgyud): 'od 'phro ba //rgyal du nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni //dpa'o de yis bri bar bya//dpa' mos mtha' yas pa'i zhal //mtshan nyid thams cad yang dag rdzogs//brgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par brgyan /
6b3(rgyud): phyag gis rdo rje pad ma 'chang //sna tshogs phyag rgyas phyag gzhan dkrigs //gtsug ldan dar gyi gcad pan can //u rgyan pad ma'i zhal ras ma //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dag la bzhugs //
6b4(rgyud): sna tshogs phyag de 'od nyi ma lta bu'i 'od//dpa'o 'i rnal 'byor ldan pa'i//dpa' 'o des ni bri bar bya //rdo rje sems dpa' ra bdag sgoms te //der ni bsnyems pas 'gro
6b5(rgyud): bar bya //dpa' bos lho ru bri bar bya //gshin rje'i 'dab ma mdzes pa la //mchog sbyin nam mkha' dge ba ma //rin chen khri legs par bzhugs //bzhin ras sngo la 
6b6(rgyud): spyan yangs ma //'dzum bcas nor bu'i 'og bag can //'gul rgyan rin chen 'phreng ba mchog//rkang bdub gdu bu rin po che//yid bzhin nor bu lag pa can //sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin dgongs
6b7(rgyud): ma //gcad phan gyis ni yang dag spras //de nas blon ldan rab song ste //nub du yang ni de bzhin no//dpal dang ldan pas legs par bri//chu las skyes pa'i yid 'phrog ma //dmar
6b8(rgyud): mo'i gos 'dzin dag pa mo //gtsug na gcod pan gyis spras ma //sbrul brgyan sna tshogs lus can ma //dar dang rin chen gyis brlabs ma //pad ma 'byed pa'i sbyor ba'i //phyag gi lo 'dab
7a1(rgyud): /dang ldan pas//zla ba'i gdan la yang dag bzhugs//nyi ma du ma'i snang ba can//ji ltar rigs pa bri bar bya//de nas byang du phyin nas su//song ste dpa' bos bri bar bya//
7a2(rgyud): mi g.yo bzhin ras grags chen ma//mar gad 'bar ba mtshungs pa'o//dkar po'i der gyis yang dag blubs//gtsug na nor bu gcod phan zad//gtsug rgyan sna tshogs dag gis spras//
7a3(rgyud): rdo rje dril bu lag bkra ma//sna tshogs brgyan dang gos ldan mas//zla ba'i gdan la yangadag bzhugs//'od kyis btams pa'i dkyil 'khor 'chog/de nas 'tshams kyi 'dab ma la
7a4(rgyud): me tog gdug pa dri ldan ma//rdo rje snang byed dag pa'i mi//mchod pa sngags rigs pa yis bri//gzugs dang sgra dang ro dang reg/rang gi bdag po'i phyogs su'o//'dab mar
7a5(rgyud): thams cad nyid du gnas//zhi dang skyeg dang dbyangs chen mo//gar gyi phyag rgya grags chen mo//sngags pas 'bad de bri bar bya//pad ma las skyes 'dab ma la//thams cad du ni
7a6(rgyud): grub pa bri//sgo rnams dag ni thams cad du//sgo skyong ba ni rnam bzhi dang //phyi rol du mi rnams la sogs//ming ni yang dag bri bar bya//de nas khrus byas lugs byugs la//
7a7(rgyud): me tog gdugs sogs kyis gdugs te//sna tshogs gos ni rab gyon la//me tog 'phreng dang gos gyis brgyan//gtsug phud gcod phan dang ldan zhing //mdzes pa'i dar gyis yang dag
7a8(rgyud): klubs rdo rje dril bu lag par ldan//sbyor ba bzang po'i rnam zhig gis//de nas thams cad dgug par bya//rdo rje ma mo dka' thub che//bzlog dang rnal 'byor ma'i phyag rgya yis//rdo rje
7b1(rgyud): sems dpa' bzlas nas su //rdo rje'i sbyor bas gar bya zhing //lan gsum du ni g.yas bskor bas //rdo rje ma mo'i dkyil 'khor rnams //dam tshig ldan pa thams cad byon //klu 
7b2(rgyud): dbyangs kyi ni rgyal pos bskul //rang gi slob ma'i tshogs kyis ni //dris pa yis ni g.yas dang g.yon//'gying ba dang ni 'dud par bya //'dir gshegs 'dir gshegs sems
7b3(rgyud): can don mdzad brtson pa po//ser sna mi mnga' sangs rgyas tshogs mchog mtha' dag dang //bde gshegs dam pa'i yum 'gyur srid pa'i skyed pa po//pha rol don mdzad dus la de ring ba 
7b4(rgyud): lags ces so//de nas de dag rang nyid kyis //rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul las byon//mtshan dag ni bzang po rnams //dkyil 'khor dag du lta bar bya//rang ngam
7b5(rgyud): gzhan la 'bab sogs pa'i //mtshan ma ji ltar mthong ba na //de nas de dag rang nyad kyis //rdzu 'phrul rnamapar 'phrul las byon//mtsham ma dag ni bzang po
7b6(rgyud): rnams //dkyil 'khor dang du lta bar bya //rang ngam gzhan la 'bab sogs pa' //mtshan ma ji ltar mthod ba na //de tshe nges par ma mo ni//bde gshegs thugs kyis
7b7(rgyud): dgongs par byon //de nas mchod yon zhabs gsil cho ga bzhin rab brtags te //de tshe 'babs pa'i sems can bsod nams ldan la bri bar bya//gal te de ni me smra ba na hU~M
7b8(rgyud): gi ye ge dang /ldan pa'i klu dbyangs can gyis yongs su bskor ba'i nyid du bya//de nas shes rab ldan pas gzhug //slob ma'i gtso bo thal sbyar te //mig la mig dar gyis g.yogs te
8a1(rgyud): //rab dga' bdag yin yang dag dga'//ma mo thams cad la dmigs te//der ni me tog dor bar bya//me tog gang la lhang gyur ba'i//ma mo de ni de yis 'grub//de nas
8a2(rgyud): go rims ji bzhin du//sgo skyong ba nas rtsams nas su//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i bar//slob ma la ni bshad par bya//dam tshig thos par gyur nas su//sdom pa dag ni bzung bar bya//
8a3(rgyud): ma mo'i sngags dang phyag rgya ni//'gro mnyam par gzhag par bstan par bya//gzhan du slob dpon blo ngan ni//nges par dmyal bar 'dod bar 'gyur//des kyang yang dag mi 'grub cing /
8a4(rgyud): myur du dmyal bar 'gro bar 'gyur ba yin//gyegs dang log par 'dren pa 'am//rims dang gdon dang dug sogs kyis//gdungs te nad kyis rgyun du ni//'chi ba ru
8a5(rgyud): yang 'gyur ba yin//rdo rje slob dpon smad pa can//rtag tu dmyal ba nyid du ni//rab tu rmongs pa'i slob ma 'gro//mkha' las rdo rje ji bzhin no//des bu ke dam tshig ni//
8a6(rgyud): khyod kyis rtag tu bsrung bar byos//ma mo kun gyi sdom pa ni//theg pa chen po'i cho gas gnas//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs te//rdo rje
8a7(rgyud): 'dzin pa thams cad kyi yum rnams kyi//dam tshig bka' stsal pa//rin chen sems kyi dam tshig gis//rnam pa gsum ni sbyang bar bya//thog mtha' bar gyi sbyor bya yis//chung 'bring mchog
8a8(rgyud): gi dbye ba yis//de 'dra'i dam tshig yongs dag pa//sangs rgyas kun gyis bka' stsal to//ji sems pa ni sbyangs kyi bar//'bad pa yis ni bsgom par bya//bud med thams cad mthong ba ni//
8b1(rgyud): lan gsum du ni bskor nas kyang //spyi bos phyag ni byas nas su//lan gsum bar du gsol ba bdab //bdag gi ma khyod bu yin te //ji srid byang chub thob par du //byang chub yan lag
8b2(rgyud): nu ma las //byung ba'i rang gi 'o mas skyongs //de nas sbyor ldan rab gang ba'i //dbang skur ba ni sbyin par bya//gser la sogs pa'i bum ba ru //dri'i chus ni yongs bkang la //dar dang 
8b3(rgyud): ras gsogs kyis g.yogs te //me tog chun pos mgul brgyan nas //rin chen sman gyis bkang ba'i //gcig lhag bcu drug bzang po la //ma mo'i snying po nyid kyis ni //brgya 
8b4(rgyud): brtsa brgyad du bzlas pa yis//sngags shes pas ni dbang bskur ro//slob ma zhi ba bzang po yis//thog mar zhu ba byas nas ni //zhabs gnyis la ni 'gos btud nas;;
8b5(rgyud): bdag ni che ge mo rdo rje che //byang chub sems ni rnam bsgom pa'o//slob dpon zhabs kyi chu skyes la //dbang bskur phyir ni zhu ba bgyid //skal pa chen po bdag la stsol//
8b6(rgyud): slob dpon mchog du rmad byung ba //dbang bskur rgyal ba thams cad kyis //sangs rgyas byang chub rab bsgrub pa //de ni bdag gis sems can rnams //don gyi dam pa 
8b7(rgyud): bdag gis bgyi //sangs rgyas kun gyis don bgyid pa //rtul shugs bzang po khyod kyi drin //de nas dbang skur sbyor ba yis //rdo rje sems dpa'i go 'phang gnas //slob ma 'ang
8b8(rgyud): ye shes rdo rje yi //go 'phang la ni gzhag nas su //bum pa bzang po gcig gam ni //yang na bcu drug dag gam ni //lnga 'am yang na bcu yis ni//dkyil 'khor du ni dbang bskur ro//
9a1(rgyud): @#//dkyil 'khor gyi ni 'dun du ni//dkyil 'khor pad ma bri bar bya//pad ma lo ma brgyad pa las //ge sar lte ba ldan pa dri //de'i dbus su rdo rje bri //sna tshogs rdo rje 'od zer 'phreng
9a2(rgyud): de'i mdun du 'khor lo bri//chen po rin chen bdun gyis bskor//lho'i phyogs su rin po che//nub du pad ma nyid yin te//byang chub du ral gri chen po'o//'bar ba'i phreng 'khrugs 'od zer
9a3(rgyud): can //grwa'i phyogs ni thams cad du //me tog gdug pa dri zhim pa //'od ldan pa ni skal pa che //sgo dang sgo ru bzhi nyid do//phyi rol du 'ang mi 'chi kun //gcig nas gcig du rgyal 
9a4(rgyud): sgrogs pa //gang byed bcer bu'i cha lugs te //ming thos pa dang ldan pa'o//dkyil 'khor de'i dbus su ni//slob ma snyems dang bcas pa gzhag //chos kyi rang bzhin
9a5(rgyud): gtam spyod de//de ni khyod ni rigs su skyes//'khor lo sgyur ba'i rgyal po che //rtag pa dam pa'i dbang phyug pa //thabs dang shes rab ldan pa'i dpal //dag
9a6(rgyud): pa'i dbang phyug kun gyi rgyal //rdo rje pad ma lag phyin te //gnang ba bzang po rab tu sbyin //bu yis bya ba chen po gyis//sangs rgyas kun gyi go 'phang gnas //der ni sbyor ba 'ongs nas
9a7(rgyud): su//gsang ba'i sdom pa sbyin par bya //'di ni mchog tu dga' ba ste //yum rnams rab tu rmad du byung ba'o//dus su 'dod pa nyid du gyis//dam tshig mchog du gyur pa'o//brtul shugs
9a8(rgyud): bzang pos ci 'byor bas //de nas yon ni phul nas su//slob dpon gyi ni zhabs la btud //ma dang chung ma bu mo dang //sring mo sring mo'i bu mo dang //bu rnams dang ni bdag nyid dang//
9b1(rgyud): rgyal po longs spyod gter dang ni //nor dang 'bru dang grong shing rta //glang po rta dang ba glang sogs//ma he dang ni ma he mo//gzhan dang gzhan yang ci 'dod pa //bla ma la ni chen pos dbul //
9b2(rgyud): bya dang mi bya rdzas kyi mchog//thams cad kyang ni dbul bar bya //de nas bcom ldan 'das rnams la //rgyan 'phreng rnams dang gzhan gang dang //sgyeg dang rtse 'jo dga' ba dang //rab
9b3(rgyud): tu dga' ba'i bde chen dang //bza' btung bca' ba la sogs pa //rnam pa du ma thams cad kyis//mchod nas gshegs su gsol bar bya //sems can kun gyi don dang ni//bdag
9b4(rgyud): gi yang ni ches cher mdzad //bder gshegs gang du gshegs pa der//ma mo thams cad gshegs su gsol;/shes rab ldan pas yon phul te //slob ma mchog dang 
9b5(rgyud): bcas pa yis//gar byas lag pa mgor gzhag ste //rims kyis 'gyed dang 'dud par //de nas bzang po rnam mnyes te //thams cad bsgrub par byed pa'i
9b6(rgyud): mchog //bdag gi bskal bzang bsal nas kyang //rang gi sras kyi gnas su gshegs//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs rdo rje ye shes yum chen mo rnams kyi phyag rgya 
9b7(rgyud): chen po'i ye shes kyi 'khor lo yongs su rdzogas+h+yo////de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi yum //bcom ldan 'das ma rnams kyis yang gsang ba 'dus pa las
9b8(rgyud): byung ba/rang rang gi bsgrub pa'i thabs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad zhus so//shes rab ye shes mchog bsgrub pa'i //sems dpa' chen pos gsung du gsol//thabs kyis
10a1(rgyud): //sems can thams cad kun //sangs rgyas byang chub 'bras thob pa //rdo rje ye shes mchog gyur pa //rdo rje sku ste rdo rje mchog//dngos grub kun gyi bde ster ba //rdo rje chen pos
10a2(rgyud): gsung du gsol //thugs kyi rin chen ldan pa'i mchog//gzi brjid rdo rje snang ba can //sems can rnams kyi rdzu 'phrul dang //bsgrub pa'i yon tan 'byung gnas gsungs //
10a3(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi rnal 'byor che //dpa' bo'i sbyor bas rab gnas pa'i //dpa' chen pos bshad du gsol //las kun gyi ni 'bras brnyes pas //sna tshogs mkhyen pa dbang phyug
10a4(rgyud): che//sems can rnams kyi yab chen po //sna tshogs dam pas bshad du gsol //las kun gyi ni 'bras bzhes byaso//de nas bcom ldan 'das rtag pa'i rgyal //
10a5(rgyud): dam pa'i dbang phyug kun mkhyen pas //gsung mchog nam mkha' dang mtshungs pa'o//zab mo'i ni chos bka' stsal 'di ni bsgrub po gang yang med//dngos grub
10a6(rgyud): bam ni sgrub thabs kyang //thams cad rnam par rtog las byung //rnam rtog kyang ni rigs ma yin//bdag dang sangs rgyas rnams kyang med//khyod rnams med cing chos nyid med
10a7(rgyud): sbyor ba med cing sems can med//de bzhin pha ma yang med//byed pa med cing skyong ba med//'bras bu med cing rgyu yod min//long spyod med cing zab mo med //shes med shes pa
10a8(rgyud): po yang med //rnam shes med cing shes bya med //brdzun min bden pa 'ang ma yin pa //des na snang ba thams cad ni //sgyu ma lta bu nyid las byung //de ltar chos rnams rnam rig na
10b1(rgyud): grol nas gos par mi 'gyur te //dngos grub chen po 'grub par 'gyur //sangs rgyas byang chub bde chen po//zab mo'i chos ni thos pa na //mkhas pa gang zhig dang gyur na//
10b2(rgyud): snying po phyag rgya sngags rig rnams //gdon mi za bar 'grub par 'gyur //sbyor ba 'di ni bcom ldan gsungs //thams cad byed ga par byed pa'i mchog//gsang ba'i rgyan la
10b3(rgyud): legs bzhugs nas //sems can rnams la brtan pa'o//de nas yum rnams tshim 'gyur te //rang byung ba la zhu ba zhus //sgrub pa'i sgrub thabs rab sbyor ba //mnga' bdag
10b4(rgyud): rnal 'byor rnams kyis gsungs //de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yum las kyi dam tshig 'byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting
10b5(rgyud): nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas //'di skad ces bka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya //de'i steng du mnyam gzhag pas //
10b6(rgyud): nam mkha'i mtha' las khyab pa'i //phyogs kun du ni rab 'phro ba'i//de'i steng du gzi brjid che //dus mda'i me dang 'tshungs 'dra ba //de'i steng du sa chen po //gser gyi
10b7(rgyud): ri bo dang mtshungs pa //de'i steng du tshur shes bya //zla ba gang ba kha 'dra ba //sngags pa yis ni cho ga bsam //rin chen bzhi ni rang bzhin bzang //stong phrag brgya bcu rtsa bzhi'i
10b8(rgyud): dpag tshad kyis ni tshad tsam pa //gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa //rta babs bzhi'i rtse mo can //ri bo dag ni bcog brgyad pa //dbus su rdo rje'i ri bo mchog//de'i steng du rdo rje che//
11a1(rgyud): //kun du chogs pas yongs su bskor//sngo sangs ma ra ka ta 'dra //sna tshogs 'od kyis yang dag 'khrugs //de'i steng du rdo rje che//rtse mo lnga pa rnam par bsam //kar po shel dang
11a2(rgyud): 'dra ba po //'od zer lnga yis rnam par spras //de'i steng du rin po che//chu skyes sngon po'i 'dab 'dra ba //sna tshogs 'od zer stong gi 'phreng //phyogs bcu nam mkha' khyab pa po//de'i gtsug
11a3(rgyud): tu pad ma che //nam mkha' khyab pa'i ni mchog//lo ma stong phrag du ma can //lte ba dang ni ze 'brus dkrigs//de'i mdun du nyi ma che //'od zer stong phrag du ma can /de'i steng du zla ba che//
11a4(rgyud): dkar po'i 'od zer 'bar bas dkrigs//nam mkha'i khams ni kun gang ba'i//gzi brjid dkar dang 'phro ba po//de'i dbus su ma mo bri //dus gsum gzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas
11a5(rgyud): kyang //yongs su dag pa mkha' 'dra ma //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor 'phro ba mo //phyag gnyis ma la thugs zhig //bsam gtan spyan sngar bsam gtan yid //bsam gtan
11a6(rgyud): mchog gi phyag rgya'i //sor mo bzhin nu rab gzhag pa //dkar po'i gos 'dzin mdzes pa'o//dkar po yis ni brgyan gyis brgyan //ral ba'i thor tshugs gcod pan bzang //gtsug gi nor
11a7(rgyud): bus 'od ldan pa//ye shes bdan la mnyam par bzhugs //ba sku rnams kyi rtse phug nas //rgyal ba'i bar du rab 'phros te //byang chub pha rol son pa mdzad //de'i thugs kar rnam bsams
11a8(rgyud): pa //ye ge mchog du mi 'gyur ba //yi ge rnams kyis mnga' bdag dkrigs//thams cad du ni legs 'phro ba //sangs rgyas rnams ni 'dun gnas pa //rab tu thal mo sbyar ba'o//
11b1(rgyud): de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' rnams //rigs sngags khro bo'i tshogs kyang ni //chos nyan pa'i tshul du yang //dkyil 'khor du ni bzhug par bya //sgo'i phyogs ni thams cad du //sgo skyong
11b2(rgyud): ba ni bzhi rnams so//sbyin pa laswogs ci rigs par //bcu drug po 'ang gzhag par bya //me tog bdug pa mer me che//ji ltar ba bzhin bzhag par bya //lha klu de bzhin gnod sbyin dang //
11b3(rgyud): dri za lha min dmyal las byung //'di rnams thams cad lhun po la //kun nas yongs gzhag par bya //de nas me tog 'phreng ldan 'jug //dri dang me tog yang dag ldan //gos kar 'dzin
11b4(rgyud): pa dpal dang ldan //brgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par brgyan //shes rab 'phro dang lhan cig du //rdo rje sems dpa' rjes dran pas //bdag dag pas ni dag par
11b5(rgyud): bya //shes rab sbyor bas gang gnas pas //sgo'i drung du legs 'khod la //klu 'di yis ni spyan drang bya //thugs rje mnga' ba chos kyi dbang phyug sangs rgyas yum
11b6(rgyud): rang gi yab kyi tshogs dang bcas dang bcas te byon //smyon cig smyon cig lha dang srin por bcas mchod pa'i //'jig rten dbang phyug yang dag byang chub thugs mnga' ba //smyon cig
11b7(rgyud): smyon cig thugs rje'i bdag nyid 'jig rten gnyen //chos kyi yi ge bde bar gshegs pas rjes bskos pa //mkha' ltar yongs dag dri med dam bcas dran par mdzad //ma mo
11b8(rgyud): rnams kyi mchog gi klus bskul byon par 'gyur //de nas bcom ldan 'das ma byon par mthong nas //rang gi me tog gi snyim pa dang mchod yon dang zhabs gsil dbul bar bya'o//
12a1(rgyud): @#//oM bdag gi mchod yon zhabs gsil 'di//ci bde bar ni gzhes su gsol //de nas de dang lhan cig tu /'dres par ru ni gyur par bsam //oM barba ta thA ga ta pra dzA
12a2(rgyud): nyA na sa ma ye sdom//de nas 'di yis brtan par bya ste //oM sarba ta thA ga te tsa le sa ma ye mA tsa la tAM//de nas 'dis mnyes par bya //oM sarba ta thA gra ta ma na san tu s+Te sa ma ye
12a3(rgyud): maM paM hU~M/e ha hi ya dza /A yA hi dza /de nas dpag tu med pa'i ngo mtshar chen po lta ba'i phyir/oM sarba oM ga sun da ri ma hA b+ha gi bad sa le smra ra smra ra spa ra spa ra /sarba a g+ha a ba ya
12a4(rgyud): beSha ma hI sa mu tra hU~M hU~M gnyis oM //'di yis ma mo bskul nas su//sangs rgyas sprin ni 'od po che//dkyil 'khor sa la spro bar bya //de nas gsang sngags 'di yis ni
12a5(rgyud): sngags te me tog do+ir bar bya//oM sarba a ga sun da ri ti tsha daM ku su ma a na dza li ma hU~M /oM de nas /ji ltar gzhed pa bzhin du /dkyil 'khor gyi bum pa rnams
12a6(rgyud): la /rigs sngags 'dis ji ltar bshad pa ltar legs par byin gyis rlabs la rang gi dbang bskur rang gi snying po rang gis blang bar bya'o//de nas las cad kyi nus pa dang ldan
12a7(rgyud): pa dang /dngos grub thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//de nas me tog la sogs dang //bza' dang btung dang ro dag gis //mchod la sngon gyi rim pa bzhin //slob ma'i rgyal po 
12a8(rgyud): bzhug par bya //'khor lo sgyur ba'i rim pa yi//sbyor ba yis ni dbang bskur ro //rdo rje rin chen de bzhin pad //las kyi rdo rje de bzhin no//zhags pa lcags kyu dril bu dang /
12b1(rgyud): sgrogs dang rnga yab dang ni gdugs //ral gri mig sman thur ma dang //rgyal po'i nye bar spyod pa dang //de la brgyan rnams stan nas su //dkyil 'khor der ni dbang bskur ro //rgyal
12b2(rgyud): ba'i yum gyi dbang bskur bas //las kun byed par rab tu bshad //dam tshig gam ni sdom pa yang //rang gi slob ma nyan du bzhug //zhi ba dang ni rgyas pa'i las //dbang dang 
12b3(rgyud): drag shul spyod pa ni //legs par rnam par dag sems kyis//ci 'dod par ni bya ba byos//las rnams kyi ni 'bras bu yang //sbyor 'di yis 'grub par 'gyur //de nas
12b4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' chen pos slar yang las dam pa rgyas par bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas 'di skad ces bka' 
12b5(rgyud): stsal to//ji ltar gzugs kyi snod byas nas //dkyil 'khor 'di ni dbus su gzhag //zho dang mar gsar 'o ma ni //tsan dan dmar po dang bsres te //ga bur dang ni 
12b6(rgyud): ldan byas la //yid la byed pas bzlas te 'grub //zla ba nyi ma bzas bzung tshe //sbyor ba bzang po'i rnam gzhag bya //thog mar me ni spar byas nas //'bad pa yis ni rlung gis bus
12b7(rgyud): gser gyi snod du bsdu bar bya //ba ru na'i khu bas spang //nyi ma dang ni zla ba la //slar yang rdzas de bskam par bya //de nas de ru lha mo'i //khrag dang sa bon blugs par bya //de yis 
12b8(rgyud): myur du rdzas dag ni//'grub par 'gyur te the tshom med //grub nas thob mar lha mo la //cung zad tsam cig sbyin par bya//de bzhin du ni slob dpon la //skal pa gnyis 'gyur dbul bar
13a1(rgyud): @#//brgya /de nas grub pa gzhan rnams dang //rang gis thog mar skal pa sbyin //de nas byin rlabs sbyor ba yis//phyag rgya dang ni lhan cig tu //rdo rje sems dpa'i ting 'dzin gyis
13a2(rgyud): zas kyi sa bon dang ni bza' //skad de la gzugs rjes te //rdo rje sems dpa' lta bur 'gyur//gang dang gang gis gzugs rnams su//bsgrub pa 'dod pa'i sngags pa yis //de'i
13a3(rgyud): dang de'i bsam gtan gyis//blo gros gtan pas bza' bar bya//de dang de'i gzugs su ni//gdon mi za bar rje bar 'gyur //gzhan pa yi ni rdzas dag dang //'di nyid kyis ni bsgrub
13a4(rgyud): par bya //mig sman gyis ni mig byugs pas //khor yug du ni mi snang 'gro //snying la ldong ros gyis byugs nas //yid kyi spyod yul nyid du 'gro//bab la yis ni
13a5(rgyud): lus byugs na //mkha' la nye bar 'gro bar 'gyur //mig sman nag po blangs nas su //nag po'i phyogs la bza' bar bya //de'i gshang ba'i mar khu las //
13a6(rgyud): dud pa mkhas pas bsgrub par bya//de yis mig la byugs na ni//mkha' la nye bar 'gro bar 'gyur //sangs rgyas kyis kyang mi gzigs shing //stobs ni dpal ldan rdo rje
13a7(rgyud): mtshon //nyid 'gyur rga dang 'chi ba med//mig sman dang ni ba lang mar //ma he dang ni ra nyid dang //ma'i mig smig nyid dag ni //mkhas pa yis ni blangs nas ni //
13a8(rgyud): sngags pas bzung ste btso bar bya//mig sman chen po mchog du bzang //'di yis mig la byugs na ni //grub pa yid du 'ong bar 'gyur //mi snang grub pa thams cad kyi //blo 
13b1(rgyud): gros chen po rgyal bar 'gyur //sangs rgyas kyis sa chen po //nges par thob 'gyur gzhan du min //rdzas kyi gnas skabs le'u 'di //mtshungs med chungs med po nye bar bshad //
13b2(rgyud): gos dang gdugs dang gsogs ral gri dang //mtshon chad ma pa bsgrub par bya //snying po snying khar byas nas nas ni //goms na myur du 'grub par 'gyur //rdo rje sems
13b3(rgyud): dpa' rgyal po chos//pad ma'i sems dpa' bzhin ras mchog//de'i zhal du byas nas su //de dang ldan par sgom par bya //mnga' bdag gar gyi dbang phyug ltar //skad cig
13b4(rgyud): de la de 'gyur ro //de nyid kyi ni spyi bo'i cher //mchog gi dam pa bsam gtan bya //skad cig de la mchod pa che'i //sangs rgyas su ni de 'gyur ro //de'i spyi gtsug nyid
13b5(rgyud): du ni //sna tshogs rdo rje sgom par bya //de ni kun gyi rgyal dbang po //las kun byed pa'i don grub 'gyur //rdo rje snying khar bsam par bya //de'i dbus su snying po lta
13b6(rgyud): de nas rdo rje rig pa 'dzin //mnga' bdag rdo rje'i dbang po 'grub //rin chen chen po che brgyad pa //snying kha ru ni mkhas pas sgom //der ni snying po bsams pa yis //rin chen rig
13b7(rgyud): pa 'dzin par 'gyur //pad ma chen po 'dab brgya pa //yid la bsgoms pas skad cig la //pad ma che ldan mnga' bdag gi //pad ma'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur //de la sogs
13b8(rgyud): pa mtha' yas pa //ji lta'i sbyor ba'i cho ga yis //mchog dang dman pa 'bring po'i //dngos grub dam pa bsgrub par bya //snying kar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam //yi ge dam
14a1(rgyud): @#//po sgom par bya//ji ltar sbyor bas dkyil 'khor gyis //thams cad bdag du bsgom par bya //de las langs de bdag zhi bas //zla 'od kha ba ltar gsil ba'i //'od zer
14a2(rgyud): sprin gyis cher gtams pa //sgrub bya'i spyi bor gzhag par bya //skad cig de la gdug par bya //gza' dang dug sogs rab 'phrog cig//nyi ma gcig gam bdun du ni //bsgrub bya zhi
14a3(rgyud): 'gyur gzhan du min //rin chen dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par //snying po'i mchog ni bsgom par bya//de las langs te bdag zhi bas //'od zer ser po'i 'phreng 'dzes pa //bsgoms pas
14a4(rgyud): de ni rgyas par byed//pad ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnaspar //mnyam par gzhag pas lha mo bsam //dmar po'i rang bzhin 'bar dag pa //bsgrub bya sems la 'phro bar 
14a5(rgyud): bya//slar yang bsnyud pa'i sbyor ba yis //rang gi lus la bzhug par bya //'khor lo sgyur ba lta bu yang //de nas ci nas dbang du 'gro //las kyi dkyil 'khor rab 
14a6(rgyud): gnas par //de'i dbus su yi ge ni//mar dkar 'od 'dra sgom par bya //'od zer sprin gyis yang dag 'khrugs //mkhas pa yis ni snying kar bsam //bsgrub bya la ni snying po
14a7(rgyud): 'od //stims pa'i skad cig de la 'bab //rdo rje gar gyis gar byed cing //phyag rgya sbyor ba'i rnal 'byor dang //rdo rje rnal 'byor ma'i sbyor ba'i //phyag rgyas thams cad ston par 
14a8(rgyud): 'gyur//'das dang ma 'ongs ci 'dod dge mi dge//lce rkyang bas ni smra bar byed //'dod dang mi 'dod dge mi dge //rdo rje ngag gis smra bar 'gyur //yang na legs par sbyar ba 'am //
14b1(rgyud): grong gi tha mar skad dag gis//'dod dang mi 'dod dge mi dge //'tsho ba 'am ni 'chi ba dang //nyams dang mi nyams rang bzhin gyi //de dag thams cad de yis smra//de las gzhan du de mi smra//
14b2(rgyud): 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje can //dbang bdag rdo rje'i dbyangs kyis gsungs //khros te lce la rtser sgoms nas ni //lha mo yi ni snying po mchog //lcags kyu'i rnam pa spros nas su//
14b3(rgyud): phyag rgya dang ni phyag rgya'i lan //rlung la gzhag ste skad gcig la //khrag rnams thams cad dgug par bya //char rgyun 'bab pa'i sbyor ba yis //rang gi kha ru dbab par bya//de ni 'thungs 
14b4(rgyud): pas sngags shes te //mnga' bdag rdo rje 'dzin bar 'gyur //nam mkha' kun du sbyor dpal ldan //nyi ma'i snang ba lta bur 'gyur //'di nyid kyi ni rnal 'byor gyis //'o ma zho dang 
14b5(rgyud): mar bzang po //mar khu chu dang me tog kun //rlung gis dgug pa nyid du bya //zhags pas 'grin pa bcings byas te //lcags kyu yis ni snying kha nas //rlung gis
14b6(rgyud): kun nas bteg nas su//lha mo de bzhin gnod sbyin mo //ma mo de bzhin lha min mo //brgyad rgya kho na bzlas pas ni //bud med tshul du dgug par bya//bsgrub byed bsgrub byed ming
14b7(rgyud): spel nas //bka' ba de bzhin rtsig pa khyim //dril bu rnams dang bum rnams la 'ang //lha mo'i ni snying po ni //brgya bzlas pas ni 'bebs par 'gyur //shes rab pha rol phyin
14b8(rgyud): ma mchog //khro bo dang ni yang dag ldan //bsal byed kyis ni bsal byas pas //las rnams dag ni thams cad bya //dur khrod ro ni ma nyams pa//mar ngo'i rgyad la sdus nasu
15a1(rgyud): @#//byang du phyogs te gzhag par bya//de'i khar ni mnyam gzhag pas //gsang sngags legs par bzlas nas su //rdzas kyi dam pa ci rigs pa //bab la mig sman rdo chung 
15a2(rgyud): dang //ldong ros dang yang yang na btsag //kar rtsi sol ba nyid dang ni //gur gum dang ni rgya spos sas//ru rta shu dag ga bur dang //tsan dan de bzhin a ka ru//sha dang khrag ni nyid 
15a3(rgyud): dang ni //de bzhin sor mo'i gdu bu ni //rnal 'byor ldan pas gzhag nas kyang //de'i ngang la sdad la zla //nyi ma shar nas de blangs nas //grub pa'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur //
15a4(rgyud): dman pas dngos grub dman pa ste //rab kyis rab tu rnam par bstan //bar mas bar mar shes par bya //mtshan ma med na dbang du byed//zhes so//skyes bu dag ni 
15a5(rgyud): thams cad dang //bud med dag ni thams cad la//reg pa tsam gyis dbang du byed //mig la byugs pa yis kyang ngo//tsan dan gyis ni yongs byugs te //gum gum
15a6(rgyud): gyis ni thig le'o//la gu yis ni gdug par bya //kar rtsi sol ba tsag gis ni //ji ltar 'dod pa'i ming dang ni //yongs su spel te bris pa yis//dbang ma song ba dbang 
15a7(rgyud): 'gro ste //rdo rje sems dpa'i bka' bzhin no //khams kyi ming ni bris pa na //dbang du byed pa'i mchog yin no //sol bas yongs su 'gugs par byed //kar rtsi yis ni 
15a8(rgyud): dug 'joms so //li khris skad cig de nyid la //snyo bar 'gyur bar the tsom med //ru rta a ka rus gdugs pas //gnod sbyin mo ni nges par 'gugs //sha dang khrag gi gdug pa
15b1(rgyud): yIs//srin po 'gugs par byed pa'i mchog//tsan dan bla rtsi bdug pa yis//lha ni 'gugs par byed pa'i mchod//ga bur tsan dan bdug pa yis//sangs rgyas mchog tu spyan 
15b2(rgyud): 'dren pa'o//ba bur nyid ni zos pa yis//gzhan du 'ang de'i dbang du 'gyur//rig pa chen mo yi ge bzhi//mnyam par sbyor bzhag pa'i sbyor ba yis//'khrug pa dang ni spel nas su//
15b3(rgyud): nges par shi ba bsgrub par bya//brtul shugs can gyis gang sgoms pa'i//bka' lung de yis myur du byed//phrag par khur te dran pa des//lhun po'i steng du skyel bar 'gyur//
15b4(rgyud): grub pa yi ni mchog rab kyi//rdzas ni blangs nas 'ong bar 'gyur//rtum pa'i yi dgas nyi ma'i//rgyal gyi nyi mo sol ba bzung //rtum po'i me tog khu ba yis//brtul
15b5(rgyud): shugs bzang pos btags nas//pad ma'i nang du gzhag par bya//de nyid mtshan mo gcig gam gsum//de nas blangs nas sbyor ba yis//mkhas pa 
15b6(rgyud): ni mar khu bya//de nas lha 'am grub pa 'am//sangs rgyas kyis kyang sngags pa ni//rigs sngags grub pa de'i grib pa 'am//gdon mi za bar mthong mi 'gyur//'gro dang mi 'gro'i 
15b7(rgyud): skye bo rnams//dbang ma grub pa 'ang dbang du byed//rang gi khrag gis ming bris la//de nyid kyis ni phye mas bkang //ji srid skams par ma gyur bar//de srid mi de 'ong bar 'gyur
15b8(rgyud): gang dang gang gi lha dag gi//rim pa 'di yis ming bris te//khro bo ldan pas bzlas par bya//de 'dis skad cig de la 'grub//de'i gzhan gzhin byed pa dang //ngang gis de ni dbang 
16a1(rgyud): //du 'gyur //dkon mchog gsum la gnod byed pa //gsod pa'i don du sngags rig pas //'bar ba'i 'phreng bas 'khrugs pa'i 'od//zhal bzhi pa la cher 'jigs pa //
16a2(rgyud): zhabs la lcags kyu thod pa can //bdag nyid myur du bsam par bya//gsang sngags dang ni spel ba'i //ming ni sol bas byugs nas su //bdug pa de ni gnan nas su//rang gi
16a3(rgyud): bdug pa'i sems kyis snun //'di nyid kyi ni ting 'dzin gyis //rengs par ru 'ang nges par byed //khams kyi sa bon dang spel ba'i //khams kyis ming ni bris nas su //khams de
16a4(rgyud): yang dag gnan nas nas //khang pa ru ni rgyun du gzhag //de nas rgya ru rengs 'gyur te //de srid du ni mi g.yor gnas //rma bya'i sgro 'am gtsug phud ni //yongs su blangs
16a5(rgyud): la bsgrub pa bya//rma bya zhes bya'i rtsa ba ni //mkhas pa yis ni yongs su bzung //rmag la sngags rig pas bskor bas //de nas rmag dpung 'jig par dang //rmongs pa
16a6(rgyud): 'am ni rengs pa 'am //skad cig de la 'khor bar 'gyur //khyi dang phag dang bya rog gi //mig ni yongs su blangs nas su //mig sman dang ni mnyam por sbyar //lha mo'i sngags kyis sngags
16a7(rgyud): pa yis //bsgrub pa po'i gzugs rnams kun //'jig par 'gyur bar the tsom med //btsan Da'i me tog bcud kyis ni //lcags kyus g.yogs pa'i sbyor ba yis//de ni kha ru nges bcug pas
16a8(rgyud): rga shi med par rnam par rgyu //rims gtong gzugs ni legs bris //de'i kha ru gnod byed ming //sbyor ba yis ni bcug nas su //yongs su mnan la shin du zlas //rims gtong de'i gzugs
16b1(rgyud): ni legs bris te //de'i kha ru gnod byed ming //sbyor ba yis ni bcug nas su //yongs su mnan la shin du zlas //rims gtong de'i gzugs kyis ni//rab tu 'dzin par gnon mi za //
16b2(rgyud): bza' 'am yang na yi dgas sam //gnod sbyin dang ni sprin po 'am //gzhan pa yi ni nad rnams kyis //'dzin pa la ni spyad mi dgos//gshin rje bris te bsrang par bya //de'i zhal du drag po
16b3(rgyud): che //rmongs pa'i bdag nyid mi 'gro na //de ni myur du nges par 'chi //gsad pa la ni yang 'di nyid kyis //yongs su mnan nas nges par zla //gang du gter ni yod snyam gyur //der ni sngags
16b4(rgyud): pa rig pas song ste //lce yongs bzung la bzlas pa bya //de yis gsal po'i tshig gis smra //tshad dang yod dang med pa dang //bgegs dang mi bgegs ji ltar gnas
16b5(rgyud): ji srid de ni bgegs smra na //de la gtor ma sbyin par bya //gal te bgegs ni mi smra na //der ni mchod pa yon rab tu rtag //pus mo gnyis btsugs gus pa yi(sa)
16b6(rgyud): nor 'dzin sangs rgyas yum la'o//phyogs bzhi dag du phur bus gdab //de nas gnas rnams bskad par bya //de nas mtshan ni bcing bar bya //mnyam par gzhag ste (ka) (ka) (ka) (ka) sor bar bya //
16b7(rgyud): sbyor ba shes pas sngags 'di yis//de nas rdzas rnams gdon bar bya //oM ba su d+ha sarba thA sa d+ha ni sA d+ha ya sA d+ha ye/u d+ha ra u d+ha ra /rak+sha rak+sha /sarba rwa ni tsa ya~M/traM traM tsa tsa Ta Ta ha ha swahA/
16b8(rgyud): yang na rig pa 'di yis kyang //las rnams dag ni thams cad bya //phyogs bcing ba dang de bzhin bsrung //phur bus skrad pa de bzhin no//rin chen gsum ni thog mar sbyin //cha gcig dag
17a1(rgyud): //ni nor 'dzin la'o//sdug bsngal can la cha gcig go//grogs rnams dag ni de bzhin mtshungs //lhag ma rnams ni rigs pas spyad //dkon mchog gsum la legs mchod 
17a2(rgyud): bya //gzhan du sdug bsngal thob 'gyur te //rgyal po chom rkun sogs paso //gal te bla ma de bzhugs te //skal ba bzang po gnyis 'bul lo//gzhan pa yi ni spun rnams la //rdo rje theg rtogs
17a3(rgyud): 'gyur ba'i //skal pa re re sbyin par bya //gzhan du dam tshig gdung bar 'gyur //'di ni bcom ldan 'das rnal 'byor can //gsang ba rgyan gyi rdo rjes gsungs //de nas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi //
17a4(rgyud): yum rnams kyis ni der bdud te //dpa' bo che la zhu ba zhus //rgyas pa yin dkyil 'khor ni //gsungs cig gsungs //gcig dpa' bo che //yum rnams kun gyi dkyil 'khor ni //
17a5(rgyud): rgyal po'i dbang phyug rab gsol ba //sems can thams cad 'dir 'byung ba'o //de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi //bdag po'i dbang phyug dpal zhes
17a6(rgyud): bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la mnyam par gzhag nas //nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo //dkyil 'khor ji ltar sngar gsungs pa //'on kyang seng ge'i khri dang ldan //de'i steng du pad ma che/
17a7(rgyud): der ni ri bong can gyis dkyil 'khor //de'i steng du nyi ma che //nyi ma'i der na dkyil 'khor mchog//cha brgyad pa la dbus rin chen //de'i steng du rgyal po'i yum //ser dang sngo ba'i 'od chen po //
17a8(rgyud): gser gyi ri bo dang 'tshungs pa //phyag gnyis kyis ni chos 'chad cing //g.yas pa yis ni mchog sbyin pa //g.yon gis rin chen lan bu mchog //rgyan rnams kun gyis rnams par brgyan //gos
17b1(rgyud): ni 'chad shing sna tshogs pa //tsug tor mu ti ga thor tshugs can //de'i 'dun du rin chen bri //g.yon du bum pa bzang po gzhag //g.yas pa ru ni dpag bsam shing //rgyab du rin chen lan bu'o
17b2(rgyud): 'tshams rnams su ni bum pa brgyad //gser gyi rang bzhin gzhag par bya //rin chen lnga yis yang dag 'khrugs //dri lnga dang ni yang dag ldan //bza' lasogs pa'i ros gang ba'i //
17b3(rgyud): bum pa bcu drug phyi rol du //ser po'i gos kyis yongs su dkyis//me tog ser po sogs kyis brgyan //de 'dun blo ldan bri bya ba //sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin dge ma //dmar dang ser dang ches
17b4(rgyud): dkar ba rin chen ldan pa'i phyag sbyin pa //ser po'i 'od ldan phreng mdzes ma //rin chen bzang po kun gyis sras;namakha'i zla ba skar ma yis//kun nas yongs su bskor ba'o //
17b5(rgyud): gtsug phud gcod pan gos lasogs//phreng ba dang ldan dges pa mo //de'i lho ru bri bya ba //tshul khrims pha rol phyin dge ma /cung zad ser ba'i rin chen lus //sna
17b6(rgyud): tshogs rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan //sna tshogs gos ni yongs su gyon //mdzes pa'i phreng ba'i chun po'i gtsug mgo ldan gtsug na gtsug tor mchog //yid bzhin rin chen dam pa'i phyag //
17b7(rgyud): g.yon pa'i dkyil dkrungs ri ni rin chen bzang //cho ga bzhin du rab du bsam //rgyab du bzod pa ma zhes bya //cung zad 'dzum zhing ser 'dzes ma //dmar po'i gos ni legs par gyon //rin
17b8(rgyud): po che'i rgyan gyis brgyan //phyag na rin chen mchog 'bri shing //dmar po'i gos ni 'chang ba mo //gtsug na rin chen mchog dang ldan //seng ge mchog gi khri la bzhugs //byad du brtson 'grus
18a1(rgyud): @#//chen mo ni //rdo rje gos chas rgyan pa la //dpa' bo rin chen dbyug gu'i phyag //g.yas pa sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin gzhan du sdug bsngal thob 'gyur te //rgyal po chom rkun sogs
18a2(rgyud): pas //gal te bla ma de bzhugs na //skal pa bzang po gnyas 'bul lo//gzhan pa yi ni spun rnams la rdo rje theg rtogs 'gyur ba'i //skal pa re re sbyin par bya//gzhan du dam tshig
18a3(rgyud): gdung bar 'gyur //'di ni bcom ldan 'das rnal 'byor can //gsang ba rgyan gyi rdo rjes gsungs //de nas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi //yum rnams kyis ni der btud te //dpa'o
18a4(rgyud): che zhu ba zhus rgyas pa yi ni dkyil 'khor ni//gsungs cig gsungs cig dpa' bo che //yum rnams kun gyi dkyil 'khor ni //rgyal po'i dbang phyug rab gsol
18a5(rgyud): ba //sems can thams cad 'dir 'byung ba'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po'i dbang phyug dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge

18a8(rgyud): po//gser gyi ri bo dang 'tshungs pa //phyag gnyis kyis ni chos 'chad cing //g.yas pa yis ni mchog sbyin pa //g.yon gyis rin chen lan bu mchog//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par rgyan
18b1(rgyud): gos ni 'chang shing sna tshogs pa //gtsug tor mu tig thog tshugs can //de'i 'dun du rin chen bri //g.yon du bum pa bzang po gzhag //g.yas pa ru ni dpag bsam shing //
18b2(rgyud): rgyab du rin chen lan bu'o//'tshams su ni bum pa brgyad //gser gyi rang bzhin gzhag par bya//rin chen lnga yis yang dag 'khrungs //dri lnga dang ni yang dag ldan //bza' 
18b3(rgyud): la sogs pa'i ros gang ba'i //bum pa bcu drug phyi rol du //ser po'i gos kyis yongs su bkris//me tog ser po sogs kyis brgyan //de mdun blo ldan bri bya ba //sbyin pa'i 
18b4(rgyud): pha rol phyin dge ma //dmar dang ser dang ches dkar ba //rin chen ldan pa'i phyag sbyin pa //ser po'i 'od ldan phreng mdzes ma //rin chen bzang po kun gyis sras //mkha'i
18b5(rgyud): zla ba skar ma yis //kun nas yongs su bskor ba'o//gtsug phud gcod pan gos la sogs//phreng ba dang ldan dges pa mo//de'i lho ru dri bya ba //tshul khrims
18b6(rgyud): pha rol phyin dge ma /cung zad ser ba'i rin chen lus //sna tshogs rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan //sna tshogs gos ni yongs su gyon//mdzes pa'i 'phreng ba'i chun po'i gtsug //
18b7(rgyud): mgo ldan gtsug na gtsug tor mchog//yid bzhin rin chen dam pa'i phyag //g.yon pa'i dkyil dkrungs rin chen bzang //cho ga bzhin du rab tu bsam //rab tu bzod pa ma zhes
18b8(rgyud): bya //cung zad 'dzum zhing ser mdzes ma //dmar po'i gos ni rnam par gyon//rin po che'i rgyan gyis brgyan //phyag na rin chen mchog 'bri zhing //dmar po'i gos ni 'chang ba mo /
19a1(rgyud): @#//gtsug na rin chen mchog dang ldan //seng ge mchog gi khri la bzhugs //byang du brtson 'grus chen mo ni //rdo rje go chas brgyan pa la //dpa'o rin chen dbyug gu'i phyag /
19a2(rgyud): g.yas pa'i sbyin pa'i pha rol song //pri yang ku ltar sngo sangs dkar //rdo rje seng ge'i khri la bzhugs //lhag ma rnams ni gzhag bya ba //dkyil 'khor byas te rin chen ldan //
19a3(rgyud): sgo dang sgo ru sgo ba ma //rin chen kun gyis rnam par brgyan //de nas phyi'i phyi nyid du //rin chen snying po'i lag pa mchog //phyogs skyong ba ni thams cad ni //go rims bzhin du
19a4(rgyud): gzhag par bya//de'i phyi rol thams cad du //mkhas pas klu dang gnod skyin bri //rin chen bum pa bzang po dang //gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan dag //gtor ma 
19a5(rgyud): rnam pa sna tshogs dang //lha gshos rab tu bzang dge ba //bza' dang btung ba'i ro mchog dang //mar dang ka ra nyid dang ni //nyid zho dang bu ram hags
19a6(rgyud): dang bcas dang //'o ma dang ni srang rtsi dang //rgyal po'i bza' ba dam pa'i mchog//bza' ba la sogs rab mchog go//de nas sngags pas 'jug par bya//rgyan
19a7(rgyud): rnams kun gyis brgyan nas su //ser po gos 'dzin dag pa dang //phreng ba dri zhim ldan pa'i las //sgo'i char ni bzhugs nas su//thog mar lha'i spyang drang bya //phyag rgya bcings
19a8(rgyud): te dbyangs snyan pas //rab tu dga' ba'i yid kyis su//lhar bcas 'dzin la sman slad du //kun du bzang po'i smon lam dran 'dzod la //rin chen dam pa seng ge'i khrir zhugs
19b1(rgyud): te//'khor dang lha de bzhin gshegs pa sbyon//de nas de dag legs byon te //sras bcas 'khor dang bcas rnams kyang //sems can rnams la dngos grub ster//bsgrub pa po la khyad
19b2(rgyud): par du //de nas thal mo legs sbyar la //rin chen gyis bkang me tog bcas //gsang sngags brjod de gtor bar bya //ci bde bar ni bzhes su gsol//de nas rang gis dbang bskur bya/
19b3(rgyud): de'i sngags dang phyag rgya'i //sa la lag pas reg nas su//snyems pa yis ni ma thob par bya//de nas slob ma bzhug par bya//me tog dris ni yang dag sdud //ser po'i gos ni yang dag gyon
19b4(rgyud): lag na rin chen dri bzang pos//dga' dang bcas te snying ba de //snga ma'i sngags kyis dor bar bya //gang gi drung du ltung gyur pa //lha de de yis 'grub pa yin //de nas 
19b5(rgyud): g.yas nas rnam bskor te //slob ma de ni 'byung bar bya //bzang po'i seng ge'i khrir gzhag ste //dbang bskur ba'i sbyor ba bya //gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan dang //mdzes
19b6(rgyud): ba'i rnga yab g.yob pa yis //de bzhin du ni dbang bskur te //bum pa brgyad dang bcu drug la //de'i sngags dang phyag rgya yis//go rims ji lta ba bzhin no //de nas sbyor bas de 'ongs
19b7(rgyud): te //'khor lo sgyur ba'i rim pa yis 'khor lo rin chen bzang po'i lag //rgyal ba rgyal ba'i sgra dge bas //gsang ba'i sbyor ba'i rnal 'byor gyis//lta ba ru ni dbang bskur ro //'dir bdag rtog can rin chen
19b8(rgyud): 'dzin //rdo rje rin chen 'dzin par 'gyur //dam tshig gi dam pa yis//sangs rgyas kun gyis don rgyas pa //'bad pas de ring blangs pa yis //'phrongs pa'i dri ma 'phrog par byed //
20a1(rgyud): //de nas dpa' bo 'chi med 'dzin //rin chen tshogs kyi mchod pa byas //gzhan pa yi ni yang du ma //rgyal po dbang phyug dam pas so //de bzhin dbang skur lnga po yis //skal med
20a2(rgyud): kyang ni mi 'gyur bdag //gling bzhi dbang phyug dpal dang ldan //gnod pa med par thob par 'gyur //gsang ba las kyang ches shin du //gsang ba yi ni mnga' bsgrags te //de'i sgom dang bsgrub
20a3(rgyud): ba'i thabs //slob ma la ni de nas slab //gur gum dang ni tsan dan dang //dri dang mar me bzang po la //dam tshig gis ni gsal byas la //lan gsum du ni mthar du gzhung //bu kye
20a4(rgyud): mthung shig gsang ba che //dam tshig shin du 'da' ba //dka' //gal te 'di las 'das 'gyur na //myur ba nyid du 'chi bar 'gyur //shi nas kyang ni dmyal sdug bsngal//
20a5(rgyud): rab mang du 'bod nyid du gnas //gal te mi ru skye 'dod pa 'am //nam zhig dngos grub thob 'dod na //de bzhin 'phongs pa'i sdug bsngal las //ring du nges par khyod kyis
20a6(rgyud): skyobs //'di dag legs thob slob ma la //gsang ba'i sgrub thabs bshad par bya //ji lta'i rgyal rigs bu mo ni//bzung nas gsal ba'i rnal 'byor bya //nyi ma bdun na nges par 'grub//
20a7(rgyud): rgyal po'i phun mtshogs thob par 'gyur //shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i mchog //dkyil 'khor sbyor bas bsam gtan bya //rgyal po cho ga bzhin bzung ste //sgrub pa pos ni myur du bsgrub //
20a8(rgyud): nyi ma gsum gyis rgyal po mchog //gdon mi za bar thob par 'gyur //bram ze'i bu mo skal chen mo//nor chen la ni bsgrub par bya //rje rigs sna tshogs sbyar ba yis //mtshungs pa nyid du
20b1(rgyud): bsgrab par bya//de yis ni dngos grub mchog rnyed de //rgyal po 'jig rten gsum gyis rjed //rmangs po dpa' bo'i sbyor ba yis //bsgrubs na sngags kyi dngos grub 'gyur //gnod sbyin
20b2(rgyud): rnams kyi dbang phyug mchog //dman pa'i sems su gshad pa dang //mchog gis mchog ni chen po dang //bar mas bar ma'i dngos grub ni //skye ba 'di la thob par 'gyur //de nas las
20b3(rgyud): rnams bslab par bya//zhi ba dang ni rgyas pa'i las //de bzhin dbang dang mngon spyod dang //gzhan pa yi ni sna tshogs ni //sems can rnams la dga' ba'o //shes rab pha rol phyin dge ma //
20b4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor sbyor bas bsgom pa dang //kun 'da' dkar po zla ba 'dra//'od zer dkar pos mdzes pa po //skye bo nad pa 'chi ka mo//de'i lus la gzhug par bya //de nas de'i lus

20b6(rgyud): kun gyi don rig cing //shes rab dran pa mchog dang ldan //de bzhin rang bzhin rnal 'byor pa //namakha' la yang yang dag bsgom //mdzes pa rin chen sna tshogs pa'i //
20b7(rgyud): thams cad du ni tshar 'bab pa //'di yis 'phongs pa rnam par 'phrog//'phongs pa rnams la rnam par rab tu rtag //mthe bo che'i tshad tsam pa'i //nor rgyun ma'i rnam pa bya //lha mo la ni 
20b8(rgyud): mnyam gzhag pas //de'i mdun du gsang sngags bzla //ji lta srid 'bum phrag kho na ste //de nas dngos grub bsgrub par bya//zla ba gang ba 'am bcu bzhi 'am //rgyad pa'i bzas bzung 
21a1(rgyud): //nyin par la //mchod pa chen po byas nas su //mkhas pas mtshan mo thog thag bzlas //de nas dga' bas gzugs rnyan de //gdon mi za bar 'bar bar 'gyur //de nas
21a2(rgyud): bzung ste mdor sdu na //mi'i 'khor lo sgyur bar 'gyur //bzlas pas mtshan mo thog thag/bzlas //de nas dga' ba gzugs rnyan de //gdon (la )mi za bar 'bar bar 'gyuro //de nas ma bzang
21a3(rgyud): byung na //rnal 'byor pa ni nor cher 'gyur //bar mas gling ni thob par 'gyur //tha mas (gang )ni (la )thob par 'gyur //thams cad du ni zlas tsam gyis//longs spyod zad par 'gyur ma yin;rgyal
21a4(rgyud): po thams cad dbang 'gyur zhing //bud med thams cad dga' bar 'gyur //snying khar rnal 'byor byas nas su//rnor bu lag par bcangs nas nas ni //nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor la gnas 
21a5(rgyud): sgom //myur du rin chen rgyal por 'gyur //phyag rgya la 'di ni byas gyur pas //brtag tu bsgom pas bsgom par bya //ji ltar 'dod pa'i grub red de //rdo rje 'dzin pa'i bka'
21a6(rgyud): bzhin no//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas bka' stsal to//de nas sbyor ba ji bzhin du //yum de rnams ni legs byon nas //las kyi dkyil 'khor legs gnas pa //
21a7(rgyud): ston pa(sa) ni zhu ba zhus //gang du rgyal ba kun bzhugs nas //bla na med pa'i don mdzad pa //sems can kun la bde mdzad pa //myur du las ni 'grub pa legs//de nas bcom ldan 'das las
21a8(rgyud): thams cad kyi dam pa' dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yum dam pa de rnams la las kyi dkyil 'khor bka' stsal pa
21b1(rgyud): rlung gi dkyil 'khor bri bar bya //de'i dbus su gru gsum pa //de'i steng du lha mo gzhag //'bar ba'i me dang 'tshungs pa la //zhal bzhi ma la cher 'jigs ma //'che ba rab tu rtsigs
21b2(rgyud): pa mo //thod pa'i 'phreng ba'i gcod pan can //rdo rje lcags kyu thod pa dang //zhags pa g.yas dang g.yon par 'dzin //pags pa chen po gos su gyon //khro mo mig 'bras rab phyung ba //
21b3(rgyud): mchi bas mchi mnan zhal chen mo //de'i mdun du bri bya ba //mtha' zhil ma mdor sdus pa //sna tshogs rtum dang drag pa'i zhal //du ma'i phyag gis yang dag 'khrugs //zhabs pa lcags
21b4(rgyud): kyu thod sogs dang //rdo rje can gyi sog le 'dzin;;thod pas lus la bres gyur cing //gcod pan chen pos 'jigs byed mo //gshin rje dpa' bos bri bya ba //mkha'
21b5(rgyud): gdod dam pa yi ni mchog//sngon por 'bar ba'i rang bzhin khros//mgo'i 'phreng bas rnam par brgyan //thod pa'i 'phreng ba'igacod pan can //stag gi pags pa'i 
21b6(rgyud): sham thabs can //ral gri chen po thod pa dang //rin chen dbyug gu 'dzin pa'i mchog//de nas lha mo legs bri ba //pad ma'i zhal ma 'jigs chen mo //dmar mo phyag ni nyi zla 'dzin //
21b7(rgyud): skal pa'i me dang 'tshungs pa mo //g.yon pa ru ni khro mo che //ma bcom ma ni ska rags 'gul //las kyi rdo rje dril ldan mchog//'chi ba rnam par rtsigs pa'i zhal //gtsug na thod pa 
21b8(rgyud): 'jigs su rung //rab rtum stobs dang gsal ba mo //skyor ldan cho ga rigs pas bri //phyi rol du ni legs bris pa //khro bcas 'jigs pa dang bcas pa //thod pa'i thor tshugs chen
22a1(rgyud): @#//po can;khrag gis gang ba'i thod pa dang //phyag rgya bzang pos phyag mtshan pa'o//de'i phyir ru lha rnams ni //snying khar rab sbyar yid dang ba //thams cad du ni yongasu gzhag //

22a3(rgyud): thod pa'i spyod pan chen po dang //khrag chang gis bkang ba'i //thod pa ldan pas 'jug par bya//thams cad 'grub pa'i ma mo rnams //'di yis spyan ni grang bar bya //dkon mchog 
22a4(rgyud): gsum la gnod byed pa //gsad pa'i don du ma mo rnams //de ring nyid du dkyil 'khor 'dir //'jigs chen ma rnams thams cad gshegs//'jigs pa'i sgra cher

22a7(rgyud): thams cad bsgrub par bya//de nas mngon spyod las rnams kun //dam tshig bsrung ba yis ni bya //dkon mchog gsum la gnod byed dang //bla ma rnams la 'khyil sla ba //rtag tu
22a8(rgyud): bsod par lha mo rnams //bgyid par bdag ni rjes su bzung //de nas cho ga de nyid kyis//slob ma'i dam pa gzhug par bya //khro bo'i lha la bskul nas su //me tog dmar po dor bya bar//
22b1(rgyud): bdag gi me tog dam pa 'di//ci bde bar ni gzhes su gsol //gdug pa 'di ni bzung nas su //gsod byed 'di ni gsol bar mdzod //lha mo gang la ltung gyur pa //de yis de la mchog
22b2(rgyud): gsol lo//de nas dbang skur byin nas su//dkyil 'khor gyi ni 'dun gzhag ste //thod pa ru ni gnas pa'i //khrag gi dbang ni bskur bar bya//de nas sgo ru bos nas su //dam 
22b3(rgyud): tshig 'bad pas 'dzin du bzhug//rab tu bdug pa'i seng ge'i khrag /bzang po 'di ni mthung zhig bu //zo shig zo shig sha chen po//btung ba 'jigs pa chen po 'thung //gzhan du ni 'chi
22b4(rgyud): 'gyur te //mkha' 'gro ma dang gza' rnams dang //gnod sbyin srin po rtum po yis//ring po mi thogs rab tu za//de nas mngon spyod las rnams ni //stong khrag dag ni bslab par
22b5(rgyud): bya //de'i sbyor ba'i rnal 'byor gyis//rang nyid khro bor bzung gyur pas //gnod byed thams cad gsad par bya //dgra rnams thams cad gza' bar bya //gdug pa
22b6(rgyud): thams cad bca' bar bya //srin po thams cad gzhom par bya //mtha' yas zhal bzhi'i sbyor ba yis//gsad dang gzung ba de yis bya //zhal bzhi ma'i sbyor ba yis//thams 
22b7(rgyud): cad 'gugs pa'i dam pa yis//mkha' gdod ma yis thams cad du //rin po che'i tshogs 'byung ngo//pad ma'i zhal gyi sbyor ba yis//chang gi dam pa bzung bar bya//ma bcom ma'i sbyor ba
22b8(rgyud): yis //sha ni 'gugs pa'i mchog yin no//nam mkha' sa rengs pa chen po ste //dpung 'byor ba'i dam pa'o//rmugs dang rmongs pa thams cad du //spyad pa ru ni mi bya'o//sngar gshad
23a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i las rnams dang //ma bshad pa'i las rnams dang //ma gshad pa 'ang ci 'dod pa //bzlas pa tsam gyis kun bya ste //thams cad du ni the tsom med//dur khrod kyi ni 
23a2(rgyud): thal ba la //dgra bo'i gzugs ni 'bad pas bya//ming dang spel bar byas nas su //ma mo'i ral gris rtub par bya //de nas nyi ma bdun na ni //brlag par 'gyur bar gdon mi za
23a3(rgyud): gang lus ji ltar gcad gyur pa //de dang de'i yan lag 'chad //zhal bzhi ma'i sbyor ba yis //sngags pa mnyam par gzhag pas bcad //de'i 'grin par zhags pa ni //mkhas pas spros te

23a7(rgyud): med nyid du 'gyur //de nas de bzhin dbang skur gyis dkyil 'khor la ni bzhag yum rnams kyis//dbang skur gsang ba'i dam tshig sbyin //gsang ba'i sgra thabs nyan du bzhugs //rang 
23a8(rgyud): yum mo'i sa bon gyis//rang gi bdag rnam rtog bdag yum min //lta bus yab du gyur pas bsgrubs nas ni//nyi ma bdun na rab tu 'grub //sangs rgyas kun gyis yum ston pa //kun
23b1(rgyud): mkhyen ye shes spyod yul te //grog po kun dang bde chen po //ye shes kun gyi mchog gi mchog//shes rab kyi ni thams cad bsgrub //las rgyas pa'i dam pa'i mchog//bye brag du
23b2(rgyud): ni skul nas su //byang chub kyi ni yon tan gyi //thams cad du ni 'od gsal ba'i//dngos grub thams cad bsgrub par bya//shes rab kyis ni las dbye bas //gzugs dang dbyibs
23b3(rgyud): nyid dag gis so//phyag rgya dang ni phyag rgya'i lan //de bzhin dkyil 'khor dam tshig dang //bza' ba ni dang nyer spyod pa//gtor ma lha gshos nyid dang ni //shin du bde ba'i stan nyid

23b7(rgyud): ye shes chen pos srung du gsol//gang gis mngon par brgyas btab nas//mnga' bdag dus mi ring bar 'gyur //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang ting nge 'dzin rdo rje zhes bya ba'i

26a1(rgyud): //gzhan dang skye ba'i sbyor ba dang //bud med sgyu ma'i rnam 'phrul dang //gling bu pi wang rdza rnga sogs//ting ting gshegs dang dung sgra dang //ro dang reg pa dri chen po //gzugs

26b1(rgyud): thams cad gsang ba brgyan gyi khor lo ni //dpa'o chen pos gsung du gsol //gang gis bkod pa dkyil 'khor chen po'i mchog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas 'di skad
