26b4(rgyud): rnam par spangs //brjod du med pa bshad par bya //de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi bdag nyid de rnams /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba brgyan gyi bkod
26b5(rgyud): ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po bka' stsal ba'i phyir//slar yang klu'i dbyangs 'dis zhu ba zhus pa //chos dbyings mi g.yo bde la legs pa'i bzhugs dam pa'i thugs //
26b6(rgyud): phyogs bcu'i mtha' yas sangs rgyas zhabs legs btud pa kye //bde mchog lha'i lha stes dbang 'jig rten bla //dkyil 'khor bde ba mchog la bzhugs gsol bde chen gyi //sa
26b7(rgyud): gnyis gtsug gis phyag byas bde ldan pad ma'i gtsug tor kye //rnam pa kun du gar mdzad sbyor ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa gsungs cig kye //'gro kun phan dgongs rab tu bzhugs gsol
26b8(rgyud): mchod pa chen po kye //cha lugs rnam pa du mas 'gro kun 'dul mdzad kun gyis dam pa kye//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis gsol ba btab cing bskul
27a1(rgyud): /ba'i tshig nyid kyi thugs la gzhag nas /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo sgyur chen po rab tu bsim pa /bde ba longs spyod pa rnam par skyings pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting
27a2(rgyud): nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas //de bzhan gshegs pa thams cad gyi byin gyis brlabs te dbang bskur nas /bde ba chen po 'khor lo sgyur ba la rab tu gnas /sku dang gsung dang thugs
27a3(rgyud): rdo rje rnams las rab tu byung ngo//dkyil 'khor snga ma bzhin bris la //de'i lte bar 'di dgod do//rdo rje chen po gsal ldan pa //'dod pa'i dbang phyug ngag dang ldan //de bzhin gshegs
27a4(rgyud): pa kun gyi yab //kun gyi thog mtha' rang 'byung ba //de la sogs pa'i rim pa yis//thams cad kun du brtags pa bya//pad ma'i 'khor lo'i rtsibs la ni //rang gi chos dang rab
27a5(rgyud): tu spel //ma mo rnams ni thams cad kyi //mchod pa la sogs rab tu rtag //sgo ru sgo ba yongs su rtag //sgo'i phyi ru sgo skyong ba //btsun mo lhan cig spyod bcas pa'i //kun
27a6(rgyud): nas lha rnams gzhag par bya //de nas rang gi sa bon gyis //klu dang gnod sbyin 'phar ma gzhag //dri za rnams dang mi 'am ci //lha min mkhas lding de bzhin no//'jig rten skyong ba thams
27a7(rgyud): cad ni //sgo ru gnyis gnyis rtag par bya//gdon dang dug dang rims dang nad //yi dgas sha za sprin po dang //dmyal ba brgyad po phyogs brgyad kyi //'khor lo'i phyi ru gzhag par 
27a8(rgyud): bya //de nas 'khor lo'i mu khyud bri //chen po rin chen bdun rang bzhin //de'i sgo ru bri bar bya //shar du 'dod chags skye gnas lag //zhe sdang drag po lus gnag pa //byang du
27b1(rgyud): legs par bri bar bya //lho ru nga rgyal nyid yin no//sog le dang ni gos nag po//de bzhan dbyug gu 'jigs chen po //'di rnams phyag brgyar brtags pa'o//de nas ji lta bzhin du 'jug//
27b2(rgyud): de nas gsang ba rdo rje'i phyag rgya nges par bcings pa'i lag par gyur pas //rang gi dam tshig gi rjes su dran pas phyag rgya de nyid kyis 'gugs pa'i rnam par dang //rdo rje'i klu 'dis bskul
27b3(rgyud): te /dkyil 'khor thams cad spyan drang bar bya ste //'dir gshegs 'dir gshegs sangs rgyas zhal ras sangs rgyas dpa' //bde bar gshegs pa yum dang bcas pa mtha' dag gis
27b4(rgyud): bskor te /'dir gshegs 'dir gshegasapad ma'i spyan ras pad las 'khrungs //dam tshig rnam kyis dwa ba'o dam pa'i phyag //klu dbyangs rgyal po 'di yis ni //rdo rje'i
27b5(rgyud): glo gros nges par bskul //byon gyur song shig song shig ces//'khod cig 'khod cig mkhod par bya //tshim zhing tshim la rab tu tshim //zhugs cig zhugs cas 'jug par bya//bag kya
27b6(rgyud): zhes brjod bskul ba yis//sa la gnas kun rab tu 'bebs //de nas me tog dor bar bya //bcom ldan 'das bde bar gzhes su gsol//lha dbang gang la lhung 'gyur bzung //de'i bsgrub thabs
27b7(rgyud): rtsam par bya//yang na yid la gang 'dod pa //bsgrub byed bsgrub po dam pas sgrub //de nyid la ni dbang skur ba //rnal 'byor pa ni mkhas pas blang //de nas slob ma bzhug par bya//brgyan rnaMs
27b8(rgyud): kun gyis rnam par brgyan //dar sogs pas gdong g.yog te /phyag rgya bcings pa'i lag bzang pos//de bzhin du ni me tog dor//sa ma ya sngo bdag gis brjod //me tog gang la lhung gyur pad de'i
27'a1(rgyud): @#//bcu mu med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad khyab pa'i rgya mtsho'i sprin rnams bkang ste//namakha'i khams na gnas pa thams cad til gi gang bu ltar 'dzam bu'i gling na 'khod par
27'a2(rgyud): gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje thogs rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug gis bkug cing bcug te/bcing nas dbang du mdzad de࿒ ;de nyid thugs kar rab tu bzhag par gyur to//de nas bcom 
27'a3(rgyud): ldan
27'a4(rgyud): 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyugachen pos gnyis su med pa'i sbyor bas byin gyis rlabs te/sngags 'dis rang gi sems so sor rtogs par mdzad do//oM tsi d+ha pra ti be d+haM ka ro mid//de'i rjes 
27'a5(rgyud): thogs nyid la /sangs rgyas thams cad kyis bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs kar long spyod rdzogs pa dang /sprul pa'i sku las chos kyi sku rang bzhin gyis 'od
27'a6(rgyud): gsal ba sems rin po che nges par byung ste/spros par mdzad do//de nyid sems 'od gsal ba thugs rje chen pos khyab pa /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyam pa nyid
27'a7(rgyud): kyi mtshan nyid bzung ba dang 'dzin pa'i rnyog pa spangs pa /nam mkha' ltar gdod nas zhi bar de las thugs rje chen po las mngon par phyogs par gzhag nas /sems can gyi khams thams cad
27'a8(rgyud): la rnam par gzigs te/mi shes pas rga ba po dang /'chi bas rnam par khyab pas //gzigs nas slar yang sngags 'dis byang chub kyi sems nye bar skyed pa /oM b+ho d+ha tsi ta ud pa ta ya mi
27'a9(rgyud): byang chub kyi sems skyed pa'i rjes thog nyid la /de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad kyis de'i thugs kar sems 'od gsal ba de/yongs su rdzogs pa'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor ltar byin gyis
27'b1(rgyud): rlabs te//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs khar rnam par gzhag go//legs par dag pa'i nam mkha' ltar dri ma med pa/spos pa thams cad spangs pa me long lta bur rtan par byas nas
27'b2(rgyud): gnas te/byang chub kyi sems chen po la sngags 'di ched du brjod pa//oM dz+nya na ra smi bi sri dza hU~M/de'i rjes thogs nyid la 'od zer rnam pa sna tshogs pa/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor las rnam par phyung ngo//
27'b3(rgyud): de phyung nas kyang chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang du zhugs te/shes bya'i dkyil 'khor thams cad gsel bar byas nas/sems can thams cad kyi ri bo'i rtse mo snyil te/rab tu gtogs par byas nas gcig tu
27'b4(rgyud): 'dres te/rdo rje'i sku chen po rnams bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs kar rjes su zhags nas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su rab tu gnas so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor
27'b5(rgyud): gyi dbang phyug chen pos//sngags 'dis byin gyis rlabs so//oM ta/'+wa badzra/slar yang sngags 'dis spros pa/oM spa ra badzra/slar yang sngags 'di sa sdus so//oM saM ha ra badzra/
27'b6(rgyud): de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes rdo rje'i snying po'i 'di ched du brjod pa/oM dz+nya na badzra/de nas bcom ldan 'das rdorje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen pos sngags 'dis byin gyis rlab pa
27'b7(rgyud): oM sarba ta tha ga ta dz+nya na badzra hU~M//de nas de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi sku dang/gsung dang thugs mnyam pa nyid rnyed pas sngags 'dis byin gyis brlabs//oM badzra ad mo ka ha~M//de bzhin gshegs
27'b8(rgyud): pa thams cad rtan pa nyid du nye bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as rnam par snang mdzad kyis de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs gzigs nas sngags 'dis byin gyis blabs
28a1(rgyud): //spyi 'or dbang bskur bya //yang na gang 'dod rnal 'byor gyi //sbyor ba bsgoms te dbang bskur bya //yang nabadag po'i rnal 'byor gyis//gal te de ni brjod ldan 'gyur //gdugs dang rgyal
28a2(rgyud): mtshan bla res sogs //b+h+ri g+hA ra'i bum pa dang //rnga yab rnam pa sna tshogs dang //seng ge'i khri bzang po dang //gser dang dngul gyi dbyug gu dang //rin chen dbyug gu du ma ni //dgyes pa'i dkyil
28a3(rgyud): 'khor nyid du gzhag //dbang bskur spros pa chen po yis//de ni bstan la gzhag nas su//rnal 'byor ldan pas dbang bskur ro//dbang bskur ji ltar rigs pa yis //gsang ba'i sbyor bas dbang
28a4(rgyud): bskur ro//thog mar sdom pa ldan pa yis//de ni dam tshig 'dzin du 'jug //'dir ni nga'i dam tshig bzung //phyi gsang ba ni rnam pa bzhi //sangs rgyas kun gyi sdom pa ni//sangs rgyas
28a5(rgyud): ye shes rab gsal byed //gdug pa thams cad gsad par bya//'dzungs pa na tsam gyi nor 'phrog bya //lta ba ngan pa brdzun du smra //srog chags gsod la smra bar bya //gzhan du
28a6(rgyud): bud med mngon 'phrod par //ci bde bar ni khrogs shig bu //gzhan rdzas 'phrog par byed rnams dang //dkon mchog gsum la 'gal byed pa//gsad dang gzhom par bya ba dang //de bzhin khrag
28a7(rgyud): rnams btung bar ru //'bad pa yis ni thams cad byos//dam tshig rnam pa gsum po yis//de nas yon ni phul nas su //de nas yon ni phul nasasu //bla ma la ni gus pa yis//zhabs la
28a8(rgyud): mgo bso nges btud te //rang lus rgyal po'i longs spyod dang //chung ma 'am yang na bu mo dang //bu rnams dang ni bran nyid dang //sring mo sring mo'i bu mo dang //ma dang rang gi gan ma dang //grong dang yul
28b1(rgyud): gyi longs spyod shing rta grong dang glang po dang //rnam par bkra ba'i rta rnams dang //nor gyis gang ba'i grong rnams dang //ba glang ma he ra rnams dang //lug dgod rnams dang gzhan rnams dang //
28b2(rgyud): rnga mo mdzes pa'i rnga mo ma//gzhan dang gzhan yang gang 'dod pa //ji ltar rigs pas mchod par bya //rims bzhin rang gi slob ma'i tshogs//mgon po mchod nas gshegs su gsol//gang
28b3(rgyud): gzhan rdo rje spun la yang //de nas bsgrub thabs dam pa bslab //srog dang thur sel sbyor ba yis//sems kyis brtan pa nyid du bya//zla ba nya ba lta bur dgang //cha shas dag ni bcu drug kyang //de'i
28b4(rgyud): dbus su rnam bsam pa //gtso chen 'dod pa'i dbang phyug go//mdor sdus nas ni spro bar bya //rtul shugs can gyis rang lus bsam //bdag nyid 'dod pa'i dbang phyug rgyal //
28b5(rgyud): 'dod pa'i zil gyis mnan nas su //rnal 'byor can bdag dpal ldan 'grub //lha dang bcas pas phyag 'tshal zhing //nad dang mya ngan 'jigs pa sel //rga dang 'chi ba rnams par
28b6(rgyud): spangs //zla ba tsam bzlas pa yis ni //dpa' bos las kun byed par 'gyur //zhi dang rgyas dang de bzhin dbang //drag shul spyod pa'i rim pa yis//dgug dang dbab pa'i las rnams ni //
28b7(rgyud): skad cig tsam zhig bskal ba yis//sngags pas thams cad byed par 'gyur //sngags kyi sbyor bas bsngags bzlos pa'i//'di ni bcom ldan rnal 'byor gsung //yan lag lnga las skyes pa'i
28b8(rgyud): bzung //'byung po'i nyi ma'i mtshan mo gung //lcags gsum gyis ni bkris byas la //rang gi kha ru gzhug par bya//bdag nyid rnal 'byor ldan pas bzlas //de ni nang par mthong mi 'gyur //ltar
29a1(rgyud): @#//bcas grub pa'i sems can gyis//thams cad rnam pa kun du'o//bskal par gnas shing shes rab che //stan bcos thams cad rab tu mkhas //gzugs dang lang mtsho phun
29a2(rgyud): sum tshogs //rig pa 'dzin pa'i mnga' bdag 'gyur //bcu bzhi'i nyin bar shi ba'i //rdol pa'i g.yon gyi rtsib logs ni //sbyor bas bzung ste 'phur 'gro'o //gzugs rnyan lag
29a3(rgyud): pa bzhi pa bya //zhags pa lcags kyu rtse gsum mtshan //g.yon par thod pa 'dzin pa'o//rlung gi dkyil 'khor la gnas pa //spyan gsum rnam par rtsigs pa'o//thod pa'i 'phreng ba'i
29a4(rgyud): spyod pan can//rtum ldan lta bu zur chen po pa //'jig+sa pa chen po'i dur khrod du //rnal 'byor ldan pas de zhig ste //cho ga ji bzhin mchod par bya //gsang sngags rgyal po zlas 
29a5(rgyud): par bya //shi ba yi ni mi dag gi //khrag dang sha dang mar khu yis//de nas lha mo de 'grub po //mtshan ma yang ni lta bar bya //mtshan ma yis ni dngos grub che //dus mi ring 
29a6(rgyud): bar bzang po 'thob //dman pas dman pa'i dngos grub ste //bar ma yis ni bar ma'o//mtshan ma med na chung ngu ni //rab tu 'grub par spyad mi dgos//lha min bzang po dgug par bya //mi dang
29a7(rgyud): mnga' ni skul ba yis//de'i skad cig nyid la byed //khrag nyid kyis ni khris pa yi//dbang du bya ba 'ang 'di nyid kyis //de bzhin bud med thams cad dgug //grub pa'i rdzas kyang ming nyid kyis //
29a8(rgyud): dgug pa'i sbyor bas dgug par bya //dmag dpung rnam par bzlog pa yin //rgyal mtshan rtse mor rnam gzhag go//rengs dang rmongs par byed pa dang //rmongs dang 'tshang ba de bzhin du //gyid dang 
29b1(rgyud): gsad pa thams cad ni//cho ga dag dang ldan pas byed //de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yum rnams rnams dang //yab rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsang ba brgyan gyi dkyil
29b2(rgyud): 'khor gyi rgyal po chen po 'di zhu ba zhus pa //gang la bzhugs nas rgyal ba kun //yum dang yab rnams de bzhin du //de'i sbyor bas sbyor bdag nyid //sems can rnams ni snod du mdzod //
29b3(rgyud): gsang ba brgyan gyi dkyil 'khor gyi //rnal 'byor chen po rnam dbye ba //gang gis ma 'ongs mi rnams ni //bde bas sangs rgyas nyid la 'jog //de nas bcom ldan rdo rje can //'dzum dang 
29b4(rgyud): bcas pa'i spyan rgyas pas //gsang ba chen po bka' stsal pa //gang phyir bdag nyid dkyil 'khor bshad //sems can dag pa'i rdo rje can //bdag nyid gsang ba'i rgyan zhes bya //snying 
29b5(rgyud): po phyag rgya sngags nyid de //bdag nyid rigs sngags dam tshig kyang //gang phyir bdag nyid sdom pa ste //bdag nyid gshad dang 'chad po po //pha dang ma rnams de bzhin no //
29b6(rgyud): dngos grub bsgrub po las kyi mchog //bde dang sdug bsngal khams gsam pa //zhes so //de nas bcom ldan 'das la de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yum dang yab rnams 
29b7(rgyud): kyis 'di skad ces gsol to//de'i phyir gsungs cig bcom ldan 'das bde bar gshegs pas gsung du gsol //sangs rgyas byang chub sems kun gyi //phan dang bde ba thob pa'i phyir//ces so /
29b8(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad kyi yum dang yab dang nye bar longs spyod pa rdo rje dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas //nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs
30a1(rgyud): @#//dang rdo rje las 'di byung ngo //sngon gshad cho ga'i rim pa yis //dkyil 'khor yang dag bris nas su //de'i dbus su bdag bsam bya//dkar dang dmar dang 'od chen po//sna tshogs 
30a2(rgyud): brgyan gyis lus la spras //dmar po'i 'phreng ba gtsug tor can //sna tshogs gos ni legs snams pa //dmar po'i der gyi bla gos can //phyag bzung g.yon na gril bu ste //snyems bcas
30a3(rgyud): 'dzam bag ldan pa'i zhal //phyag gzhan na ni rdo rje can //snying khar rab tu gsor mdzad pa //pad ma rgyas 'dra'i spyan mnga' ba //zhal kyi dkyil 'khor nyi shar mtshungs //rdo rje seng ge'i khri la
30a4(rgyud): bzhugs //mar po'i gos ni 'chang ba po //dmar par sdus nas bri bar bya //g.yon du shes rab yongasu bri //sngon bshad pa'i gzugs can la //g.yas pa'i phyag ni 
30a5(rgyud): 'chad pa'o//lho ru sbyin pa chen mo ni //phyag rab rin chen brtsol ba mo //byang du bzod pa chen mo ni //byams pa'i yan lag 'dzin pa mo //'dun du brtson 'grus chen mo ni //
30a6(rgyud): rdo rje'i lag pa chen bzang ba //shar dang lho'i mtshams su ni //bsam gtan pha rol phyin ma bri//bsam gtan phyag can spyan yangs ma //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor 'od bzang po //bden
30a7(rgyud): bral du ni 'phel ma bri //rin po che'i brgyan gyis brgyan //nor gyi rgyun ni stong phrag gis sems can mang po bde bar byed //smon lam bri bye rlung gi ni//mtshams su yang dag mtshon
30a8(rgyud): par bya//dbyangs snyan pa'i ngag brjod pas //sems can thams cad bskul bar byed //blo gros ldan pas dbang ldan du //thabs kyi ye shes slob pa mo//lag pa dag gis gar byed pa 
30b1(rgyud): skye ga dang dga' bas 'khrugs pa bri//dpa' bos shar sgor bra bya ba //shes rab pha rol phyin dge ma //ye shes lcags kyu mdzes pa'i phyag //dmar po'i dmar gyis mngon par mdzes
30b2(rgyud): dbang ni nub gyi sgo ru ni //'ching ba'i bdag nyid bri bar bya //shin du byi ru dang mtshungs pa //rdo rje lcags sgrog 'dzin pa mo //byang gi sgo ru bri bar bya //stob kyi pha rol phyin mchog ma //rgyal
30b3(rgyud): ba thams cad mnyes byed pa //dril bu bzang po 'khrol ba'i bdag //gshin rjer cho 'phrul bri bar bya //'bar ba'i sbri ni gyis yang dag 'khrugs//phyag na rdo rje zhags bkra ba //rdo rje'i klog
30b4(rgyud): 'phreng dang mtshungs ma'o//nga'i mdun na shes ldan gyis //'dod pa'i dbang phyug rgyan pa bri//pad ma ra ga'i 'od gsal ba //mda' dang gzhu ni 'dzin pa'i mchog//de'i mdun du dkar po bri
30b5(rgyud): zla ba'i 'od 'dra grags chen po //zla ba shar ba'i 'od //mdzes pa //'dzum bag can la gzhon nu'i gzugs//rdo rje rdzang sgrogs rgyal po ni //dril bu 'khrol ba dang ldan pa //
30b6(rgyud): de'i rgyab du bri bar bya //gser dang gur gum dang 'dra'o//lho'i phyogs su bri bya ba //mkha' 'gro'i dbang phyug dpal dang ldan sngon po char srin dang 'dra ba'o//lag na ril gri
30b7(rgyud): rin chen ldan//de'i mdun na dpa' chen 'dod pa'i //rgyal po bdag nyid chen po bri //'od zer dmar po lag na gzhu //rin chen 'bar bas rnam par brgyan //de'i lho ru bri bya ba //
30b8(rgyud): rdo rje rnam par smod pa mchog//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor lhag pa mchog//rab tu rno ba'i 'bar ba'i phreng //rdo rje dam pa 'dul mdzad pa //rdo rje rin chen mchog gi tog //kha tog dkar
31a1(rgyud): //po cher spro ba //gos sngon rnam par 'chang ba mo//rdo rje DA ki'i dbang phyug rigs //lag na rin chen dam pa mdzes//de'i rgyab du bri bya ba //rgod dang bcas pa'i bzhin
31a2(rgyud): ras mchog //nga'i rgyab du bri bya ba //dri'i dbang phyug dbang dbang phyug che //lag na pad ma snying khar 'chang //ban du me ma tog rnam 'dra ba //de la mngon par chags pa'i //rdo rje sgrol
31a3(rgyud): ba spyan 'dzes bri //kar ser bzang po sna tshogs pa //lag na ud pal sngon po bzang //de'i g.yon du dag pa'i //lag na 'khor lo cher dkar bri //sngon pos legs pa'i gnas thab pa ta
31a4(rgyud): mchog tu 'dzum pa'i bzhin ras can //lo ma'i rtse mo rgyab du bri //lag na yid bzhin nor bu mchog//kha dog sngo sangs dpa' chen po //sems dpa' ldan pa'i thugs can no //de'i lho'i
31a5(rgyud): cha ru ni //chos 'cheng mchog gis bri bar bya //rgyu mtshan phyag ldan rab dkar ba //nyi ma shar ka'i snang ba 'dra //nga'i byang du bri ba bri //stug po bkod pa'i grogs po
31a6(rgyud): chen po //shin du ljang ba'i ma rgad mtshungs //las kyi rdo rje mchog 'dzin pa'o//de'i mdun du bri bya ba //rdo rje skyong ba yid 'phrog bkri//gzhad cas rdo rje'i lag pa
31a7(rgyud): can //rgyan rnams kun gyis rnams par brgyan //de'i lho ru bri bya ba //'phro ba'i rtse mo legs brgyan pa //lhang tsher nag po bung ba 'dra //rdo rje'i gsal shing 'dzin pa'o //rgyab
31a8(rgyud): du rdo rje 'ching ba bri //lag pa yang dag sbyar sngams pa //rin chen kun gyi 'do dag cing //'dzum bcas mchog tu ngo mtshar ba //byed g.yon du bri bar bya //lag na 'khor lo'i
31b1(rgyud): 'gal me'i 'khor lo can //legs so dar la bab pa'o //rab rgyas pad ma lta bu'i zhal //de nas sgo'i cha dag du //shar du rdo rje mi bayo ba //lho'i sgo ru rdo rje me//lag na rdo rje
31b2(rgyud): lcags kyu che //lag na rdo rje zhags pa 'bar //nub du rlung ni sngon po ste //rdo rje lcags sgrogs 'dzin pa'o//lus ngan du ni chu bris te //zhags pa ldan pa'i dril bu 'dzin //de nas
31b3(rgyud): sbyor ldan 'ongs nas su //dkyil 'khor gyi ni shar du bri //shar du mda'i mtshon cha lags //rab dmar pad ma ltar 'bar ba //lho ru me tog chen po can //sngon po dbang phyug rdo rje ma
31b4(rgyud): lag na rin chen snyi ma can //rin po che'i rgyan gyis rgyan //rdo rje nyer zhi dkar mdzes ma //rgyab du rtag tu bri bar bya //lag na pad ma bzang po 'dzin //pad ma'i 
31b5(rgyud): 'phreng ba rnam par brgyan //zhal ras pad ma'i mdzes ma na //sngo sangs kyi yon tan gyi ldan //lus ngan du ni shes ldan gyis //g.yas su rdo rje rtse gcig bri //
31b6(rgyud): shar gyi mtshams su rdo rje ni //dus tshig zhes bya bzang mo bri //dbyar gyi sprin ltar mdzes pa mo //lag na bdug spos grags chen mo //nub kyi mtshams su ri ri ta //lag na me tog phreng
31b7(rgyud): ba 'bar //cung zad dmar la dkar ba mo //sna tshogs brgyan gyis rnam par brgyan //nub byang du ni bri bya ba //lag na yi ge sna tshogs che //lag na sgron me rab 'bar ba //cung zad sngo ba'i
31b8(rgyud): gos gyon pa //dri'i glang po ltar bgying ba'i //rol pas sdams pa mkhas pas bri//sngo sangs dmar po mthon mthing 'dra //rab tu dgod cing mig 'dzes pa //rdo rje 'dod mi mchog

32a2(rgyud): sogs dang ni mthon mthing 'dra //rin chen sgron ma rin chen lag //mda' mchog ldan pa'i lha mo ni //nub kyi dkyil 'khor dag la bri//'od kyis snang bzhin mdzes pa la //lag

32a6(rgyud): rjes su mnyes pa'i pho nya ste //nub tu rtag tu bri bar bya //rgyal ba thams cad mnyes byed ma //chags pa'i zhags pas lags ba mdzes //khrag 'gugs pa'i pho nya mo //byang du gnas pa

32b1(rgyud): phyogs su yang dag rten //lha mo chen mo thog ma ma;lag na rdo rje bcub shing can //dmar zhing sngo ba dri bya ste //ji lta ba'i rim pas so //gdul dka' 'dul ba'i lha mo ni //sgo skyong ba'i gnas
32b2(rgyud): na gnas //dpa' bo rdo rje dbyu gu'i lag //byin za 'bar ba mtshungs ma'o//de nas dran mo bri bar bya //rang gi phyag rgyas lag pa mtshan //'jig rten gsum 'ching bran mo//nub du yang
32b3(rgyud): dag bri bar bya //'ching ba chen po'i lag pa can //rab dmar gur gum 'od zer can //bran mo 'jigs pa'i me gdong ma //sgron bcas lag pa'i rtse mo mchog //rab gnag pa la lus
32b4(rgyud): chen mo //cho ga ji bzhin rtag tu bri //bran mo brtsigs shing rnam 'gyur gdong //'khrug pa'i tshul la rab 'jig+sama //lag na thod pa rlon pa can //shin du dmar zhing 'do
32b5(rgyud): chen mo //sku gsung thugs 'ching bran mo ni //shar du yang dag bri bar bya//dkar zhing dmar la lus che ba//zhags pa lcags kyu'i lag pa mchog//de nas rol pa
32b6(rgyud): mo bri bya //go rims ji lta ba bzhin no //de bzhin gshegs pa dges pa ni //mdzes shing rin chen gyis nyer brgyan //mda' gzhu'i lag can dmar ba mo //gos dang phreng ba dmar mo 'dzin
32b7(rgyud): byang du sngags pas bri bar bya //kun nas 'bebs ma che ba'i mchog//mdzes pa'i lag pa 'dzin pa mo //bde ba 'bebs pa legs par gnas //'jig rten gsum 'joms khro ba ni //rdo rje rol
32b8(rgyud): pa mo zhes bya //sha drag shul chen pos sdigs par byed //mnyam par gzhag pas shar du bri //rab tu dga' ba mdzes rtog ma //sngags pa spro bas rgyab du bri //'dod chags phyag rgya'i lag
33a1(rgyud): //pa dmar//'gro ba'i gtso bo mnyes par mdzod//dga' byed zhes bya bri bar bya//thams cad rdo rje rol pa mo//dga' ba'i rtse 'byo lag can bkra//brgyan rnams ku gyi brgyan pa ni//
33a2(rgyud): sngags pa cho ga shes pa bris//rdo rje hU~M mdzad legs gnas pas//phyi rol rdo rje bram par bya//rang gi rig ma g.yon du gnas//dbang por mig gsum bri bar bya//rdo rje can gyi rtse gsum 'dzin
33a3(rgyud): g.yas pa yis ni phyag 'tshal zhing //rang gi chung ma sogs pas skor//chu ltar tshangs pa chen po ni//gdod pa bzhi pa bri bar bya//sgo ru sgo ba chen po ni//gdod pa ba zhi ba bri bar bya//sgo ru sgo
33a4(rgyud): ba chen po ni//rdo rje lcags kyu stobs pa che//kun nas dmar zhing cher rtum pa//sangs rgyas mchog du spyan 'dren pa'o//lho ru legs par gnag pa'i mchog//lag na 
33a5(rgyud): zhags pa dam pa bri//sems dpa' grags pa chen po//dang //sangs rgyas rnams ni 'jug par byed//'ching byed drag po chen po ni//bstan pa'i rdo rje yang dag 'dzin//bal
33a6(rgyud): po se'u'i me tog 'tshungs//rags shing stan las mi g.yo ba'o//ne tso khug pa rta dang mtshungs pa'o//mnyes byed mchog ni bri bar bya//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyes byed cing //rigs
33a7(rgyud): sngags pha rol phyin rnams kyang //rdo rje dbyu gu'i lag pa mdzes//zhi ba sogs kyis yongs su bskor//gshin rjer 'khor lo'i lag pa ni//shin du mdzes pa'i rdo rjes mtshan//ser
33a8(rgyud): po'i gos 'dzin gnag po po//mtshon cha sogs pas yongs su bskor//lus ngan du ni dbang po bri//rdo rje' lag pa rab tu mdzes//de nas mchod pa rnam par bri//rdo rje me tog mdzes pas
33b1(rgyud): gsungs //lag me tog snyam pa mdzes//me tog 'phreng bas rnam par brgyan //sngags pas rdo rje bdug pa bri //yid byed pa'i dam pa ni //lag na bdug pa mig yangs ma //mkhur tshos cung
33b2(rgyud): zad dkar ba ma'o //lha mo rdo rje snang byed dam //gser sregs pa'i mdog 'dra ba //lag na rdo rje mkhas pas bri //rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par brgyan //dri zhim lus la cha lugs bzang 
33b3(rgyud): lag na dri'i snod mdzes ma //ud pal sngon po'i lo ma mtshungs //'dzes pa'i gzugs la yid 'phrog ma'o//gzugs kyi rdo rje mdzes pa'i gzugs //rgyan rnams kun gyis ranam par
33b4(rgyud): brgyan //lag na rdo rje me long 'dzin //'dzum bcas dgod pa'i dkyil 'khor can //sgra'i rdo rje sna tshogs lus //kar dang sngo ba'i lus dang ldan //pi wang gling bu ting ting sogs//
33b5(rgyud): sgrogs pa shes rab can gyis bri //ro'i rdo rje skal chen mo //lag na ro'i snod 'dzin pa //rgyan rnams thams cad yang dag rdzogs //mtshan nyid kun dang yang dag
33b6(rgyud): ldan //rdo rje reg bya sngo sangs che//chu skyes sngon po 'dab 'dra'i mig //rnam par gsal zhing cher rgod pa //rang gi dpung pa sdams pa'o//gnod pa'i rdo rje dgod bcas
33b7(rgyud): pa //rnal 'byor pas ni mdor sdus te//bri bya sna tshogs gos bzhin bkar //rgyan rnams thams can 'dzin pa mo //rdo rje skyeg mo dkar mo che//sgyeg dang bcas shing yid du
33b8(rgyud): 'ong //rkyed ba khu tshur gnyis gzhag cing //mdzes pa'i 'phreng ba'i chun po bcug //rdo glu mkhan legs dmar lus su //glu len pa la rab tu rtson //chu skyes dmar po rgya 'dra'i
34a1(rgyud): @#//bzhin;klu len lag pa rab tu 'dzes//rdo rje gar ma mkhas pas bri//gar dang bcas pa'i lag pa can //snga sangs dkar po cher dkar ba //mig che ba la 'dzum cas mchog//rdo rje sil
34a2(rgyud): snyan grag mo che//du ru ra'i rnga mdzes gdung //rdo rje rnga chen lha mo che//rnga chen gdung ba bri bar bya //rdza rnga ma ni mkhas pas bri//rdza rnga gdung bar byed pa mo//lha mo mu kun rnga gdung ma//mu kun ma bzhin mdzes ma'o
34a3(rgyud): phaT ha ni rdung byed ma;rdo rje ba ha zhes bya //rdza rnga chen po rdung byed ma//rdo rje rdza rnga mdzes ma dri //gu ny+dza rdung byed dga' ba mo//rgod cas pad ma'i mig can ma//rdo rje gun dza dpa' bos bri //las kun gyi ni dam pa 'grub
34a4(rgyud): lag na rdo rje ting ting cag //rdo rje ting ting cag bri bya//lag na rdo rje cang de ma//kha dog dkar po 'od zer che//rdo rje mkhar rnga rdung ba'i lag//rdo rje mkhar rnga ma bri bya //rdo rje pi wang sgrang ba mo//

34b1(rgyud): snyi ma can //lag pa du ma gdong rgya ma'o//rdo rje gar ma bsnyems bcas ma//las bcu ba dan dang ldan pa//rnal 'byor pas ni cho ga gzhin //rdo rje sbyor bas rab gnas bri //sna tshogs gar

34b5(rgyud): par byed //rdzi ma'i bcas pa'i mig gnyis ni//steng dang 'og du 'phangs pa yis//mthong ba 'gugs bye nyan de chen gyis//sangs rgyas thams cad dbang du byed pa'o//sgo ru 
34b6(rgyud): sgo ba chen po ni //rdo rje lcags kyu stobs po che //thams cad bsrung zhing cher rtum pa //sangs rgyas mchog tu spyan 'dren pa'o//lho ru legs par gnas pa'i mchog//lag na zhags pa dam pa bri

36a1(rgyud): //de bzhin bu mo dag kyang ni//som nyi rnam par bral sems gyis//khyod kyis rtag tu brtan par bya//sems can thams cad gsad bya zhing //rtul shugs spyad pa skyang bar bya//'dzungs

36b5(rgyud): yis ni legs bzung ste//lce'i sbyor brtse mo mkhas pas sbyor//rdo rje 'am yang na chu skyes kyis//phyag rgya dang ni phyag rgya'i lan//de nas 'dzin ma rnam par smin//de nyid la yang mkhas
36b6(rgyud): pas bya//gsang ba brgyan gyi sbyor ba yis//gsang ba'i bdo ba bsgrub par bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das kun kun du bzang pos bka' stsal to//de nas yon ni dbul bar bya//bla mar phyag 'tshal
36b7(rgyud): mchod byas nas//ma dang ma mo nyid dang yang //sring mo yi ni bu dag dang //sring mo 'am yang na chung ma dang //rgyal po longs spyod skye bo'i tshogs//grong dang yul 'khor grong khyer dang //nor dang

37a1(rgyud): @#//par 'chod//pha dang spun yang mchod par bya//de bzhin spun zla'i bu yang mchod //bza' btung yid du 'ong ba dang //gos dang brgyan dang me tog dang //de bzhin dri dang gdugs pa
37a2(rgyud): sogs//ji ltar mdog pa thams cad kyis//mchod pas byas te btang bar bya//lha rnams dag ni thams cad la //de bzhin du ni mchod pa bya//gsang sngags rgyal ba gshegs su gsol//badzra
37a3(rgyud): mu zhes brjod pas gshegs//de nas sngags pas slob pas slob ma'i mchog de la//gsang ba rnam par 'dzin la gsang ba'i //rgyal pos gsal mdzad gsang bdag gis//gsang ba mtha' dag

37a5(rgyud): tsom med//lha mo shes rab che de yang //rang nyid y+nga rdo rje 'chang chen ste//nam mkha' kun gyi mchog dpa'o//rga dang 'chi ba med par 'gyur //'dod pa dbang phyug rnal 'byor che //

37a7(rgyud): pa'i khams kyi dbang phyug kun //bran dang 'dra bar 'khod par 'gyur //namakha' 'khor yid 'ong ba//namakha'i sa bon bsgom par par//de ni sbyor bas spros nas su//rang gi snying khar bzhug
37a8(rgyud): par bya //gtsug nas rin chen chen po ni//srid pa 'joms par bsgoms par bya//yid bzhin rin chen rang bzhin 'dzes//nyi ma bdun na de 'gyur ro//'dam skyes dmar po'i dbus su ni//
37b1(rgyud): 'bad pas kun ni dri yis gang //dri'i dbang phyug rgyal po che//bdag nyid sems dpa' yid ches bsgom //de nas myur du blo ldan te//dri za'i bdag po'i bdag por 'gyur //dri yis phyag rgya 'ang
37b2(rgyud): de 'di 'tshungs//myur ba nyid du ni//rtan dang g.yon pa'i dug 'gugs byed//don yod kyi ni dkyil 'khor du//snying po snying gi sbyor ba bsam //yid la bsam pa'i dngos grub 'gyur //de yi phyag
37b3(rgyud): rgya 'ang de 'ang mtshungs//rin chen gyi 'ang rin chen che//rin chen mig 'od gsal la//de bzhin dri dang ldan pa ni//sgrub pa pos yis 'grub par 'gyur //de la sogs pa'i dpe ba yis//de lasogs
37b4(rgyud): pas dpe' yis//lha gzhan dag kyang rig par bya //dam 'khor ram ni dam tshig kyang //sgom pas myur du 'grub par 'gyur //snying po snying dang phyag rgya dang //rigs sngags
37b5(rgyud): kyi 'ang sbyor ba yis//de rnams kyi 'ang de nyid nas//sgoms na myur du nges par 'grub //'di ni bcom ldan rdo rje can //gsang ba'i brgyan dag dam pas gsungs //
37b6(rgyud): phyag rgya bzhi'i mtshan gyi mchog//phyag rgya thams cad 'grub par 'gyur //zhes so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yab dang yum de rnams kyis gsol pa//myur du sbyor ba
37b7(rgyud): mchog bsgrubs pas//sgrub pa pos ni ji ltar 'grub //de dag thams cad myur du gsungs //bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang bdag gis//snying po sngags dang phyag rgya dang //rig sngags
37b8(rgyud): dag ni legs par 'grub //btsun mo mchog gi gnas dbang phyug//dpa' bo chen pos gsung du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad
38a1(rgyud): @#//kyi gtsug tor rnam par rgyal ba rnam par sbyings pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yab dang yum rnams kyi zhu ba'i tshig rdo rje 'di 
38a2(rgyud): nyid kyi sku dang //gsung dang thugs rnams las phyung ngo//lha rnams kyi snying ni snying po dang//sngags dang phyag rgya de bzhin du //dkyil 'khor gyi ni legs par gshad//ji ltar sngon du kha dog sogs
38a3(rgyud): de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis slar yang 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyis gsung du gsol//sems can rnams la phan bde'i phyir//rnal 'byor ma'i phyag rgya dang //
38a4(rgyud): mtshan ma la sogs dam pa rnams //de nas bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang //rdo rje dam par gsor bar mdzad //lag pa'i mtshan ma bka' stsal pa //gang gis lha rnams mchod 'gyur ba //
38a5(rgyud): lag pa zla ba nyi ma ru //mkhas pa yis ni bri bar bya//shes rab thabs kyi sa bon ni//rnal 'byor can gyis rtag tu spro//slar yang sdud ba'i sbyor ba yis//rdo rje pad ma rnams gsam
38a6(rgyud): bya//de nas shes rab pha rol phyin//sor mo'i rtse mor bgod par bya//de nyid sbyor bas sdus nas su//phan tshun nges par sbyar bar bya//rdo rje pad ma mnyam sbyor bas//bde ba myangs pas 'grub par 'gyur
38a7(rgyud): 'ching ba chen pos bcings nas su//lha mo'i sa bon gyis mtshan pa'i //gsang sngags rgyal po sngags pas ni//yang dang yang du byin gyis rlab//bcings te 'od ldan rnam pa gnyis//rkyang nas rtse mo
38a8(rgyud): sbyar bar bya //de 'di snying po zhes bya'o//gnyis pa grags pa ldan pa yin //snying po yis ni mtshan byas nas //rims kyis snying kar bskor bar bya//rdo rje mnyam par sbyar bya te //der ni bcings
38b1(rgyud): pa bzhug par bya//'di ni dam tshig zhes bya ste//dam tshig ma lus bsgrub par byed//'di yis lha mo mtha' yas zhal//thams cad du ni nges par 'grub//de nyid sdigs pa lta bur sbyar//las kun byed pa'i
38b2(rgyud): mchog du 'gyur//rdo rje gnyis ni pad ma yis//sbyor ba rtag tu mchod du rmad//de las phyi rol bskums pa ni//gsam pa ru ni 'gyur ba yin//snying dga' byed pa zhes byar brjod//rdo rje gnyis po de bzhin
38b3(rgyud): sbyar//dam tshig dag byed nyid du 'gyur//de nyid kun gyi 'byung gnas gsal//chen po'i las ni byed par 'gyur//de nyid mdzad spur sbyar ba rdzid//phyag rgya bdag nyid chen por 'gyur//de nyid chu skyes
38b4(rgyud): rnam par ru//snying khar gzhag pa snying po mchog//de bzhin rdo rje mnyam sbyor ba//dam tshig bdag nyid ched du/'gyur//snying khar kun sdigs sbyor ba yis//rab rgyas pa'i bzhin
38b5(rgyud): ras mchog//mnyes par byed pa thams cad kyang//snying gi dkyil 'khor nyid du bzung //'di ni dam tshig dngos grub che//bdag nyid chen pos bslab par bya//las kyi rdo rje
38b6(rgyud): snyems pa yis//las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub byed//dbab dang 'khor ba nyid du ni//gar byed chen po de bzhin du//de bzhin thal mo sbyar nas su//snying khar gzhag pa snying
38b7(rgyud): por bshad//spyi bor bskor ba byas pa ni//dam tshig zhes bya bdag nyid che//steng du sdigs pa las ni 'gyur//mchod pa ma lus rab bsgrub byed//snying po la sogs 'og tu bzung //chos la sogs par
38b8(rgyud): yongasu brtag//de nyid rkyang ste snying po zhes//su legs par bshad//bskor ba dam tshig zhes bya ste//las kyang thams cad de bzhin no//de nas thal mo rdo rje gnyis//steng sgreng snying po
39a1(rgyud): @#//laswogs yongasu rtag/sngon bshad lha mo thams cad kyi//gsang sngags rig pas shes par bya//gnyis su byas la g.yas min pas//bsgrub bya'i 'grin la bzung nas su//mngon par
39a2(rgyud): phyogs te bzhag par bya//gzugs kyi snying po ches che'o//de nyid kha'i phyogs gzhag pa//sgra'i snying po'i yid 'phrog pa'o//de nyid cung zad bskums nas su//lce'i rnam pas kha ru gzhag //
39a3(rgyud): ro myang ba'i sbyor ba yis//snying khar legs sbyar sbyor ba dag gis//de nyid la 'ang rnam pa bzhi//phyag rgya sngags pas shes par bya//ro 'phrog pa'i las rnams so//de ni gnyis su byas nas sa
39a4(rgyud): g.yas pa 'am ni g.yon pa yis//snying khar legs sbyarasbyor ba dag gis//ring ba'i phyag rgya ngo mtshar che//'di yis chos dang dam tshig dang //las kyang rab ste skye ba yin//
39a5(rgyud): cho ga shes pas rnal 'byor sbyar//reg pas 'bebs pa'i mchog yin no//de bzhin sbyar ba so sor bya//g.yon ba rkyed par gzhag par bya//g.yas pa kha'i phyogs su gzhag //
39a6(rgyud): khro ba'i tshul gyis de bzhin du//'di ni gzhad ldan zhes bya ba'i//phyag rgya mchog du rmad 'byung ngo//de las bkrol te snga ma bzhin//rkyed pa ru ni legs par gzhag //de las lengs te slar
39a7(rgyud): yang khar//mkhas pas dal gyis skyod par bya//de gnyis snying khar yongs su sbyar//snyems par 'dud dang 'gyed par byed //ji srid spyi bor btugs pa yis//chos dang la sogs kun du
39a8(rgyud): 'gyur//snying po la sogs sngags rim pas//phyag rgya 'di yis brgyas btab na//dngos grub thams cad thob 'gyur te//rdo rje sems dpa'i dka' bzhin no//thal mo mnyam par legs par
39b1(rgyud): legs par sbyar //g.yon pa'i steng du de bzhin gzhag //de dag snying khar bsgreng bar bya//de ni phan tshun shes rab yin //sngags pa chen pos sbyor bar bya//gzhan ni sbyor bas rnam par spro//
39b2(rgyud): 'di ni chos kyi pha rol du//'gro ba'i chos kyi mchog tu bshad //de bzhin thal mo mnyam por sbyor//snying khar g.yon par gzhag par bya//'do ldan rdo rje'i gdong pa dang//mdor sdus nas
39b3(rgyud): ni sbyar nas su//g.yas pa mthur du 'phyang bar bya//de bzhin steng bstan mchog sbyin no//me tog snyim pa yang dag sbyar //rdo rje gnyis ni dbus su gzhag //bzod pa gnyis po de la sbyar //
39b4(rgyud): de ni steng du legs par bzhag//de ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi //yid bzhin rgyal po'i 'bras bur ster//de ni sbyor bas so sor bya//g.yon pa'i snying khar yongasu gzhag //g.yas pa
39b5(rgyud): spro ba'i sbyor ba yis//yongs su zhing bskor nas su//g.yas pa ru ni gzhag par bya//rdo rje thal mo rkyang nas su//rdo rje'i steng du mdzes ma ste//phyi rol bskum ba'i spyo ba yis//
39b6(rgyud): de'i steng du gdong pa gzhag //phyag rgya 'di ni phyag rgya che//brtson 'grus pha rol phyin dge ma'o//de bzhin lag pa steng du sbyar //rtsibs mchog rin chen ma ru 'gyur //lhag ma'i bya ba de nyid kyis
39b7(rgyud): de bzhin gzhag cing bsam par bya//sbyor ba de yis bcing ba ru//sngags shes rtso bo sngags pas bya//rdo rje la thal mo mnyam par sbyar //khro bo rdo rje'i sbyor ba yis//sbyong khar blo gros ldan pas bzung
39b8(rgyud): de ni lcags kyu rnam pa yis//snying khar blo gros ldan pas gzung//de ni lcags kyu'i rnam pa yis//ltas nas lus can 'gugs par byed//de nas sor ma so so rnams//cung zad bsnol te brkyang bar bya//
40a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rje 'che ba lta bur sgreng//kha sbyar sgrol byed ngo mtshar che//rgya 'di rnams kyi rgyas btab pa//las rnams thams cad byed par 'gyur//de las thal mor byas nas su//gzi brjid rdo rje de 
40a2(rgyud): bzhin no//de bzhin lcags kyus dgug pa ste//de'i mtha' gdong zhags pa'o//sog le'i thal mo nges par ni//'dir ni thod pa nyid du 'gyur//'di ni gnyis pa nyid yin te//las kun gyi ni dam 
40a3(rgyud): pa bsgrub//de bzhin bcing ba legs bcings nas//rab tu sbyin pa rkyang bar bya//g.yas pa steng du byas nas su//gsang ba lta bur bcing bar bya//'di ni rdo rje mdung thung ste//srin po thams cad phye
40a4(rgyud): mar byed//'dir ni de bzhin thod pa dang//zhags pa lcags kyu nyid dang yang//sog le dang ni gri thung gi//rdo rje thal mo'i tshogs bcas dang//rdo rje thal mo so sor bya//lag pa
40a5(rgyud): g.yas pa steng du ni//rin chen lta bur byas la bzung//sor mo thams cad spro bar bya//g.yon pa 'ang de bzhin spro bar bya//thal mo sgreng ste shes rab dang//las kyi rdo rje rtse mo sbyar
40a6(rgyud): 'di ni las rnams thams cad kyi//sna tshogs las mchog bsgrub par byed//de bzhin dgug dang zhags pa dang//thod pa rdo rje nyid yin no//de las thal mo thod pa ni//phyag rgya'i dbus su bu ga ni//
40a7(rgyud): sgron ma can zhes legs bshad de//'di ni rdo rje ngo mtshar che//zhags pa lcags kyu de bzhin du//slob dpon blo gros ldan pas shes//gzungs rnams dag ni thams cad kyi//sngar bshad pa'i 
40a8(rgyud): phyag rgya nyid//thod pa rdo rje 'ang shes par bya//phan tshun gcig la gcig tu ni//thams cad gzungs kyi phyag rgya ru//dkyil 'khor du ni rab tu bsgrags//'di rnams sbyor ba ji lta bar//
40b1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor bzhi ni rtag par ru//rdo rje slob dpon gyis bya ste//phyag na rdo rje'i bka' bzhin no//'di ni bcom ldan rdo rje gsung //kun bzang bdag nyid chen po pa//sems can kun mchog las
40b2(rgyud): kyi mchog//sangs rgyas mchog rtso mnga' bdag che//zhes so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang po yang //'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug ye shes rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la
40b3(rgyud): snyoms par zhugs nas//de dag thams cad la yang dang yang du gzigs te//sems can khams thams cad brtson 'grus zhan pa//spro pa dman pa dman pa la mos par gzigs nas /de rnams
40b4(rgyud): la bka' sgo bar dam pa'i ma mo rnams kyis shod/rang rang gi ni sgrub sna tshogs//de nas de bzhin legs tshim nas//rdo rje can la gsol ba ni//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//
40b5(rgyud): rdo rje dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//de'i steng du rdo rje che//rdo rje la ni dngos po mchog//nyi ma gser mdog dang mtshungs par//gsang sngags shes pas de sgom bya//rang gi lus las spros
40b6(rgyud): nas su//rang gi rnam par bsam par bya//de nas sngags bdag 'grub par 'gyur//sngags kyi de nyid bdag po cher//kun du 'gro ba sna tshogs che//rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra ba gzhan//gnyis pa de nyid
40b7(rgyud): bsgom pa ni//dkyil 'khor pad ma las byung ba//dmar po'i gos la gzi brjid che//'bar ba dag la me dang mtshungs//rang las spro bya thams cad du//'jig rten dbang phyug lta bur 'gyur//dkyil
40b8(rgyud): 'khor dbang chen bzhag par bya//dkyil 'khor la ni cher dge mo//dang po lha mo drod dang ldan//rang gi lus la nges par khyab//bsdus nas bdag dang 'dra ba'i bdag//yid bzhin nor bu'i snang bar
41a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur //rlung gi dkyil 'khor dbus son par //'gro ba'i sgo rtan lha mo ni//rang lus lus ldan thams cad du//ljong khu dang ni ljang ser ma//las kun bsgrub pa'i dam pa'i//rdo rje 'dzin pa
41a2(rgyud): dpal ldan 'gyur //ma mo bzhi dang yang dag ldan //shes rab ye shes dam pa gnas//dkyil 'khor kun gyi dam par ru//mkhas pa yis ni bsgom par bya//kun gyi bdag po dpal dang ldan//kun bzang mnga' bdag 
41a3(rgyud): chen po ni//bsam gtan dkyil 'khor sbyor ba yis//thams cad nyid na bsgrub par bya//chos kyi yi ge yang dag sbyar//lha min bdag por bdag 'gyur ro//zhes bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos bka' stsal to//
41a4(rgyud): gsang ba brgyan gyi rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang //de bzhin gshegs pa mthas cad kyi yab dang //'khor dang bcas pa rnams slar yang 'dus par gyur te/bcom ldan 'das kun
41a5(rgyud): du bzang po la dbyangs kyi rgyal po 'dis gsol ba btab pa//thaMs cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes che//mkha' la rlung ltar cher gshegs pa/yab kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes bzang //kun mchog dam pas gsung du 
41a6(rgyud): gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang pos de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rgyal po mchog //rdo rje dpal zhes bya ba'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//ye shes chen po'i phyag rgya dang //dkyil
41a7(rgyud): 'khor 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang /thugs rdo rje rgyas pa las nges par phyung ngo//dbus su nyi ma chen po ni //hU~M yig sbyor bas bsam par bya//sor mo kun gyi rtse gnas te//sa bon lnga'i yab dgod bya//tshogs
41a8(rgyud): kyi nang ni thams cad du//yi ge thams cad rnam par bsam //nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dag la ni//bzang po thams cad rnam bsam bya//'khor lo chen po'i phyag rgya'i tshul //lag pa g.yon par bsam par bya//
41b1(rgyud): de'i dbus su shes rab che//de bzhin gzhan yang dgod par bya//de nyid spro ba'i de nyid ni//gsang sngags 'di yis sbyar bar bya//o~M pra dz+nya pa ya ma ya yo go hU~M oM//de nas phan tshun sbyar nas //rdo rje thal
41b2(rgyud): mo rab tu gnas//rdo rje sngams pas byas nas su//rdo rje sngams par rnam par bsam //de nas shes rab che sgreng ste//rtse mo cung zad snums par bya//'dod pa'i dbang phyug snying por 'gyur //snying khar gzhag na snying
41b3(rgyud): po 'grub//de nyid sdigs pa'i sbyor ba yis//las kyi mchog du 'gyur ba yin//de nyid snying khar sngams pa ni//phyag rgya chen po zhes su grags //thams cad kyis kyang mdor sdus pa'i //las rnams thams
41b4(rgyud): cad rab sgrub byed//de bzhin legs par bcing bar byed//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin pa gnyis//phyi'i rgyab du skums par bya//mkha' 'gro'i dbang phyug snying dga' ba'o//shes rab de ni de bzhin sbyor
41b5(rgyud): dam tshig zhes par rab tu grags //lha ma rnam pa spro bar bya//las kyi gnas 'khod chen por grags//shes rab dag ni zum tshul gyis//dri'i dbang phyug rab dges ma'o//bzod dang brtson 'grus
41b6(rgyud): gdong pa gnyis//lo ma'i tshul du rkyang bar bya//dam tshig grub pa ster bar gshad//'di las rtse mo rnam phye ma//las kun byed pa zhes bshad de//re ba thams cad rdzogs par byed//de nyid rtse mo snying khar sbyar //
41b7(rgyud): bdag nyid chen po'i phyag rgya mchog //rgyal po mchog lta'i rgyal po ru//'di yis myur du 'grub par 'gyur //rdo rje sngams pas shes rab 'od//dril bu' rnam pa snying po mchog//bzod dang brtson 'grub gdong
41b8(rgyud): sbyar ba //dam tshig mnyam pa nyid du gnas //cung zad gdong pa skum par bya//las byed pa'i mchog tu 'gyur //gdong pa legs par rkyang nas su//steng du shes rab rtse mo gcig//'di ni chen por shes bya ste//
42a1(rgyud): @#//dbang phyug thams cad byed pa'i mchog//sna tshogs byed pa'i rtse mo ste//'di yis myur du 'grub par 'gyur //rang gis sngams las shes rab sgreng //rdo rje spra ba'i chos dang ldan //gdod pa
42a2(rgyud): phye ste rin po che//'di ni dam tshig ldan pa'i mchog //de gnyis de bzhin rnam par bzhag //sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin dag dag //rang rkyang zhi bzhi sems dpa'i //las rnams thams cad byed par bshad //de bzhin tshul
42a3(rgyud): khrims pha rol phyin //'di ni chen po'i dam pa'o//bsams gtan la bzod dang brtson dag //legs par brkyang ste bstod pa yis//snying po'i zla ba ltar bcings pa//dam tshig 'od zer 'phro ba'o//phan tshun gyi ni
42a4(rgyud): bdod sbyar ba//'di ni lag ces dam pa ste//lhag ma khu tshur ltar bcas pa //phyag rgya chen po zhes su grags//rdo rje sngams pa legs par bcing //rtse mo'i rnam par sbyar bar bya//shes rab 'ba' zhig 
42a5(rgyud): rtse mor bya//lhag ma dag ni de bzhin no//lhag ma sngams te bzhug par bya//snying po chos ni byed pa'i mchog//khu tshur g.yon pa g.yon du bzung //rdo rje rdo rje can gyis brkyang //g.yas pa'i byin
42a6(rgyud): pa rkyang nas ni//rna ba'i rtsa bar dgug par bya//'di ni las byed shes bya ste//sangs rgyas byang chub rab bsgrub byed //rdo rje snyams dpa' rkyang nas su//snying khar 'khor lo'i tshul bya ba //g.yas pa'i rtse 
42a7(rgyud): mor lcags kyur bya//sbyin pa sbyin pa'i pha rol son//de bzhin bcings la legs sngams bya//gnyi ka de bzhin lcags kyu ste//de bzhin 'og ma bcug nas su//snying khar gzhag pa las rjes 'breng //de nyid dgug
42a8(rgyud): par sbyar ba ni//thams cad 'gugs par rab tu 'grub //de bzhin byin pa skums nas su//yang dang yang du rkyang bar bya//snying khar skor ba zhes su bshad //de nyid 'og du bstan pa ni//dam tshig can zhes rab du grags//
42b1(rgyud): rdo rje chos su bshad pa ste//kha ru g.yo bar byed pa mchog//las rnams rnam par tshim zhes bya//las kun byed pa'i dam pa ste//lag pa bcings pa las bkrol te//shes rab 'dzod du rab tu rtags//'di
42b2(rgyud): ni dam tshig cher 'dzes pa//de phyir da ltar chos mdzod do//g.yas pas rab tu bsgribs pa ni//mi snang 'dzod de de nges legs//rin chen ldan pas pad ma'i gdong //snying khar gzhag pa snying por 'gyur //
42b3(rgyud): sngams nas go bya byas nas su//bskor ba dam tshig zhes su 'dod//'khyud pa chos ldan zhesu grags//snun pa las byed pa ru brjod//sngams pa las nyid rnam par spro//bskor ba dam tshig mchog yin no//
42b4(rgyud): lag pa'i mtha' ma chos chen po//kun sdud las kun byed pa'o//sngams las sbyin na sgreng bar byed//rtse mo gsum bya gnas mdzes pa'o//de nyid du bskor nas su//spros pas chos bsgrub byed pa'o
42b5(rgyud): rdo rje kha ru sbyar ba las//dbang bskur dam pa bsgrub par byed//de nyid steng du spro ba ni//las kun byed pa'i mchog yin no//snying khar legs bzung snying po ste//mdzod spur gzhag pa dam tshig
42b6(rgyud): mchog//khar gzhag pa ni chos shes bya//las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub pa'o//'di ni bcom ldan rdo rje can//re ba kun rdzogs mdzad pas gsungs//rdo rje sngams pa rnam par bcing //pad
42b7(rgyud): ma'i rnam pa mchog du bya//ster byed pa ni mig ltar bya//snying khar gzhag pas kun mchog 'grub//de nyid mig gzhag las yin no//gzhad pas chos te dam pa'i chos//dam tshig gsang ba'i mig yin te
42b8(rgyud): pad ma'i 'dzod la rab tu gnas//thal mo yang dag sbyar ba ni//snying po'i mchog du rmad 'byung ba'o//kha ni cung zad phye ba ste//dam tshig mchog gi mchog yin no//snying khar10 shin du gsal phye ba//ma lus las
43a1(rgyud): @#//ni rab tu bsgrub //rdo rje'i nang du pad ma ni//chos steng mchog du rmad 'byung ba'o//'di rnams kyi ni myur 'grub ste//sngags pas sngags kyis bskul bas so//de bzhin 'ching ba legs
43a2(rgyud): bcings te//snying khar 'khor lo nyid du bya//'khor lo gsum pa nyid du bya//'khor lo la gsum pa nyid du 'gyur //rtsibs bzhis legs pa rgyan chos su lta//bskor ba las kyi rdo rje'o//'di
43a3(rgyud): rnams ji lta bzhin sbyar na//grub pa'i dam pa 'grub par 'gyur //rdo rje sdams pa legs bsgreng ste//shes rab myu gu dag du bya//sbyin pa rin chen ltar byas pa //sems dpa'i gnas su nges par
43a4(rgyud): sbyar //'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub pa'o//snying khar chos kyi dam tshig dang //snying po rigs sngags legs par sbyar //'di ni sangs rgyasathams cad kyi//
43a5(rgyud): las rnams thams cad rab sgrub pa'o//snying khar chos//kyi dam tshig dang //snying bo rigs sngags legs par spyar //de bzhin shes rab gnyis po bsgreng //'og gi 'khrug pa 
43a6(rgyud): de bzhin no//mthe chung bzang po kha sbyar ba //kun dga' rdo rje ldan par bya//gnas ni bzhi'i sbyor ba yis//phyag rgya bzhi ni rab tu 'grub//spyi bor lasu bshad pa ste//snying khar snying po rtog par bya//
43a7(rgyud): bskor ba yis ni dam tshig ste//dag gis kun bskul chos yin ces//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs //bcings nas shes rab dag ni bsgreng //'od ldan dag ni rtsa bar sbyar //'di ni dang por shes
43a8(rgyud): par bya//thams cad nas ni thams cad 'gro//bcings la de bzhin shes rab gcig//gtso mor 'che ba bdug yid 'phrog //de bzhin du ni 'di sbyar bas //las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub pa'o//gsang
43b1(rgyud): ba'i bcing ba legs bcings te//rdo rje nang du bzhin no//snying khar rnal 'byor pa yis sbyar //de snyed las kyi ming bzang po //rdo rje rab rkyang 'grin pa bzung //kun du bskor ba rab tu bya//'di yis
43b2(rgyud): las thams cad ni//byed 'gyur srung 'khor sogs go 'phang bzang //lha mchog bzhin rnal 'byor//rnal 'byor de nyid rab tu sbyar//phyag rgya rtags dang ming rnams ni//lha rnams kun la sbyar
43b3(rgyud): bar bya//'di ni bcom ldan rnal 'byor che'i//dbang phyug dpal rdo rje'i sems dpas gsungs //de nas de rnams legs tshim nas//phyag 'tshal nas ni thal sbyar te//zhu ba rdo rje bla na med //
43b4(rgyud): dpa'o rdo rje can la zhus //yongs su rtogs pa kun spangs pa'i//sangs rgyas rnams ni rab byung ba//yab dang yum ni bla na med//ci phyir 'di dag rtags pa lags//'khor ba las ni leg+sa
43b5(rgyud): pa'i //sangs rgyas ji ltar 'byung ba lags //grong gi chos ni bsnyen pa lags //ji ltar thar pa rab tu 'grub //gang du rnal 'byor grol bar 'gyur //gang na 'ching ba rab tu gnas //
43b6(rgyud): gang gis rnal 'byor pa gang 'grub //gang las rnal 'byor pa zhes bya//gsang sngags ji ltar gsang sngags lags //snying po zhes bya'i snying po gang //phyag rgya gang phyir phyag rgyar 'gyur //rigs sngag+sa
43b7(rgyud): ji ltar rig pa lags //ji ltar lha rnams dgug pa dang //bzhug pa rnal 'byor pa yis bgyi //rnam par grol dang rnam bcing dang //mnyes pa dang bskul ba de bzhin du//ji ltar gnyis su med 'gyur
43b8(rgyud): zhing //ro gcig 'gyur ba'i lus lags pa//btsum pa mkhyud pa nyid dang yang //mthong nas rjesu chags pa dang //lag pa'i dges pa ji snyed pas //phyag rgya ji ltar 'grub par 'gyur //gar
44a1(rgyud): @#//dang klu dang cher dgod pa//sgyeg dang smra ba ji ltar lags //gzugs dang sgra dang de bzhin dri //gtso bo ro dang reg par 'gyur //me tog bdug pa mchog dang dri //mar me gsal ba mchog 
44a2(rgyud): gang lags //mchod pa thams cad ji ltar 'grub //ji ltar mchod pas 'grub par 'gyur //rgyal bas zhu ba 'di dag ni//'phreng ba rdo rje can la zhus //zhes so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos
44a3(rgyud): zhu ba thams cad rnam par spyod pa//longs spyod pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis zhu ba rgyas pa 'di dag byung ngo//shes rab yum du
44a4(rgyud): legs bshad de //yab ni thabs las mkhas pa'o//'di ni bstan pa nyid kyis ni //grong las thar pa rab tu 'grub //bag chags sbyor ba dang ldan pas//gnyi ga dag ni gdon yin te//grol dang
44a5(rgyud): ma grol zhes su bshad //de ni rnal 'byor pa ru bshad pa ste;snying po la ni de bzhin no//yid la rnam par gzhag na ni//gsang sngags mkhas pas 'grub par 'gyur /thar pa chos
44a6(rgyud): ni rgya mtsho yis//lha rnams gang la rgyas btab pa'i//phyag rgya 'di yis 'grub 'gyur te//dngos grub bde ba bla na med //rigs sngags ye shes ma lus te//rigs sngags pha rol phyin par
44a7(rgyud): bshad//de shes pas ni 'grub 'gyur te //grub nas bde ba rnam pa gsum //shes rab la ni shes rab ni//gzhug dang dgug pa de bzhin no//gshegs su gsol ba btang bas shes//bcing ba 
44a8(rgyud): brtan par byed pa'o//mnyes pa yis ni legs tshim yin//bskul ba phro ba nyid du bshad //bsam pa gcig du gyur pa ni //gnyis su med pa zhes su brtags//ro gcig de nyid bshad pa ni //
44b1(rgyud): 'jig rten pa'i lha ru brjod//btsum pa rdo rje rnam 'byor te//rdo rje ye shes zhes su bshad //shes dang mi shes mnyam sbyor bas//rdo rje ye shes zhes bshad do//shes dang mi shes mnyams sbyor bas
44b2(rgyud): tsum pa na zhes nges par gshad //gnas pas rtag zhes bshad pa ste//rtags ni lus zhes brjod pa yin//de'i 'khyud ba reg par shes//chags pa mthong ba'i phyag rgya'o//lag pa'i 'od rnams
44b3(rgyud): lha mo ste//rdo rje pad mo mnyam 'grod pa'o//phyag rgya 'di las phyag rgya ste//lag pa'i dgod pa'i phyag rgya'o//gar dang klu sogs rnams kyis ni//lha rnams thams cad mnyes par 'gyur //'di rnams
44b4(rgyud): kyis ni sangs rgyas rnams //mchod na dngos grub thams cad ster//zhes bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos bka' stsal to////gsang ba brgyan gyis bkod pa'i tshe rgyud chen po las //
44b5(rgyud): gsang ba rgyan gyi bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po yongasu rdzogs s+ho////de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yum rnams
44b6(rgyud): kyis //bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang po chen po la //de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi mchod pa dang //mchod pa'i lan dang /phyag rgya dang /phyag rgya'i lan dang /gar dang /gar gi lan dang /klung dang
44b7(rgyud): klu'i lan dang /sil snyan dang /sil snyan gyi lan dang /rnam 'gyur dang /rnam 'gyur gyi lan dang /dgug pa dang /dgug pa'i lan dang /bda' dang bda' lan dang /bri ba dang bri ba'i lan dang /blta ba
44b8(rgyud): dang /blta ba'i lan dang /dam tshig dang dam tshig gi lan dang /dkyil 'khor gyi lan dang /gsang ba dang /gsang ba'i lan dang /gsang chen dang /gsang chen gyi lan dang /de kho na nyid kyi lan
46a1(rgyud): @#//la sogs pa rnams dang /klu'i rgyal po 'dis gsol ba btab par gyur to//sangs rgyas sems can kun gyi don//ye shes kun don thams cad mdzad//thams cad khyab pa sems can don//
46a2(rgyud): gnyis dang gnyis med phyal 'tshal stod//'di dag thams cad gsung du gsol//bsgrub pa po la phan dges pas//bsgrub pa po la dngos grub la dga' ba//gang gis dngos grub thob 'gyur ba//de nas 
46a3(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la lta ba rnam par bsgyings pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/'di skad ces bka' stsal to//me tog bdug pa dri zhim 
46a4(rgyud): dang //gos bzang brgyan dang na bza' dang /btung ba ro dang sgron ma gsal//mal khri bstan dag yid 'ong ba//'di yis sangs rgyas thams cad mchod//'di ni bde bar gnas par byed//slob 
46a5(rgyud): dpon rdo rje can dang ni//khyad par du ni bsgrub pas bya//rgod dang sgyeg dang de bzhin glu//mchod sil snyan gar dang rab dga' bar//mchod pa'i lan te shin du ni//sangs rgyas phyag rgyas 
46a6(rgyud): 'grub par 'gyur//gzugs dang sgra dang drang ni//ro dang reg pa yid 'ong ba//'di ni mchod pa'i lan du grub//sangs rgyas byang chub bde ba che//phyag rgya skyes bu'i rnam pa ste//phyag rgya'i lan ni rang gzugs 
46a7(rgyud): can//gnyis pa phyag rgya chen po ste//phyag rgya'i lan ni las bzang can//phyag rgya rang gi bdag por shes//phyag rgya'i lan ni bsgrub pa po//ji srid dbye ba stong phrag ni//dbye ba bzhi ru rtag par bya//
46a8(rgyud): zhes so//gar gyi gar gyi sbyor ba yis//gar gyi lan gar mkhan ma'o//ji srid dbye ba stong phrad kyang //dbye ba bzhi ru rtag bar bya//kun du bzang po la rten nas//ji srid las kyi rjes 'breng ba'o//las 
46b1(rgyud): kyi sbyor ba yang dag brten//ji srid bzang po'i rjes 'breng ba'o//bla 'og yang dag bskyed pa yin//gar dang gar gi steng skyod pa//rtog pa'i sbyor bas rtag par bya//rtog pa yis ni rab tu gsang //ji ltara'i sil snyan 
46b2(rgyud): rdung spyod cing//gdung ba'i bdag nyid can gyis bdung //de las byung ba sil snyan lan//sil bas sil snyan nyid du brjod//sngags kyi yi ge'i cho ga sbyar//ji srid las mchog spyod yul ba//de nas sbyor bas mthon
46b3(rgyud): nas su//kun du bzang por 'gro bar bya//sgrogs pas klu zhes brjod pa ste//'dren pa gsum las legs par byung //dbyings rnams rnam drug shes par bya//klu'i phyag rgya zhes su 'grub//de nas sbyor bas
46b4(rgyud): mthong nas su//ji srid mgo dang snying khar son//'khrin pa sna'i sbyor ba dang //'gul pa g.yo pa nyid kyis so//'gyur bas rnam par 'gyur ba ste//len dang gtong ba'i cho ga rim//chos rnams
46b5(rgyud): thams cad blang ba dang //gdon gyi tshogs rnams bzlog pa ste/rnam 'gyur lan ni slar yang 'dir//sems kyi rnam par rtog pa'o//ji srid las ni rjes sbyor ba//sangs rgyas rnams kyi rjes su
46b6(rgyud): 'breng //sems dang yid kyi rnam sgyings nas//gdug pa thams cad bzlog par bya//lag pa rkang pa'i rim pa kun//rnam 'gyur gyi ni len yin no//'khrog pa rkug par shes bya ste/gtang ba dag ni dbrog
46b7(rgyud): ba'i lan//'grog pa mchog tu shes par bya//bgyegs kyi gnas ni 'or pa gnon pa'o//'khrog pa'i lan ni rab bzlog ste//sdigs pa'i rkang pa'i rgod par shes//bda' ni dran pa'i don yin te//phan
46b8(rgyud): tshun yongs su brjod pa yis//grub dang ma grub sbyor ba rnams//slob dpon slob ma la sogs pas bsgrub//bda' lan lha mo rnams kyis ni//bskul ba so sor bskul ba'o//bri ba smra dang bri ba ste/mthong
47a1(rgyud): @#//nas bri ba 'dri ba'i lan //legs pa'i gzugs mthong lta ba ste//lha'i ye shes spyod yul lo//lta ba'i lan ni de la slar //sbyor ba mchog gis sbyar ba'o//lha dang lha mo rnams
47a2(rgyud): kyis ni //g.yas lta ba lta ba ste//ji snyed lta ba'i sbyor ba yis//bsgrub par byed ba lan yin no//dam tshig sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//bka' brtags byas pa nyid du 'dod //gang phyir thos pa 'dzin
47a3(rgyud): pa'i //bsgrub pa lan du shes par bya//dkyil 'khor lus su bshad pa ste//ye shes lnga yis byin rlabs pa'o//dkyil 'khor la ni lha mo rnams//gcig tu byed pa'i dam pa'o//gsang ba
47a4(rgyud): sbas pa'i bshad pa ste//gsang ba'i lan ni legs basbas pa'o//gsang chen sems kyi 'du shes te//bde ba'i rnam par rtogs pa'o//so sor rtog pa lha mo dang //lha'i bde ba bde
47a5(rgyud): chen po//sangs rgyas sras bcas thams cad kyi//gsang chen lan du brjod pa'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang pos bka' stsal to//de nas de rnams slar 'dus par gyur te//

48a1(rgyud): //bcas rjesu chags //de las rdo rje'i gdong pa sbyar //yang dang yang du dgrol bar bya/'di yis las rnams thams cad kyi//byed 'gyur 'di la gdon mi za //de nyid snying khar khu tshur te //rdo rje

49a1(rgyud): @#//'dus pa rdo rje'i sbyor ba ldan pas legs brton te//rdo rje' bdag nyid ngo bor mdzad pa'i mdzes rdo rje//dpal gyi rin chen rin chen 'byung gnas sprin //rin chen rnams la rin chen gyis dges
49a2(rgyud): rin chen mdzes//mdzes pa'i brjid ldan rin chen rgyal mtshan gyis//skyo bo rnams kyi dngos dang sdig pa rnam bcom nas//dpal gyi pad ma pad ma'i bla ma pad sems dpa'//pad ma'i rab dges pad

49a6(rgyud): rgyas ye shes khyod la 'dud //sangs rgyas bzi bzing khyod la 'dud//sangs rgyas dpa'o khyod la 'dud //rdo rje khro bo de las 'khrungs //sdig pa bcom pa khyod phyag 'tshal //phyag na rin chen khyod la

49a8(rgyud): 'dod chags chags pa khyod la 'dud //'dod chags bral la phyag 'tshal 'dud //sna tshogs rdo rje khyod la 'dud //sna tshogs sbyor ba khyod phyag 'tshal //sna tshogs tshul can khyod la 'dud //sna tsho gas
49b1(rgyud): bde ldan phyag 'tshal 'dud //sna tshogs bde ldan phyag 'tshal 'dud//stong ba'i rgyal po 'di yis ni//chos dang ldan pa thams cad du //nyin re bzhin ni stong byed na//bsams pa'i dngos grub thob par

61b1(rgyud): bstan pa las /gang dag 'o ma'i thigs pa 'od zer bcas par gyur pa dang /de nas 'od zer gyi 'phreng ba las /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku rnam par phyung ste /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku de rnams //

104a1(rgyud): //phyag rgya 'bad pas bri bar bya//rdo rje pa nang sha dang ni//dril bu rab tu mdzes pa dang //dbyug gu dang ni tho ba dang //dbyug pa rdo rje mtshon pa dang //lcags sgrog dang ni rtse gsum dang //'di

108b2(rgyud): pa'i don dang/skye ba dang/rga ba dang /na ba dang/'chi ba'i lam las 'da' ba'i don du 'gyur ro/ma/chos thams cad ma byung ste/rdo rje'i gnas 'dis 'gog pa la 'jug pa'i don dang/chos thams cad 'gog
108b3(rgyud): pa'i don du 'gyuro//kham 'di la rnam gnas rnal 'byor pa/bdag gi de nyid rab rtogs te/bdag gi de nyid rab rdzogs nas/sangs rgyas kun mtshungs mnyam med 'gyur/de la sogs pa'i dngos
108b4(rgyud): grub thams cad dang/phun sum tshogs pa thams cad dang/gter thams cad dang/'khor lo thams cad dang/rdo rje thams cad dang/ral gri thams cad dang/yid bzhin gyi nor bu dang/dngul chu dang
108b5(rgyud): bcud len rnams dang /gyen du 'gro ba dang/rkang 'og dang/mi snang ba dang/rig pa 'dzin pa dang/'khor lo sgyur ba nyid dang/brgya byin dang tshangs pa dang/khyab 'jug dang/dbang phyug chen po
108b6(rgyud): dang/byang chub sems dpa'i sa rnams nges myur du 'grub par 'gyuro//yid ni rtse gcig gyur pa yis //sangs rgyas rjesu dran sgos nas ni /nyi ma'i dkyil dang khor dbus gnas par/sangs gya
108b7(rgyud): sangs rgyas sku gzugs sgom par bya/sbyor ba de'i ka drin gis/sangs rgyas kun mtshungasamanyam med 'gyur/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus par gnas par /'od kyi dkyil 'khor rab gsal
108b8(rgyud): ba'i kun tu bzang po phyag rgya che/legs par mnyam par gzhag par sgoms /lo ni phyed kyi skyon ba yis/kun bzang po 'dra bar 'gyur //rdo rje rin chen phyag rgya che/legs par ma nyam
109a1(rgyud): @#//par gzhag par sgom/rgyal po'i dbang skur nyid dang ni/yang na dbang phyug thob par 'gyur /rdo rje chos kyi rgya phyag rgya che/de ni rang gis rtan par sgom/'jig rten kun gi dbang phyug kyang/thob

109a4(rgyud): gzi brjid dra ba phyag rgya che/dam pa'i don du sgom par bya/gsang ba'i rgyal po thob par 'gyur /dngos grub gzhan lta ci smos/bdugs bkar gtsug gtor chen po ni/dam pa'i don du sgom bar bya
109a5(rgyud): gtsug tor ye shes thob 'gyur na/dngos grub gzhan/lta ci smos//rgyal ba'i gtsug tor phyag rgya che/de ni yang dang yang du sgom/chos kyi rgyal po rnam rgyal nyid/mdor sdus pa ni thob par 'gyur/rnam

109a7(rgyud): mnyam dang mi mnyam pa/gtsug tor rnams kyis legs par bshad/phyag rgya de rnams bdag nyid mchog/de ltar rnal 'byor sapas sgoms na/lhag pa'i lha ni gcig bu nyid/thog ma tha ma med

110b2(rgyud): o~M o~M/dag rnam par spyod pa ni ngag gi spyod pa'i chos kyi dbyings rnam par dag pa'i phyir chos thams cad kyisgor lta bar bya'o//o~M ba~M/cha zhes bya ba ni grib ma mi dmigs shing rang bzhin gis chos thams

110b6(rgyud): chos thams cad kyi sgor lta bar bya'o//oM pha~M/chos thams cad nye bar zhi ba'i phyir chos thams cad kyi sgor rnam par gzhag go/o~M o~M rgyun du byed pa ni/nges par 'byung ba'i ni don du ma 'phags
110b7(rgyud): pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa nye bar gzhag pa'i phyir chos thams cad kyi sgror lta bar bya'o//o~M ya~M/de kho na nyid ni spros pa thams cad rnam par spangs pa'i phyir chos thams cad ki sgor rnam par gzhag go//

111a1(rgyud): //bar bya'o //o~M hri /kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa mtshan nyid rnam par dag pa'i phyir chos thams cad kyi sgor lta bar bya'o//a ka kha ga ga dang //rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba'i
111a2(rgyud): mtshan nyid bzung ba'i phyir/chos thams cad kyi sgor gnas so//o~M tsa tsha dzra dz+nya nya//snang ba ni gsal zhing 'bar ba'i phyir chos thams cad kyi sga'o//o~M Ta tha Ta D+ha ta/sgom pa ni chos thams cad

114a2(rgyud): cad chos kyi dbyings kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//ngo bo nyid med pas chos thams cad stong pa nyid kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'i//'tshe ba med pas chos thams cad bdag med pa'i
114a3(rgyud): phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//skad cig nyid pas chos thams cad mi rtag pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o //skye ba dang /rga ba dang/'chi ba'i mtha' dang ldan pas//chos thams
114a4(rgyud): cad sdug sngal gi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o/ /sngon gyi mtha' dang phyi ma'i de bzhin nyid pas chos thams cad de bzhin nyid kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btang pa'o//gdod
114a5(rgyud): ma nas yongasu dag pas//chos thams cad yang dag mtha'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'//'dus byas dang 'dus (ma)byas mnyam pa nyid pas chos thams cad chen po'o//
114a6(rgyud): stong nyid la kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab 'apa'o//spros pa'i dmigs pa rnam par bral bas chos thams cad mtshan med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'i//re pa thams cad las rnam
114a7(rgyud): par grol bas chos thams cad smon pa med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas (btab pa'o/)btab pa'o//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis mtshan nyid gyis ni//chos thams cad mi rtag pa'i+i phyag rgyas
114b1(rgyud): rgyas btab pa'o//phung po lnga yongasu 'dus pa'i mtshan nyid kyis ni chos thams cad sdug bsngal gyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//rang bzhin gyis bdag med pa'i chos thams cad med
114b2(rgyud): pa'i phyag rgyas sa rgyas btab pa'o//shin du mi g.yo pa'i phyir chos thams cad zhi ba'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o/don dam pa'i bden pas yongasu khyab pa'i phyir chos thams cadabaden pa'i phyag
114b3(rgyud): rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//'tsho ba rnam par mi gnas pa'i phyir chos thams cad dri ma med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa 'o//shin du gdon mi za bar gnas pa'i phyig//chos thams cad
114b4(rgyud): 'khrul pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o//sngon gyi mtha' dang phyi ma'i mtha' cad pa'i phyir chos thams cad/ji lta ba bzhin gyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab la'o//mi rnams kyi gzhi
114b5(rgyud): med pa'i phyir chos thams can kyis sgo 'dogs pa'i phyir chos thams cang phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//byed pa pos byas pa'i phyir chos thams cada'ajig pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabapa'o//rang
114b6(rgyud): bzhin gyi ma byung ba'i phyir chos thams cad 'byung ba med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas gtab pa'o//rang bzhin gyis ma skyes pa'i phyir chos thams cad'gog pa med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas pa btab pa'o//'gag
114b7(rgyud): pa med pa'i phyir chos thams cag gdod ma nas skye ba ba med pa'i phyag rgyas rgya btab pa'o//rnam par spyod kun du spyod pa thams cad dang bral ba'i phyir//chos thams cad spros pa med pa'i
114b8(rgyud): phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//gzugs rnam par lta ba med pa'i phyir chos thamasacad mtshan nyid med pa'i phyir phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'oganas yongasu bcad pa'i phyir chos
115a1(rgyud): @#//thams cad nye bar sbyong ba med pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//mi mthun pa'i phyogs nye bar ma dmigs pa'i phyir chos thams cad bkur mi 'debs pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba'o//
115a2(rgyud): mngon 'du byed pa thams cad med pa'i phyir chos thams cad las kyi rnam par smin pa med pa'i phyir phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//skye ba dang gnas pa las rnam par grol ba'i phyir chos thams cad 'dus
115a3(rgyud): ma byas pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabapa'o//namakha' dang khyad par med pa'i phyir chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o //mtshan nyid gcig pa'i phyir chos thams cad gzugs
115a4(rgyud): dang tshor ba dang 'du shes dang 'du byed dang /rnam par shes pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btabs pa'o//nyon mongs pa med pa'i phyir chos thams chad kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab:pa'o//nyon mongs
115a5(rgyud): pa dang bcas pa med pa'i phyir chos thams cad khams dang skye mched phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku dang mnyam nyid thob pas chos thams cad khams dang
115a6(rgyud): skye mchod kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btabapa'o //mtha' gcig du 'jig rten gi khams rnam par dag pa'i phyir chos thams cang 'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//lha'i rjes
115a7(rgyud): su byed pa'i phyir chos thams cad phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//mtha' gcig tu chos la phan 'dogs pa'i phyir chos thams cadakyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//thams cad kyi las sna
115a8(rgyud): tshogs byed pa'i phyir chos thamasacad las kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o //thams cad dam tshig rjes su sgrub pa'i phyir chos thams cad dam tshig gi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o/ /sangs rgyas
115b1(rgyud): kyi chos thams cad rab tu mi gnas pa'i phyir cho thams cad sangs rgyas kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//chos thams cad 'jig rten las 'das pa'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//theg pa
115b2(rgyud): chen po'i phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'i//bsang sngags kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//phyag rgya'i rgyas rgyas btab pa dang //dkyil 'khor gi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa dang//mnyam pa nyid kyi phyag
115b3(rgyud): rgyas rgyas btab pa dang //rdo rje ye shes kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab ba dang //thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi phyag pa rgyas btab pa dang /me long lta bu'i ye shes kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa dang//
115b4(rgyud): mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi phyag rgyas rgyas gdab pa dang/so sor rtog pa'i ye shes kyi phyag rgyas sa rgyas btab pa dang//bya ba grub pa'i ye shes kyi phyag rgyas rgyas btab pa dang //chos
115b5(rgyud): thams cad kyi chos dbyings rnam par dag pa'i ye shes kyi phyag rgya rgyas rgyas btab pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes phyag rgya'o/mu~M /'jam dpal phyag rgya'i tshig 'di 
115b6(rgyud): dag ni mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i ye shes kyi tshig//de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi byin gyis rlabs pa'i tshig/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes yongasu rdzogs par byed pa theg pa chen po'i
115b7(rgyud): tshig//sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi phyag rgya'i tshig 'di rnams la//'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i bcom ldan 'das rnams dang chos kyi phung po brgyad khri bzhi
115b8(rgyud): stong dang/byang chub kyi byung ba ma yin no//oM oM aHoM//sngags pas phyag rgyas btab na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi phyag rgya dang ldan pa'i hU~M//myur du ma skyes
116a1(rgyud): //pa'i chos rnams la bzod pa thob par 'gyur ro///de nas de ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sbyor ba thams cad kyi gsang ba chen po'i phyag rgya'i ye shesu 'gyur/na/snying gi
116a2(rgyud): dbus su brtag du sgoms//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus son par//'dab ma'i 'phreng bas 'khrigs pa'i 'od//pad ma chen po 'dab brgyad pa//'bar ba'i 'phreng
116a3(rgyud): ba'i 'od//de'i steng du rtag sgom pa//yi ge yi ni rtso bo mchog/thog ma tha ma med zhi ba//kha ba lang 'od 'dra 'od//dpa' bo kun mnga' zla ltar dkar//
116a4(rgyud): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ltar 'od gsal//zhi ba'i chos kyi rang bzhin sku//kun mkhyen ye shes gyis gang ba//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gnas kyi gsad//
116a5(rgyud): ra sa ya na dang mtshungs mchog//kha yig mkha' 'drar shes par bya//thig len des rnam par smras//gsang ba chen po mchog gi mdzes//thog ma tha
116a6(rgyud): ma med pa'i bdag//de'i steng du che rdo rje che//rtse mo lnga dang yang dag ldan//de'i steng du sgrol ma che//'bar ba'i 'phreng bas 'khrugs pa'i 'od//de'i steng du
116a7(rgyud): brtags bsam pa//yiga'i mchog gi go 'phang ngo//man ngag mchog las shes bya ste//gzhan du na ni nga mi smra//sangs rgyas mkhas pa thams cad kyis//
116a8(rgyud): mdor sdug sdus nas ni 'di gsungaso//rnam par grol ba gsum thob nas//tho ba 'gyur 'di ni gzhan du min//lce ni rkan la sbyor bar bya//sna'i rtse mor yi ge
116b1(rgyud): hUM //dbang po gnyis ni snyoms 'jug pas//phyag rgya'i go 'phang tshig gi ni//shes rab ldan pas rang seMs sgom /snang ba med cing spros med pa //shin du mchog tu phrar shes bya //
116b2(rgyud): khyab bdag bsod nams ye shes dngos //bsam bya bsam gnas gcig byas ste//mig dang sgron me'i 'od bzhin du kun tu 'bar ba'i dang ldan pa//mgrin pas 'gram pa brtsams nas
116b3(rgyud): ni//dkar ser po sngon po 'i 'od//dmar po yang ni thams cad du //'bar ba'i 'di dag 'bad pa yis//sgrub po dam pas mtshon par byed//snying khar gsal bar byas
116b4(rgyud): nas su//de bzhin thams cad spyo bos sod//gnas de la ni mthong nas su //dbang po thams cad dga' bar bya//de las mthong nas 'jig rten ni//
116b5(rgyud): mtshungs par mthong nas thams cad du //slar yang de ru 'ongs nas ni//de'i 'jug par rab tu bsam//yid kyis de lngar sgoms nas ni//rang nyid 'khor lo
116b6(rgyud): sgyur lta bus //dbang po lngas ni dmigs na yang //mi rnams lus ni dag par 'gyur //ji ste bud med yon tan dang //ldan pa kun tu gnas pa ni//kun nas bcug ste bcings
116b7(rgyud): nas su /zhi ba'i chos kyi rim pa ni//shin tu mnyam par dbang du byas te sgrib par bya //de rnams lhan cig yongs sten na //sngags pas mchog gi dngos grub thob//bsang sngags
116b8(rgyud): mchog tu dges pa rnams //shes rab pha rol phyin pa che//zhi ba'i chos kyi rim pa ni //shin du mnyam par gzhag pa sgom//rdo rje sems dpa'i dngos grub gang //de grub gzhan lta smos ci dgos
117a1(rgyud): @#//mchog ni brtag par shes par bya//bar mas phun pa sus 'tshogs pa ste//stong pa nyid dang mtshan med mchog//smon pa med pa zhes ni grags//chos kun bdag med pa'i
117a2(rgyud): mchog//kun mkhyen ye shes rdzogs par byed//sa bcu yi ni stobs bdag gis//pha rol phyin pa thams cad thob//rdo rje sems dpa' mchog sgoms na//rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra bar 'gyur//de nyid
117a3(rgyud): 'di ni bcud kyis len//gnyis su med pa'i ye shes mchog//theg chen mchog tu yid 'ong ba//stong nyid sgom pa'i bdag nyid can//gnyis su med pa'i yeshes mchog//gang gis rtag
117a4(rgyud): tu sgom byed na//de 'ang rdo rje 'dzin rgyal po//yon tan mchog las skyes par 'gyur//bud med gang zhig sngo sdangs ma//brgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par rgyan//lus ngag
117a5(rgyud): yid kyi sbyor yis//yongs reg i la ngag tsum pa//de'i tshe ni sngags pas rten//mnyam med gsang ba'i ye shes mtshungs//bud med gang zhig kha dog dkar//
117a6(rgyud): pad ma'i 'dab ma 'dra ba'o//de dang lhan cig sngags pas sdang//dam pa'i dngos grub thob par 'gyur//bu mo gang zhig kha dog dmar//ud pala'i brgyan 'tshang ma//sngags pas de dang
117a7(rgyud): lhan cig gnas//shes rab pha rol phyin pa mtshungs 'gyur//bud med i 'dra ni la 'tshungs//gsang ba rdo rje'i rnal 'byor gis//sngags pa mchog shes pas brten//rigs 'dzin rnams
117a8(rgyud): kyis stobs chen 'gyur//gsang chen ma ra ka ta mtshungs//gang gis rtag tu bsten pa ni//de 'ang rdo rje 'dzin rgyal po//myur du gsang ba'i dam par 'gyur//bud med pad mar ga 'dra//
117b1(rgyud): brgyan rnams kun gyis rgyan//dbang po rnams ni snyoms 'jug pas //rdo rje 'dzin pa lta bu sgrub//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//gnyis su med pa'i ye shes mchog //gang gis rtag
117b2(rgyud): tu sgom pa ni//mnyam med sangs rgyas kun dang 'tshungs//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi//mchog gi gnas gyis sgoms na ni//rnal 'byor mchog gis mi ring bar//rdo rje 'dzin par lta
117b3(rgyud): bur 'gyur//yang dag spong ba rnam pa bzhi//legs par mnyam par gzhag pas sgoms//dbang po gnyis ni snyoms 'jug pas//mnyam med 'jig rten dbang phyug 'tshungs //
117b4(rgyud): so so dbang dag rig pa bzhi//shin du mnyam bzhag sgoms na ni//zhen med rdo rje sangs rgyas 'tshungs //mnyam med 'jam pa'i mchog dang 'dra//mi 'jigs pa ni rnam pa bzhi
117b5(rgyud): rdo rje'i sbyor rnam sgom par bya//dbang po gnyis snyoms 'jug pas//rdo rje 'dzin pa lta buste//rdzu 'phrul gyi ni rkang pa bzhi//shin du mnyam gzhag sgoms nas ni//dbang po gnyis ni mnyam
117b6(rgyud): sbyor bas//rdzu 'phrul ldan pa'i mchog tu 'gyur//tshangs pa yi ni gnas bzhi po//tshad med pa'i sems kyi ni//rdo rje sbyor ba bdag sgoms na//rdo rje 'dzin pa lta bu 'brub//sbyor ba'i bdag nyid dbang
117b7(rgyud): po lnga//dam pa'i ngan gis sgoms nas ni//dbang poganyis ni snyoms 'jug pas //mnyam med rdo rje sangs rgyas 'tshangs//yid kyis stobs ni rnam pa lnga//shin du mnyam gzhag
117b8(rgyud): sgoms nas ni//'khor lo sgyur ba bca brtson 'grus che//stobs 'dzin lta bu nyid du 'gyur//byang chub yan lag rin chen bdun//gsang sngags sgom pas sgoms nas ni//rtog pa ye
118a1(rgyud): @#//shes sbyor ba yi //khyab bdag chos kun 'dzin par 'gyur //'phags pa'i yan lag rgyad kyi lam//kun mkhyen ye shes rdzogs byed pa //de nas sgom pa tsam gis ni //'jig
118a2(rgyud): rten shes las rab tu 'ang //rjes su dran pa rnam pa drug //bdag nyid rtse gcig gis sgoms nas//sngags pas rdo rje 'dzin pa ru//sngags pas dus mi ring bar 'gyur //stobs bcu po ni
118a3(rgyud): yang dag sgoms //bsgoms pa'i rnal 'byor de nyid kyis//bsod nams ye shes yongs rdzogs te//rtag tu stobs bcu'i dam par 'gyur //stobs bcu poni yang dag sgoms//pha rol phyin
118a4(rgyud): bcu po ni yang dag sgoms //bsgom rnams dang ni dbang rnams dang //sa rnams kun ni nges skyed nas//myur du mtshan ma dag par 'gyur //bud med nu ma shin du snum //rje dar rje rang 
118a5(rgyud): de bzhin du//mchu yis mchu ni yongs su bzung //'das pas dam du a ling ka //rnam grol gos kyis sgribs nas nis//byang chub sems ni sgom par bya//rdo rje ye shes brtson 'grus
118a6(rgyud): che//rdo rje dbyings su rtag rtan pas//rnam pa sna tshogs gangar na //rnal 'byor shes pa des sten bya//sngar bzhin thams cad byas nas su//bud med rnams kyi nyer gnas snun //sbyor ba 'di ni
118a7(rgyud): mkhyen pa yis//stobs chen sangs rgyas rab rgyu 'grub //gsang ba 'di ni dam pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyis skyob pa'o//gsang ba 'di'i sbyor ba yis //yi ge dam pa 'grub par 'gyur //ma 'dres
118a8(rgyud): pa'i chos bco brgyad //sangs rgyas chos su grags pa ste//de rnams sgom pa dang ldan pas //sangs rgyas byang chub rab ster byed//bsam gtan bzhi po thob nas ni //gzugs med pa'i rtag par
118b1(rgyud): yang //'gog pa'i snyoms par 'jug ldan pas//sangs rgyas byang chub sgrub pa pos //byang chub phyogs ni sum bcu bdun //chos ni mchog tu rmad byung ba//de rnams sgom pa dang ldan pas //
118b2(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa'i skyob pa'o//sngags rnams thams cad rab grub cing //sbyor ba thams cad thams cad du'o//de nas 'bad pa thams cad kyis//sangs rgyas chos nyid sgom par bya//'jig rten 
118b3(rgyud): 'jig rten 'das pa'i lam //ye shes rin chen rab rnyed dka' //mchod dang dbang skur byin rlabs las//rnyed 'gyur gzhan du ma yin no//zhu zhing gsol ba bdab pa dang /bdag nyid dbul bar 
118b4(rgyud): de bzhin no//sangs rgyas sogs la yid rang zhing //de bzhin yongs su sngo ba dang //byang chub sems dpa' ni yang dag skyed //khyad par du ni kun du'o//thog ma bar dang phye ma red
118b5(rgyud): du//ji ltar bzhin du rjes sgoms pas//sangs rgyas thams cad sgrub byas te//'di la the tshom yod ma yin//'gro ba sems kyi spro ba na //brtan pa'i snying po legs par bshad
118b6(rgyud): stong nyid de bzhin nyid las byung //rdo rje sems dpa' de bshad do//'gro ba thams cad kug nas su//sangs rgyas byang chub sbyang bar bya//don yod ye shes dag pa ste//rdo rje rgyal po mchog ces bshad //
118b7(rgyud): rdo rje chags pas chags gyur nas //rang bzhin gyis ni rtan par 'gyur //'gro ba ma lus lus med pa//de'i sems ni chags pas gshad //chos rnams kun ni rang bzhin gyis//rnam par dben
118b8(rgyud): zhing gnyis su med//gang tshe dag pas bdag nyid mthong //de tshe rdo rje legs par bshad //rdo rje sems dpa' bzhin po'i//ming dang rda'i sbyor ba'o//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//sdom pa
119a1(rgyud): @#//mchog du dam pa'o//khams gsum pa'i rgyal srid che//tshogs gnyis rdzogs par bya ba ste//sa dang pha rol phyin mchog rtso//rdo rje dbang skur las byung ba//de bzhin gshegs
119a2(rgyud): kun ye shes te//rlog pa med cing yi ge med//'od kyi dkyil 'khor bzi brjid mchog//brtan pa rtag pa nyid du 'gyur//nam mkha'i mdzod ni mdzod chen po//chos rnams thams cad legs
119a3(rgyud): gnas pa//yid bzhin nor bu rdo rje che//rdo rje me tog ni bla na med//ye shes snang ba rin chen che//yongs su skye ba yongs su lta//de bzhin gshegs kun gsang ba ni//rdo rje bshad pa zhes su
119a4(rgyud): bshad//chos kyi bdag med las byung ba//gnyis su med pa'i ye shes mchog//chos nyid dam pa bsam du med//rdo rje chos zhes rab tu grags//rdo rje blo gros snang ba che//
119a5(rgyud): shes rab ye shes dri'i mchog//zag med shes rab chen po pa//de ni rdo rje rnon por bshad//sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos kyi 'khor lo//gnyis su med las byung bar mtshon//
119a6(rgyud): yan lag bcu gnyis rten 'brel cing//rdo rje rgyu zhes nges par bshad//dgongs pa'i skad ni spros pa'i mchog//yul rnams kyi ni bshad pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi bzlas pa che//de ni rdo rje smra bar
119a7(rgyud): bshad//sems can kun gyi don la sbyor//sangs rgyas kun gyi mchod pa che//gdod nas dag pa'i rnal 'byor che//de ni rdo rje las su bshad//bla na med pa'i byams pa che//srung ba'i bya ba gang na
119a8(rgyud): med//dag pa'i chos ni brtson chen po//rdo rje srung ba zhes su grags//shes rab thabs kyi khro bo che//sangs rgyas kun dang rjes su 'thun//nyon mongs thams cad zla ba che//de ni gnod
119b1(rgyud): sbyin chen po brjod //lus ngag yid ni yang dag sbyor //sgrib pa med cing yi ge bral//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi bsang //de ni rdo rje 'tshams sbyor bshad //rdo rje ye shes rnal 'byor che //
119b2(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi stobs po che//thog ma tha ma med zhi ba //rig pa mchog ces bya bar bshad //de nas yang 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad legs 
119b3(rgyud): par mchod nas klu dbyangs pa//oM sarba b+hud d+ha pa ra me shwar b+hud+ha b+hud+h+ya /la li hi su ga b+ha ga su ra ta ma ha /A di ma hA bo d+hi b+hud d+ha b+hud d+ha/yo gI sha ra ta tha bi ta da ma ka ta thA ga ta /byang chub 
119b4(rgyud): sems skyed las 'khrungs pas //sangs rgyas kun la phyag 'tshal lo//mchog gi dbang phyug dam pa'i chos//gdad nas zhi ba la phyag 'tshal //byang chub sems dpa' skyob 
119b5(rgyud): pa po //rdo rje can kun la phyag 'tshal //de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje che//sangs rgyas byang chub la phyag 'tshal//mi dmigs go 'phang la gnas pa //mtham las 'das pa 
119b6(rgyud): la phyag 'tshal //'dren pa rnams kyi stobs po che//hU~M gi yi ge ma lus pa //thams cad mkhyen pa mtha' dag dang//shin du yongs su mya ngan 'das //'jig rten dbang phyug mgon bzang po
119b7(rgyud): rdo rje ye shes dri ma med //rnam par dag pa rdo rje mchog //dngos grub thams cad rab gsol byed //rnal 'byor dbang phyug rnams kyi mchog //dus gsum gsum pa dbang gyur pa//sems 
119b8(rgyud): can rnams la phan don du//khyab pa khyod la phyag 'tshal 'dud//rdo rje snying po rgyan las dkyil 'khor chen po yongasu rdzogas+h+yo////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs
120a1(rgyud): @#//pa rnams slar yang 'dus par gyur nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs kar zhugs te/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po rdo rje snying bo'i rgyan
