256a7(rgyud): 'jigs pa chen po'i dus dang 'dra//ud pal sngon po'i 'dab ma bzhin//de'i kha dog rnam par bsam//drag po chen po zhal zhil pa//'bar ba'i 'phreng ba 'khrigs pa'i 'od//spyan gsum pa la 'khro
256a8(rgyud): gnyer bster//phyag drug pa la mchog tu 'bar//me'i khams dang 'dra ba'i mdog/le tri'i mdog ltar shin tu dmar//de'i khro gnyer bster ba yis//bdug pa mdul mdzad 'dzin par mdzad//khro bo'i
256b1(rgyud): rgyal po gnyis pa ni//yang dang yang du bsam par bya//ma lus sa rnams thams cad du//gson po'i bdag nyid chen po des//'gugs pa dang ni dbang byed na//skye bo gzhan lta smos ci dgos//khro bo gzigs pa'i
256b2(rgyud): thabasgrubs pa'i thabs so//de nyid bcom ldan rnal 'byor te//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug gyur//rta 'brin zhes su grags pa yin//rtag tu bka'i mdog 'dra ba//rgya skyeg+sa
256b3(rgyud): 'dog 'dra dmar ba po//chu dang gzi brjid can reg pa//me'i dkyil 'khor dag dang ldan//'dod ldan rnams la 'dod pa 'byung//bdug pa can rnams bdug pa 'byung//zhi ba la yang zhi ba 'byung//stobs ldan
256b4(rgyud): rnams la stobs kyis sbyor//zhal bzhi pa la phyag bzhi pa//bsam gtan bzhi dang mnyam par ldan//phyag bzhi pa la brtson 'grus che//seng ge ltar gnas mche ba bzhi//rta 'grin rgyal po chen po ni/
256b5(rgyud): dam pa'i dbang phyug sgrub bya ba//skad cig nyid la gnas nas ni//thams cad lhang sgrub par bya//sngags ni oM a mri tod b+ha bod b+ha be hU~M phaT/oM a mri tod b+ha ba hU~M phaT/g.yas nas g.yon
256b6(rgyud): gyi sbyor ba yis//g.yon rkyang ba ni rab tu sbyor//khro bo'i dkyil bkrung bcas nas ni//'jig rten thams cad rab tu gnyes//pad ma'i dbang phyug rgyal po 'dis//sa'i dbang phyug kun gyi mchog/rta 'grin
256b7(rgyud): pa ni stobs po che//stobs kyi sbyor ba sgrub par bya//dbang phyug chen po laswogs pa//tshogs kyi bdag po gzhan dag dang//mi dang bdug pa'i bsam pa can//sa rnams thams cad 'grub par byed//
256b8(rgyud): rta 'grin gyi sgrub pa'i thabs so//de nyid bcom ldan rnal 'byor te//gcig pu nyid ni lhag pa'i lha//shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid 'gyur//khro bo mchog tu mi 'gyur ba//kun sbyor bdag gi rnal 'byor gyis//
257a1(rgyud): @#//thugs rjes 'gro ba'i dbang du song //de nas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//sna'i rtse mor yid kyis ni//g.yo ba med par bsam par bya//slob dpon mi g.yo khro bo che//de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
257a2(rgyud): cad brtan//ral gri zhags pa 'dzin par spang //thog ma tha ma med pa'i mchog/pri yang kun ltar sngo ba'i gzugs//'khregs shing byis pa lta bur gnas//ma lus pa'i sa rnams kun//chos dbyings mnyam
257a3(rgyud): dang mi mnyam pa//khro bo lnga pa dran dang ni//pho nyas kyang ni rnam par brgyan//skye bo kun gyis yongs su gang //sangs rgyas kun gyi spyan snga na//ka lung yang ni yongs su rtsol//sngags ni na ma/man+ta badzra Na tsan+Ta maha 
257a4(rgyud): ro sha Na s+ya bA tra hU~M Ta ka haM maM//khro bo gzi brjid chen po 'di//gzugs mang mnga' ba stobs po che//'gro ba thams cad 'gugs par byed//dbang du yang ni mdzad par gnas//rlung dang me 
257a5(rgyud): las yang dag byung //'bar ba lta bu'i gzi brjid can//sngags dang phyag rgya swogs kun mchog/lus dang ngag dang yid bzhin no//dgug dang bshugs dang bcing ba dang //dbang du bya ba sgrub par 
257a6(rgyud): byed//de laswogs pa mtha' yas mchog/namakha'i khams kyi mthas blas mchog/khro bo'i phyag rgya rang 'byung ba//drag po thog ma med par 'phro ba bzhin du gnas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis 
257a7(rgyud): rdo rje snying po rgyan gyi rgyud las////khro bo'i dkyil 'khor 'di yongs su rdzogs s+ho////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad slar yang 'dus te gshegs
257a8(rgyud): nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhIn gshegs pa thams cad kyi gzungs thams cad kyi dbang phyug gi rgyal po bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba btab pa/bcom ldan 'das bzungs rnams thams cad kyi dkyil 
257b1(rgyud): 'khor bskyed par mdzad du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis gzungs kyi dkyil 'khor bka' stsal pa//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//gzungs rnams kyi ni dkyil 'khor dge/gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa 
257b2(rgyud): rta babs kyis ni rnam par mdzes//thig bzhi dang ni yang dag ldan//dkyil 'khor dam pa rab tu bshad//de'i dbus su pad chen po//'o ma kun gyis shin tu mdzes//nyi shu rtsa lnga'i 'dab ma ldan//rdo rje rin 
257b3(rgyud): chen legs par brgyan//de'i dbus su rtag bri ba//dpag med shes rab pha rol phyin//de'i nye 'khor thams cad du//sna tshogs las ni 'bar rnams la//nyi shu rtsa lnga dbab pa yis//shes rab pha rol phyin 
257b4(rgyud): ma 'bri//shar gyi 'dab nas brtsams te//thog mtha' nyid kyang rtag par bya//phyag rgya'i yi ge ci rigs par//go rims bzhin du rnam par dgod//phyogs cha'i dbye ba tha dad kyis//kha dog dbye ba thams cad du
257b5(rgyud): rnam dbye mchog tu sna tshogs pa//chos nyid mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//de las 'od zer bsam mi khyab//sngo ser la sogs dbye ba yis//shes rab snang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis//'gro ba 
257b6(rgyud): thams cad gsal bar bya//'dus par gyur nas de dag kun//lha nyid kyi ni gzugs ji bzhin//shes rab chen po gzugs chen po//'bar bcas mdzes pa'i gzi brjid can//de'i lte ba'i dkyil 
257b7(rgyud): 'khor du//sngags kyi dkyil 'khor kun nas rten//sku ni gser gyi mdog dang 'dra//brgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par brgyan//rang gi ting nge 'dzin la bzhugs//du ma yi ni gzugs ldan ma//shes rab 
257b8(rgyud): pha rol phyin ma ni//sems can khams ni rnam sbyong mdzad//kun nas sems can rnams kyi yum//sems can kun gyi thams cad du//thams cad lus la rnam par khyab//sems kyi rgyu bzhin 
258a1(rgyud): @#//legs par zhugs//zag pa med cing bsam mi khyab//dri ma med cing rang gis stong//namakha' dang ni mtshungs pa 'am//dngos dang dngos med rnam par spangs//thog ma tha ma med cing zhi
258a2(rgyud): chos rnams kun gyi dbang phyug sku//sdig pa kun las rnam par grol//bden pa gnyis la rnam par gnas//dam pa dang ni dam pa'i don//chos kyi sku ru de bshad do//dbang po kun kyang rnam par
258a3(rgyud): shes//sems dang thams cad thams cad du//yid kyi rnam shes zhes su bshad//shes rab blo dang blo gros dran//lhag mthong dang ni ye shes kun//kun nas nyon mongs mi mthun phyogs//rtag dang 
258a4(rgyud): chad pa'i mtha' dang ldan//mtha' gnyis po ni rnam par spangs///kun rdzob dang ni dam pa'i don/rnam pa nyid de rab rtags pa//ji ltar bud shing la rten nas//me'i sa bon gnas pa dang//
258a5(rgyud): til la til mar ji bzhin no//bur shing ro bzhin rab tu brtag/me tog dri ni ji bzhin nam//zho la ma gsar ji bzhin du//rnal 'byor pa'i ye shes sgrub//bdud rtsi lta bu'i snying po 'byung//
258a6(rgyud): rtsub shing dang ni rtsub stan las//ji bzhin rtsub pas me 'byung ste//de bzhin ye shes rnal 'byor pas//sgrub pas yongs su rtag par bya//shes bya'i dkyil 'khor thams cad la//yang nas yang du
258a7(rgyud): srung bar bya//mun pa dang thams cad rnam sel ba'i//nyi ma'i 'od zer ji bzhin bdag/namakha'i dri ma med rten nas//nyi ma zla ba ji bzhin gnas//'char ba yis ni gsal byed pa//'bar ba stong
258a8(rgyud): phrag gi ni gnod//mun pa na 'joms skar ma la//kun nas kyang ni mdzes pa yin//rnal 'byor pas ni ji ltar sems//mkha' bzhin shin tu dri ma med//gzung 'dzin las ni nges par grol//
258b1(rgyud): mnyam dang mi mnyam dngos po kun//der gnas rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis//ye shes nyi zla mtshungs pa yi//shes rab pha rol phyin sgoms te//gsang sngags 'di ni bsam par bya//rang bzhin gyis 
258b2(rgyud): ni dag pa'i dngos//rang bzhin med par gnas//rang bzhin gyis ni 'od gsal kun//gdod nas ma skyes rnyog pa med//yi ge ri de nyid de lta bu//gnas dang mi gnas rnam par spangs//sde tshan bzhi 
258b3(rgyud): pa'i gnyis pa//la thig le sgra yis rnam par brgyan//de nyid bcom ldan mgon po ste//shes rab pha rol phyin mar bshad//oM badzra swahA/oM swahA/oM shru ti mri bi tsa ye swahA//sangs rgyas rnams 
258b4(rgyud): kyi shes rab 'dis//bsam pa thams cad rdzogs par byed//'di ni sangs rgyas rnams kyi yum//de nyid 'khor lo rnam par bsgyur//'dis ni dngos grub thams cad dang //grub pa nyid ni byed pa 
258b5(rgyud): gnas//bstan bcos rab tu 'chad byed dang //shes bya thaMs cad khyad par du//thams cad mkhyen pa'i chos rnams dbang phyug dam pas shes par 'gyur//shes rab kyi pha rol du 
258b6(rgyud): phyin pa'i sgrub thabs so//shes rab pha rol phyin gnyis pa//rdzu 'phrul chen po mkha' dang mnyam//gser gyi mdog can 'bar ba can//zla ba'i ba dan la legs par bzhugs//brtson 'grus 
258b7(rgyud): chen mo bsam gtan bya//sngags kyi yi ge rnam par brgyan//mchog tu rnal 'byor ma rnams kyis//sangs rgyas zhes bya btags pa yis//de'i gsang sngags rab bshad bya//nyon cig sa rnams 'dod 
258b8(rgyud): pa rnams//sngags ni/oM ru ru sU ru dza la ti Sh+tha sid d+ho lo tsa ne sarba ar+tha sar d+ha ni/swahA//'di de mchog tu bsang ba ni//rnal 'byor ma la mchog tu bshad//dam chen yi ge'i mthar thug pa//
259a1(rgyud): @#//bsgoms pas dge ba rab tu ster//chos kyi phyag rgya 'di bzhin du//sems can thams cad dbang du byed//dpung dang bcas pa myur bkug ste//dran du gyur te nye bar gnas//rol cig
259a2(rgyud): g.yo' ba'i zhal mnga' ba//sgyeg pa laswogs nyams dang ldan//rol cig snang ba'i gar mdzad pa'i//sangs rgyas spyan ni 'grub par 'gyur//mig gi phyag rgya rab tu gsal//mig gi phyag rgya 'di
259a3(rgyud): bzhin du//'jig rten gsum kyang gar byed do//sangs rgyas spyan gyi phyag rgya'i bsgrub pa'i thabs so//chu'i dkyil 'khor dges pa na//bcud kyis len dang mtshungs pa mchog/mtha' yas rgyas shing
259a4(rgyud): spangs pa yi//dri ma med pa'i mkha' la gnas//dkyil 'khor de'i dbus su ni//lha mo mchog tu mdzes pa mo//'dam bu mda' rgyu mdog ltar dkar//grub pa ral pa'i gcod pan 'chang//
259a5(rgyud): 'gro ba mtha' dag snang mdzad ma//rtag tu namakha' bzhin du gnas/sngags ni a tre bi ga tra ka tre swahA//gos dkar mo'i phyag rgya che//dung dang kun da zla ba 'dra//sngags kyi yi ge 'phreng ba che//pad
259a6(rgyud): ma'i rgyal po rab tu gnas//'gro ba mtha' dag snang 'dzad na//sgrub pa pos na sgrub par bya//dam pa'i lha mo 'grub par 'gyur//dam pa'i dkyil 'khor sgrub pa'i thabs so//zhi ba'i yi ge mnyam par
259a7(rgyud): ldan//zhi bar mdzad cing 'jig rten gyi//sa gsum sgrub par byed pa'i thabs//gos dkar ma'i sgrub pa'i thabs so//sngo sangs dkar mo sna tshogs gzugs//rnam pa sna tshogs rgyan gyis brgyan/
259a8(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//sgyeg pa'i nyams kyis yongs gang ba//'gro ba mtha' dag skyob mdzad ma//thugs rje'i thugs dang ldan pa can//rab tu 'bad de sgrub par bya//rol ba yi ni
259b1(rgyud): yi ges mchod//sngags ni oM ta re tu re swahA//chos kyi phyag rgya mtha' yas mchog/sgrol mas legs par rnam par gnas//gsang chen bzhin du thams cad la//bcud kyi len dang 'dra ba'i mchog/kun tu

259b3(rgyud): pa'i gcod pan dag gis brgyan//'dab bu mda' rgyu'i mdog ltar dkar//dpa' ba la swogs nyams kyis brgyan//brtson 'grus chen mo 'jigs sel ma//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//rab
259b4(rgyud): tu 'bar ba'i me bzhin du//shin tu gsal ba'i gzi brjid can//hUM gi yi ge zab mo'i sgra//sa gsum po ni 'phrul par mdzad//khro gnyer can zhes legs par grags//sngags kyi phreng bas rnam par
259b5(rgyud): brgyan//sngags ni oM b+ha na ya sa ni tra sa ni//tra sa ni tra sa tra sa ya b+hi ku tri T+Ta be ta Ti bI re Ta shwe te shI ta dze Ta ni/oM tra ka ki hUM swahA//bsang sngags 'di ni brtson 'grus che//gsang sngags kun
259b6(rgyud): gi bdag nyid mchog/'gro ba kun kyang mgu byed na//bdug pa'i sems lta ci smos ci dgos//khro gnyer sgrub pa'i thabs so//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnasapar//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor rdo rje
259b7(rgyud): ldan//rdo rje'i phreng bas yongs su skor//gzi brjid dkyil 'khor kun nas bkris//der gnas rtag tu lha mo ni//stobs chen pri yang kun ltar ljang//phyag bzhi pa la de bsam par bya//rgyan rnams kun gyis
259b8(rgyud): rnam par brgyan//rnal 'byor ma rnams cher zhi ba//dngos grub thams cad rab rtsol cig/sngags kyi ye shes 'phreng ba che//rdo rje'i dbus song rab tu gnas//'khro bo hUM mdzad gang ba'i mtha'//
260a1(rgyud): @#//dga' ba'i nyams laswogs pa ldan//lha mo 'jig rten gsum rmongs//dngos grub chen mo rab rtsol ma//skad cig tsam gyis 'gro ba kun//nges par dbang du byed par 'gyur//rdo rje'i rlung gi

260a3(rgyud): lha mo 'jig rten gsum gis mchod//sngags ni oM ku la d+ha dI ya na ma/shaM ka re sha shan ti ka re/g+hu TA g+hu TA/g+hu TI ni/g+ha ta ya g+hu TI ni swAhA/oM ku lan d+ha ri/b+han d+ha b+ha d+ha hUM phaT//bsang sngags 'di 
260a4(rgyud): ni don gyi mchog/sangs rgyas kun gyis gsungs pa ni///sa gsum po ni 'gugs byed cing //tshangs pa laswogs lha rnams kun//'gro ba thams cad 'gugs byed la//rang gi dbang

260a6(rgyud): las bdag nyid ni//rdo rje lugu rgyud kyi sku//phyag bzhi pa la lha mo de//rdo rje yi ni rgyan gyis brgyan//dmar po 'bar ba de yis ni//sa gsum po ni 'ching bar mdzad//tshangs pa khyab 'jug dbang 
260a7(rgyud): phyug che//bkug nas dbang du mdzad par gnas//dngos grub thams cad 'dzin pa dang //de'i thugs kar rdo rje che//'bar ba'i 'phreng ba 'khrigs pa'i 'od//de'i 'bar ba lcags sgrog gi/rnam par 
260a8(rgyud): rdo rje'i mtshan mas mtshan//nges par phyung nas de dag kun//chos dbyings dang ni mi mnyam pa'i//'jig rten khams ni mtha' dag tu//kun nas rnam par bkang nas kyang //sgom bya rdo rje thams cad du//
260b1(rgyud): khro bo dang ldan spro ba'i zhal//lto ba 'chang zhing sku che ba//mche ba bzhi yis rnam par brgyan//'bar ba yis ni 'phreng ba gsal//gzugs mdzes drag pa'i gzugs dang ldan//sngags ni oM badzra shri 
260b2(rgyud): ka lA/ya di pa ta yA/mahA pa lA ya/mahA bi d+ya/ra dza ya te tra dzA li ta/ha sta ya na mo/ni la ka n+thA ya ki rA ta ru pa la m+b+ho da rA ya/bu b+hi ku tri mu khA ya/ma dzA rA/mu kha ya tsa tu ra daM Sh+Ta ka rA lA ya/e h+ye hi 
260b3(rgyud): a s+ya sha rI rA ma nu Ta bi sh+ya sha ra~M gra ha na/haM sta ya na mo/gri bI gri bI ha na/ha sta pA da~M gri h+na ba sha/a be sha ya/ka~M pa~M ya ka~M pa~M ya/b+h+ra~M me b+h+ra~M ma ya ma Tan+t+ra la ma d+h+ye/bi ku rba bi ku rba ts+tsha rA tsu rA/ku T+Ta ku T+Ta/
260b4(rgyud): spe Ta ya spo Ta ya/ma rma ni gri h+na ya/hri da ya/ny+tsin n+ta ba la ba la/ka ny+tsi rA ya tsi/de ban ka/yak+sha rak+sha rak+sha sa/bi shA tsan tra ya  sa ma ya/ma nu sma re dzi ta ma nu smA ra/hA hA ni d+hi ni d+hi ni/shi na shi/

260b7(rgyud): grub ni//dngos grub 'gugs par 'dod pa yis//thams cad gus pas sbyin par byed//bud med gzugs kyis sgrub par bya//rdo rje lu gu rgyud ma'i thabs so//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus 
260b8(rgyud): gnas par//pad ma dmar po'i rab tu gnas//phyag bzhi ma la du ma'i gzugs//dung dang kun d+ha zla ba 'dra//thugs rje yi ni de nyid 'di//rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//zhi ba laswogs 
261a1(rgyud): @#//nyams dang ldan//chos rnams kun gyi bdag skyes mchog/rnal 'byor pas ni rtag tu sgom//sangs rgyas mtha' yas yang dag 'byung //der bdag yang dag sgom pa ni//skal byed ma zhes 
261a2(rgyud): yongs su grags//sngags kyi 'phreng ba'i yi ge ni//kun nas khro ba'i mu tig 'dra//nam mkha'i khams ni yongs gang la//ji srid de'i spyod yul can//ji srid de'i 'od zer sgom//rnal 'byor ye shes kyi ra 
261a3(rgyud): ba sprul//sngags ni oM tsa le tsu le da swAhA//'di'i sngags kyi mchog rtso ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi mchod pa'o//'tshungs pa med pa'i dngos grub ster//lha dang lha min bcas pas mchod//'di 
261a4(rgyud): yis sdig kun mdzad byed cing //phun sum tshogs kun rab rtsal//rab mdzad//pad ma'i phyag rgya yang dag ldan//sangs rgyas nyid kyang sbyin par mdzad//skul byed ma'i sgrub pa'i thabs so//
261a5(rgyud): bsang ba mchog gi dgos pa na//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//sangs rgyas kun bdag soms dpa' ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/'di ni rang 'byung bcom ldan 'das 
261a6(rgyud): gcig tu nyid ni lhag pa'i lha//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma'i dra ba'i sdom//'dod chags med cing chags bral med//dbu mar dmigs par mi 'gyur ro//bud med kun gyi 
261a7(rgyud): sgyu ma'i rgyan//'di ni gnyis med theg pa'i mchog/sgyu ma dag ni thams cad las//bud med sgyu ma khyad par 'phags//rang bzhin gyis ni de 'grub pa//nus pa yi ni rang bzhin gyis//
261a8(rgyud): 'jig rten gsum gyi bud med 'di//bkur bar bya bar 'os pa yin//ngan spyod rnams kyis kyang 'grub ste//rnyed pa kun dang bde dang spros//thams cad med kyang sgyu mas 'grub//
261b1(rgyud): rang 'byung ngo bo yongs sgyur ba//sna tshogs sgyu ma'i phyag rgya 'dzin//shes rab pha rol phyin mar bshad//kun las sna tshogs phyag rgya ni//kun la sna tshogs sdom pa yis//sangs rgyas
261b2(rgyud): kun dang mnyam par sbyor//stong pa nyid kyis de nyid skul//kun la kun nas thams cad du//kun tu kun nas rang nyid kyis//sangs rgyas thams cad brtan dang g.ya'o//thams cad dngos po
261b3(rgyud): rang nyid 'gyur//sems dang sems can ting 'dzin dang //byang chub spyod pa sna tshogs nyid//sa dang pha rol phyin spyod pa//dbang po nyid du yang bshad do//bdud dang bdud las rgyal ba nyid//

270a1(rgyud): @#//gar gi sbyor bas so//rdo rje sngams pa de nyid skyed pa'i phyogs su bzung ba ni/rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgya'o//rdo rje sngams pa las 'dzub mo lcags kyu ltar bzung ba ni rdo rje rgyal po'i

349b4(rgyud): mtshan nyid nga yis stan//dang por snyen pa 'dren dus su//mi sdug gzugs kyang kun ston dang//sdug pa'i gzugs ni kun stan kyang /rang gi ting 'dzin btang mi bya//sprul sbal laswogs gzugs
349b5(rgyud): byung yang//de la 'jigs pa'i sems mi bya//rta dang nya dang spyang khu'i gzugs/gang byung de dag sgral ba yin//'bu dang dom dang nya ljibs dang//ra dang bud med ral pa can//byung yang de dag sgral ba yin/

349b8(rgyud): rmi lam mtshan ma kun zlog cing//gsum nas gnyisu zlog byed kyang//de las 'jigs pa'i sems mi bya//drag po las kyi sngags rnams kyang//drag du 'dul shing gnod pa yin//ldang ba'i dus ni thams cad du//
350a1(rgyud): @#//thun rdeg khyung gi bro bdungs bya//snyan pa'i skad rnams 'byin byed cing //las 'di ma byed gnod mi byed//de skad rjod kyang gnyan mi bya//snyan dang mi snyan gang rjod kyang //gang gi 'ang rjesu 'gro ba min//rang gi

350b2(rgyud): pa rnam par sgom//de stengs me'i 'khor lo yang //'dab ma gsum dang ldan par sgom//de stangs klu gnyan thams cad kyi//nyon mongs phral ba'i shes pa ni//thig le dag tu sgom pa yin//gnyen po khyung gi
